中文摘要翻译: 遥感图像编辑旨在根据自然语言指令修改遥感图像，同时保持地理规则和传感器观测特性。现有基准主要针对自然图像或通用视觉场景，因此可能无法充分捕捉遥感编辑所需的地理推理、区域控制和传感器一致性能力。为填补这一空白，我们提出了RS-RIE-Bench，这是首个面向推理引导的遥感图像编辑基准。RS-RIE-Bench将任务组织为三类：时间推理、因果推理和空间推理。这些类别涵盖了遥感场景中的时间演化、因果结果和空间成像一致性。评估协议包含三个维度：目标区域合理性、非目标区域保持性和图像质量一致性。我们进一步通过跨评判一致性分析和分层专家评审证明了基于多模态大语言模型评估的可行性。对8个开源和闭源图像编辑模型的系统评估表明，当前模型在推理引导的遥感编辑方面仍存在明显局限性。即使在最强模型上，严格联合满足准则下的总体准确率也仅为24.28%，而所有8个模型在松弛联合-4准则下的平均成功率为32.23%。因果推理和空间推理仍然特别具有挑战性，部分开源模型在某些类别上接近零。这些结果表明，RS-RIE-Bench能够有效揭示当前模型在地理推理、区域控制和传感器一致性生成方面的局限性，也为未来遥感智能编辑模型提供了标准化基准和清晰的研究方向。

</details>


### [58] [Not All Patches are Equal: Sampling Matters for Visible-Infrared Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20238)
*Qiwei Ma,Bin Deng,Junjie Zhu,Qiangjuan Huang,Puhong Duan,Ke Yang,Xudong Kang,Shutao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出重要性感知采样（IAS）策略，根据补丁可靠性调整训练重点，解决可见光-红外预训练中均匀对比学习的不可靠问题，在多个VIS-IR基准上取得一致提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VIS-IR对齐方法采用均匀的逐补丁对比学习，但成像物理差异导致部分空间配对区域天然不可比，强行对齐会阻碍表征学习和下游迁移。

Method: (i) 从红外结构线索导出补丁权重并重新加权对比目标；(ii) 使用轻量采样器学习软重要性掩码，可从手工先验热启动；(iii) 采用补丁课程学习策略，从高可靠区域逐步扩展到困难补丁。方法即插即用，兼容补丁级/相关性级对齐和图像级对比基线。

Result: 在多个VIS-IR基准上，包括红外语义分割、红外目标检测、可见光语义分割和跨模态检索任务，均取得一致且显著的改进。

Conclusion: 从采样视角重新审视VIS-IR预训练，通过重要性感知采样有效缓解了不可靠对齐问题，是一种通用且有效的即插即用方案。

Original Abstract: Visible-infrared (VIS-IR) alignment is a key pre-training task for robust multi-sensor perception. Most existing methods use uniform patch-wise contrastive learning, but this can be unreliable in VIS-IR data because imaging-physics differences make some spatially paired regions inherently less comparable, and aligning them with equal strength hinders representation learning and downstream transfer. In this paper, we revisit VIS-IR pre-training from a sampling perspective and propose Importance-Aware Sampling (IAS), which adjusts training emphasis based on patch reliability. Specifically, IAS (i) derives patch weights from infrared structural cues and uses them to reweight the contrastive objective; (ii) learns a soft importance mask with a lightweight sampler, optionally warm-started from the hand-crafted prior; and (iii) employs a patch curriculum learning strategy that gradually expands from high-reliability regions to harder patches. It is worth noting that IAS is plug-and-play and works with both patch-/correlation-level alignment (e.g., UNIV-style) and image-level contrastive baselines (e.g., ImageBind-style). Extensive experiments on multiple VIS-IR benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, including for IR semantic segmentation, IR object detection and VIS semantic segmentation and cross-modal retrieval task. Code will be released on https://github.com/KlayMa527/IAS.

中文摘要翻译: 可见光-红外（VIS-IR）对齐是实现鲁棒多传感器感知的关键预训练任务。现有方法大多采用均匀的逐补丁对比学习，但这在VIS-IR数据中并不可靠，因为成像物理差异使得某些空间配对区域天然具有较弱的可比性，以同等强度对齐这些区域会阻碍表征学习和下游迁移。本文从采样视角重新审视VIS-IR预训练，提出重要性感知采样（IAS），根据补丁可靠性调整训练重点。具体而言，IAS：(i) 从红外结构线索导出补丁权重，并用以重新加权对比目标；(ii) 使用轻量采样器学习软重要性掩码，可选择从手工设计的先验进行热启动；(iii) 采用补丁课程学习策略，从高可靠性区域逐步扩展到困难补丁。值得注意的是，IAS是即插即用的，既适用于补丁级/相关性级对齐（如UNIV风格）也适用于图像级对比基线（如图像绑定风格）。在多个VIS-IR基准上的大量实验表明，IAS在强基线上取得了一致的改进，涵盖红外语义分割、红外目标检测、可见光语义分割以及跨模态检索任务。代码将在https://github.com/KlayMa527/IAS上发布。

</details>


### [59] [Vera: Identity-Faithful Human Subject-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20247)
*Yulong Xu,Xinyue Liu,Shujuan Li,huafeng shi,Yan Zhou,Jiwen Liu,Xintao Wang,Yu Shen Liu,Huaibo Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Vera是一个统一的人中心S2V框架，通过百万级身份对齐数据集、身份聚焦掩码监督（IFMS）和参考感知逐层注意力（RALA），显著提升了单人和多人视频生成中的人物身份一致性、角色绑定准确性，并减少了身份混淆和过度参考复制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有主体到视频（S2V）生成方法在通用类别上取得了进展，但在以人为中心的生成中仍存在身份关键细节漂移、多人物场景下的身份角色绑定错误、属性交换以及过度复制参考图像外观等问题，亟需一种统一且能保持严格身份一致性的生成框架。

Method: 1）构建百万级身份对齐的人像图像-视频数据集，通过跨片段人物级检索提供显式身份对应与多样化参考；2）身份聚焦掩码监督（IFMS），通过空间聚焦监督增强身份感知学习并减少无关干扰；3）参考感知逐层注意力（RALA），在DiT主干中调控视频token与参考身份特征的交互，保持稳定的身份锚点并增强逐层身份读出能力。

Result: 实验表明，Vera在人类身份一致性、多人物主体绑定、运动自然度方面均有提升，同时减少了身份混淆和过度的参考图像复制行为。

Conclusion: Vera通过数据构建与模型设计的协同优化，有效解决了人中心S2V生成中的身份一致性问题，尤其适用于对身份保真度要求较高的单人和多人视频生成场景。

Original Abstract: Subject-to-video (S2V) generation has made substantial progress in preserving reference subjects across diverse categories, yet generic subject consistency remains insufficient for human-centric generation. A video may appear globally consistent while identity-critical human details still drift across frames, poses, and interactions. This issue becomes more severe in multi-person scenarios, where incorrect identity-role binding leads to subject confusion, attribute swapping, and excessive copying of reference-specific appearance cues. We propose Vera, a unified human-centric S2V framework for single- and multi-person generation. We first construct a million-pair identity-aligned human image-video dataset through person-level cross-clip retrieval, providing explicit identity correspondence and diverse references. Built on this dataset, Vera introduces two complementary designs. Identity-Focal Masked Supervision (IFMS) strengthens identity-aware learning with spatially focused supervision while reducing interference from irrelevant artifacts. Reference-Aware Layer-wise Attention (RALA) regulates how video tokens interact with reference identity cues in the DiT backbone, preserving stable identity anchors and enhancing layer-aware identity readout. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Vera improves human identity consistency, multi-person subject binding, and motion naturalness, while reducing identity confusion and excessive reference-image copying.

中文摘要翻译: 主体到视频（Subject-to-Video, S2V）生成在跨多类别的参考主体保持方面取得了实质性进展，但通用的主体一致性在以人为中心的生成中仍显不足。一段视频可能在全局上看似一致，但涉及身份关键的人体细节仍会在帧间、姿态变化和交互过程中发生漂移。这一问题在多人场景中更加严重，错误的身份-角色绑定会导致主体混淆、属性交换以及对参考特定外观线索的过度复制。我们提出Vera，一个统一的以人为中心的S2V框架，支持单人和多人生成。我们首先通过跨片段的人物级检索构建了百万对级别的身份对齐人像图像-视频数据集，提供显式的身份对应关系和多样化的参考信息。在此数据集基础上，Vera引入了两种互补设计。身份聚焦掩码监督（Identity-Focal Masked Supervision, IFMS）通过空间聚焦监督加强身份感知学习，同时减少无关伪影的干扰。参考感知逐层注意力（Reference-Aware Layer-wise Attention, RALA）在DiT骨干网络中调控视频token与参考身份特征的交互方式，保持稳定的身份锚点并增强逐层身份读出能力。大量实验表明，Vera在人类身份一致性、多人物主体绑定以及运动自然度方面均有提升，同时减少了身份混淆和过度的参考图像复制。

</details>


### [60] [How Does Urban Context Relate to Residential Building Health? A Vision-POI Fusion Framework for Building-Level Housing Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20263)
*Kun Zhao,Helei Ren,Guilin Tang,Tianyi Chen,Zhehui Song,Xing Liu,Lijian Zhou,Yuhong Zhao,Xiang Gao,Jinming Jiang,Qichao Ban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种视觉-POI融合框架，将多视角图像检测与POI邻里上下文结合，用于住宅建筑健康评估。实验表明多视图聚合显著提升性能（Macro-F1从60.84%提升至74.95%），而POI上下文仅带来小幅附加增益（至76.79%），POI信息作为补充先验而非替代视觉证据。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自动化建筑检查研究主要依赖单张图像，鲜有考察周边城市功能上下文（如POI）能否为建筑级评估提供补充信息。

Method: 首先采用多目标检测模型从单张图像提取问题位置、类别和置信度，再跨多视图聚合构建建筑级表征；随后提取500m、1000m、1500m缓冲区内的POI特征，通过Pearson/Spearman相关性分析及FDR校正筛选候选上下文特征；最后使用代价敏感随机森林分类器，在社区隔离的空间交叉验证下融合视觉和POI特征。

Result: 多视图聚合将建筑级Macro-F1从Direct Detection的60.84%提升至74.95%；融入POI上下文后进一步升至76.79%，但额外增益较小且因问题类别而异。

Conclusion: POI信息作为补充性上下文先验而非视觉证据的替代品，也非建筑状况的因果决定因素。

Original Abstract: Housing-level urban physical examination is essential for identifying residential building problems and supporting targeted urban renewal. Existing automated inspection studies primarily rely on individual images and rarely examine whether surrounding urban functional context can provide supplementary information for building-level assessment. This study proposes a vision-POI fusion framework that combines multi-view visual inspection with POI-derived neighborhood context for residential building health assessment. The empirical dataset covers 92 old residential communities, 3,237 residential buildings, and 25,608 field-acquired inspection images in Qingdao, China, encompassing seven categories of housing-related issues. First, multiple object detection models are evaluated to extract issue locations, categories, and confidence scores from individual images. The image-level outputs are subsequently aggregated across multiple views to construct interpretable building-level representations. Second, POI features are extracted within 500m, 1,000m, and 1,500m neighborhood buffers to characterize surrounding functional environments. Pearson and Spearman correlation analyses, combined with false discovery rate correction, are used to identify candidate contextual features. Finally, visual and POI features are integrated using a cost-sensitive Random Forest classifier under community-isolated spatial cross-validation. The results show that multi-view aggregation provides the main performance improvement, increasing the building-level Macro-F1 from 60.84% under Direct Detection to 74.95%. Incorporating POI context further increases Macro-F1 to 76.79%, although the additional gain is modest and category-dependent. POI information therefore functions as a supplementary contextual prior rather than a substitute for direct visual evidence or a causal determinant of building condition.

中文摘要翻译: 住房层面的城市体检对于识别住宅建筑问题和支持精准城市更新至关重要。现有自动化检测研究主要依赖单张图像，鲜有考察周边城市功能上下文能否为建筑级评估提供补充信息。本研究提出一种视觉-POI融合框架，将多视角视觉检测与POI衍生的邻里上下文相结合，用于住宅建筑健康评估。实证数据集涵盖中国青岛市92个老旧小区、3,237栋住宅建筑和25,608张实地采集的检测图像，涉及七类住房相关问题。首先，评估多种目标检测模型以从单张图像提取问题位置、类别和置信度得分；随后跨多视图聚合图像级输出，构建可解释的建筑级表征。其次，在500m、1,000m和1,500m邻里缓冲区内提取POI特征，以刻画周边功能环境；采用Pearson和Spearman相关性分析结合错误发现率校正，筛选候选上下文特征。最后，在社区隔离的空间交叉验证下，使用代价敏感随机森林分类器融合视觉和POI特征。结果表明，多视图聚合提供了主要的性能提升，将建筑级Macro-F1从直接检测的60.84%提升至74.95%。融入POI上下文后，Macro-F1进一步升至76.79%，但额外增益较小且因问题类别而异。因此，POI信息作为补充性上下文先验，而非直接视觉证据的替代品或建筑状况的因果决定因素。

</details>


### [61] [Self-supervision drives representational convergence in medical foundation models more than clinical supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20274)
*Soroosh Tayebi Arasteh,Sebastian Ziegelmayer,Mahshad Lotfinia,Lisa Adams,Sven Nebelung,Jakob Nikolas Kather,Daniel Truhn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 医学图像编码器之间的表征收敛性被高估：收敛主要由自监督目标驱动，而非规模或临床监督；编码器间可迁移性有限，但线性分类器可跨编码器转移，保留约85%性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究假设不同医疗图像编码器因规模扩大和临床监督而趋向共享表征结构，但该假设缺乏验证，且相似性度量方法不可靠。论文旨在系统检验这一收敛性是否真实存在、其产生原因以及临床可用性。

Method: 对18个图像编码器和7个文本编码器进行受控对比分析（参数规模7M~27B，涵盖5种成像模态、650,982张胸部X光片）。通过固定数据、架构和规模，仅改变训练目标来分离因果效应，并在合成模型中复现结果。

Result: 收敛性虽高于随机基线但程度有限：自监督编码器对齐度最高（胸部X光40.4%），标签监督（21.1%）和图像-文本（3.3%）远低；收敛性不随规模增长（Spearman 0.302, p=0.223）；收敛限于模态内，未达到临床语言水平，不反映放射科医生对病例相似性的判断；但线性分类器可跨编码器迁移，在五个外部医院数据集上保留约85%的编码器内性能。

Conclusion: 医学影像编码器的收敛性由预训练目标决定，而非规模或临床监督继承而来。互操作性应通过预训练目标来设计，并在共享几何结构最薄弱的环节（患者亚组、临床判断）进行验证。

Original Abstract: Medical image encoders from different groups are increasingly treated as interchangeable, on the assumption that scale and clinical supervision concentrate their representations onto a shared structure. Whether this convergence is real, what produces it, and whether it is clinically usable are untested, and the similarity measures behind such claims are fragile. We present a controlled dissection across 18 image and 7 text encoders, all open-weight and run locally, spanning 7M to 27B parameters and five imaging modalities, including 650,982 chest radiographs from six datasets. To isolate cause, we train encoders that vary only the objective under fixed data, architecture, and scale, and reproduce the effect in a synthetic model. Convergence is modest but above a random floor, driven by the self-supervised objective, not clinical supervision: matched self-supervised encoders aligned most (40.4% on chest radiography), with label-supervised (21.1%) and image-text (3.3%) far lower, and did not grow with size (Spearman 0.302, p=0.223) or capability. It is within-modality, does not reach clinical language, and does not reproduce how radiologists judge case similarity. Yet a linear classifier transfers across encoders and to five held-out hospitals, retaining about 85% of within-encoder performance. Convergence in medical imaging is therefore set by the pretraining objective, not inherited from scale or clinical supervision. Interoperability is accordingly something to design for through that objective, and to validate where the shared geometry is weakest, across patient subgroups and against clinical judgment.

中文摘要翻译: 来自不同团队的医学图像编码器日益被视为可互换的，其假设是规模和临床监督将其表征集中到共享结构上。这种收敛是否真实、由何产生、是否可用于临床，均未经检验，且此类主张背后的相似性度量并不可靠。我们对18个图像编码器和7个文本编码器进行了受控剖析，全部为开源权重并在本地运行，参数规模从7M到27B，涵盖五种成像模态，包括来自六个数据集的650,982张胸部X光片。为分离原因，我们训练了在固定数据、架构和规模下仅改变训练目标的编码器，并在合成模型中复现了该效应。收敛性虽高于随机基线但程度有限，由自监督目标驱动，而非临床监督：匹配的自监督编码器对齐度最高（胸部X光片40.4%），标签监督（21.1%）和图像-文本（3.3%）远低，且不随规模增长（Spearman 0.302, p=0.223）或能力提升而增强。收敛性限于模态内部，未达到临床语言水平，且不能复现放射科医生判断病例相似性的方式。然而，线性分类器可跨编码器迁移，并泛化至五个外部医院数据集，保留约85%的编码器内性能。因此，医学影像中的收敛性由预训练目标决定，而非从规模或临床监督中继承。互操作性应据此通过该目标来设计，并在共享几何结构最薄弱的环节——跨患者亚组和临床判断——进行验证。

</details>


### [62] [Multimodal Large Language Models for Remote Sensing Image Understanding: Domain-Specific or General-Purpose?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20284)
*Qiwei Ma,Chunping Qiu,Xinjun Cheng,Xiaoyu Zhang,Puhong Duan,Ke Yang,Xudong Kang,Shutao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统综述并诊断评估了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在遥感图像场景理解（RSISU）中的能力边界、跨任务泛化性和任务特定局限，发现通用视觉MLLMs在多项RSISU任务上可媲美甚至超越专用遥感MLLMs，并指出了当前模型在空间推理、细粒度理解等方面的不足及未来方向。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有遥感MLLMs缺乏对其能力边界、跨任务泛化性和任务特定局限的系统性理解，亟需一份全面的综述和诊断性评估来厘清专用模型与通用模型的实际表现差异。

Method: 通过系统性综述回顾遥感MLLMs的技术演进（模型设计、多模态学习、训练数据、下游能力），并在多种RSISU任务和基准上对比遥感MLLMs与通用计算机视觉MLLMs的性能。

Result: 遥感MLLMs在领域特定场景（如遥感视觉定位和高分辨率视觉问答）中具有竞争力；但通用CV-MLLMs在多项RSISU任务上无需遥感微调即可媲美甚至超越专用模型。当前MLLMs在空间关系推理、细粒度视觉理解、指令多样性和跨任务格式泛化方面存在局限。

Conclusion: 通用CV-MLLMs展现出强迁移能力，遥感专用MLLMs未能持续领先。未来方向包括可靠评估、多模态高分辨率推理、高效部署和工具增强型遥感智能体。

Original Abstract: The rapid development of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) has introduced a flexible paradigm for remote sensing image scene understanding (RSISU), enabling natural-language interaction with remote sensing imagery. However, a systematic understanding of the capability boundaries, cross-task generalization, and task-specific limitations of existing remote sensing MLLMs (RS-MLLMs) is still lacking. This paper presents a systematic survey and diagnostic evaluation of MLLMs for RSISU. We review the technical evolution of RS-MLLMs, focusing on model design, multimodal learning, training data, and downstream capabilities. We further compare RS-MLLMs with general-purpose computer vision MLLMs (CV-MLLMs) across diverse RSISU tasks and benchmarks. RS-MLLMs remain competitive in domain-specific settings, particularly remote sensing visual grounding and high-resolution visual question answering. More notably, general-purpose CV-MLLMs can match or even outperform these specialized models on several RSISU tasks without remote sensing-specific fine-tuning. These findings demonstrate the strong transferability of general-purpose CV-MLLMs and show that current RS-MLLMs do not consistently outperform them across diverse RSISU tasks. Current MLLMs also face limitations in spatial and relational reasoning, fine-grained visual understanding, instruction diversity, and generalization across heterogeneous task formats. Based on these findings, we outline future directions toward reliable evaluation, multimodal and high-resolution reasoning, efficient deployment, and tool-augmented remote sensing agents. This survey provides a systematic reference for developing robust, generalizable, and practical MLLMs for RSISU.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的快速发展为遥感图像场景理解（RSISU）引入了一种灵活范式，实现了与遥感影像的自然语言交互。然而，现有遥感MLLMs（RS-MLLMs）在能力边界、跨任务泛化性和任务特定局限性方面仍缺乏系统性理解。本文对用于RSISU的MLLMs进行了系统综述和诊断性评估。我们回顾了RS-MLLMs的技术演进，聚焦于模型设计、多模态学习、训练数据和下游能力。我们进一步在多样化的RSISU任务和基准上，将RS-MLLMs与通用计算机视觉MLLMs（CV-MLLMs）进行了比较。RS-MLLMs在领域特定场景中保持竞争力，特别是在遥感视觉定位和高分辨率视觉问答方面。更值得注意的是，通用CV-MLLMs在多项RSISU任务上无需遥感专用微调即可媲美甚至超越这些专用模型。这些发现证明了通用CV-MLLMs的强大可迁移性，并表明当前RS-MLLMs在多样化的RSISU任务上并未持续优于它们。当前MLLMs在空间和关系推理、细粒度视觉理解、指令多样性以及跨异构任务格式的泛化方面仍面临局限性。基于这些发现，我们概述了面向可靠评估、多模态高分辨率推理、高效部署和工具增强型遥感智能体的未来方向。本综述为开发用于RSISU的鲁棒、可泛化且实用的MLLMs提供了系统性参考。

</details>


### [63] [Diverse-Intent Multi-Turn Fashion Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20291)
*Mingqiang Tang,Haokun Wen,Meng Liu,Yupeng Hu,Weili Guan,Xuemeng Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对多轮时尚检索中异构意图转换未被探索、以及文本桥接导致细粒度视觉线索丢失的问题，提出了DIM-Fashion基准数据集和FashionAM多模态对齐框架，实验验证了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多轮检索方法假设每轮交互都遵循相同的属性编辑范式，未探索异构意图转换；同时，现有方法依赖文本化桥接多模态查询与视觉检索，导致细粒度视觉线索丢失。本文旨在填补这些空白。

Method: 提出FashionAM框架，结合多模态大语言模型（MLLM）和视觉-语言预训练（VLP），将多模态对话查询直接与面向时尚的画廊嵌入空间对齐，避免中间文本化过程。同时构建了包含26K多轮会话的DIM-Fashion基准数据集，涵盖7个任务、13个时尚检索数据集，包含多样化的意图转换和回滚行为。

Result: 大量实验表明，FashionAM在多个任务上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的DIM-Fashion基准和FashionAM框架有效解决了多轮时尚检索中异构意图转换和细粒度视觉线索丢失问题，为相关研究提供了新的基准和方法。

Original Abstract: Real-world fashion search involves interactive retrieval across multiple turns. However, existing multi-turn retrieval methods are built on a restrictive assumption that every interaction follows the same attribute-editing paradigm, leaving heterogeneous intent transitions unexplored. Moreover, existing approaches often rely on textification to bridge multimodal queries and visual retrieval, which may lose fine-grained visual cues. To address these gaps, we introduce DIM-Fashion, a benchmark of 26K multi-turn sessions constructed from 13 fashion retrieval datasets across 7 tasks, featuring diverse intent transitions and rollback behaviors. We further propose FashionAM, an MLLM-VLP framework that directly aligns multimodal conversational queries with a fashion-oriented gallery embedding space, avoiding intermediate textification. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of FashionAM over existing approaches. The dataset and code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

中文摘要翻译: 现实世界中的时尚搜索涉及跨多个轮次的交互式检索。然而，现有的多轮检索方法建立在一个限制性假设之上，即每轮交互都遵循相同的属性编辑范式，这使得异构意图转换未被探索。此外，现有方法通常依赖文本化来桥接多模态查询与视觉检索，这可能会丢失细粒度的视觉线索。为填补这些空白，我们引入了DIM-Fashion——一个由来自7个任务的13个时尚检索数据集构建的26K多轮会话基准，具有多样化的意图转换和回滚行为。我们进一步提出了FashionAM，一个MLLM-VLP框架，该框架直接将多模态对话查询与面向时尚的画廊嵌入空间对齐，避免了中间文本化过程。大量实验表明，FashionAM相比现有方法具有有效性。数据集和代码将在论文被接收后公开发布。

</details>


### [64] [Evolving Cache Schedules for Fast Diffusion Policy Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20293)
*Siying Wang,Kangye Ji,Di Wang,Fei Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EVO提出了一种无需重新训练的加速框架，通过进化搜索全局优化扩散策略中缓存刷新调度，在保持近完整性能的同时实现最高8.05倍的动作生成加速。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于缓存的加速方法采用均匀分配计算的方式，忽略了不同Transformer块之间的异构冗余特性，导致性能与效率之间的权衡欠佳。

Method: EVO将缓存刷新调度问题建模为块-时间步格子上的进化搜索问题，引入冗余感知初始化生成有前景的调度候选，并通过目标条件早停验证机制在达到性能目标后终止搜索。优化后的调度可直接用于预训练的扩散策略，无需重新训练。

Result: 在多个操作基准测试上，EVO在保持近完整性能的同时，实现了最高8.05倍的动作生成加速，FLOPs从15.77G降低至1.96G。

Conclusion: EVO通过进化搜索实现了全局最优的缓存刷新调度，有效解决了扩散策略中计算冗余问题，为实时部署提供了高效且无需重新训练的加速方案。

Original Abstract: Diffusion policies achieve strong visuomotor control by iteratively denoising action chunks, but repeated denoising makes real-time deployment computationally demanding. Cache-based methods reduce inference cost by reusing intermediate activations, but existing training-free schedules typically allocate computation uniformly across blocks, ignoring heterogeneous redundancy across blocks and leading to a suboptimal performance-efficiency trade-off. To bridge this gap, we introduce Evolving Cache Schedules (EVO), a training-free acceleration framework that globally schedules cache refreshes via evolutionary search. EVO represents each candidate as a complete schedule over the block-timestep lattice. Thus, redundant transformer computations during iterative denoising can be skipped through cache reuse while preserving closed-loop rollout performance. To make the search practical, EVO introduces redundancy-aware initialization, which seeds the population with promising schedules, and target-conditioned early stopping, which verifies and terminates once a desired performance target is reached. The offline-optimized schedule can be directly plugged into pretrained diffusion policies without retraining. Extensive manipulation benchmarks show that EVO preserves near-full performance while substantially reducing computation, achieving up to 8.05x action-generation speedup and reducing FLOPs from 15.77G to as low as 1.96G. Source code is available at https://github.com/pillom/EVO.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散策略通过迭代去噪动作块实现强大的视觉运动控制，但重复去噪使得实时部署面临巨大的计算压力。基于缓存的方法通过复用中间激活来降低推理成本，但现有的无训练调度通常在各模块间均匀分配计算，忽略了不同模块间的异构冗余特性，导致性能与效率之间的权衡欠佳。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了演化缓存调度（EVO），一种无需重新训练的加速框架，通过进化搜索全局优化缓存刷新调度。EVO将每个候选方案表示为块-时间步格子上的完整调度，从而在保持闭环部署性能的同时，通过缓存复用跳过迭代去噪过程中的冗余Transformer计算。为使搜索切实可行，EVO引入了冗余感知初始化，为种群播种有前景的调度方案，以及目标条件早停机制，在达到预期性能目标后验证并终止搜索。经离线优化的调度可直接嵌入预训练的扩散策略中，无需重新训练。广泛的操作基准测试表明，EVO在大幅减少计算量的同时保持了近乎完整的性能，实现了最高8.05倍的动作生成加速，FLOPs从15.77G降至仅1.96G。源代码已发布于 https://github.com/pillom/EVO。

</details>


### [65] [Toward Reliable RGB-D Semantic Segmentation: Handling Missing Modalities via Condition Dropout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20326)
*Xuchen Zhu,Yajuan Wei,Shuang Hao,Jiwei Jiang,Guanxiang Mao,Fang Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种名为Condition Dropout (ConD)的简单持续训练范式，解决RGB-D语义分割模型在单模态缺失时性能严重下降的问题，在保持完整模态精度的同时提升鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RGB-D语义分割模型假设RGB和深度模态始终可用，但实际中传感器故障或遮挡常导致某一模态缺失。仅使用RGB或深度虽包含足够信息，但基于完整模态训练的模型在缺失一个模态时无法有效利用剩余模态，导致性能严重下降。

Method: 从预训练的RGB-D模型出发，ConD增加第二阶段训练：随机模拟完整、RGB缺失和深度缺失三种输入情况，冻结原始编码器，训练带零初始化特征注入的复制编码器。

Result: 在NYU-Depth V2和SUN RGB-D数据集上的实验表明，ConD在模态缺失下提升了鲁棒性，且在模态完整时还有轻微的性能提升。

Conclusion: ConD是一种简单有效的持续训练范式，能够缓解RGB-D语义分割中模态缺失导致的性能退化，同时保持甚至提升完整模态下的精度。

Original Abstract: RGB-D semantic segmentation has achieved remarkable progress, yet most models assume that RGB and depth are always available. In practice, failures or occlusions of surveillance sensors often remove one modality. Although RGB or depth alone can contain sufficient cues, models trained only on full-modality inputs fail to exploit the remaining modality once one is missing, causing severe degradation. We tackle this issue with a simple continued-training paradigm, \emph{Condition Dropout (ConD)}, which mitigates degradation while preserving full-modality accuracy. Starting from a pretrained RGB-D model, ConD adds a second stage that randomly simulates complete, RGB-missing, and depth-missing inputs, freezes the original encoders, and trains copied encoders with zero-initialized feature injection. Experiments on NYU-Depth V2 and SUN RGB-D show that ConD improves robustness under missing modalities and even yields slight gains when modalities are complete. Our code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

中文摘要翻译: RGB-D语义分割已取得显著进展，但大多数模型假设RGB和深度模态始终可用。在实际应用中，监控传感器的故障或遮挡常导致某一模态缺失。尽管仅使用RGB或深度本身已包含足够线索，但仅在完整模态输入上训练的模型在缺失一个模态时无法有效利用剩余模态，从而导致严重的性能退化。我们通过一种简单的持续训练范式——Condition Dropout（ConD）来应对这一问题，它在保持完整模态精度的同时缓解性能退化。ConD从预训练的RGB-D模型出发，增加第二阶段训练：随机模拟完整、RGB缺失和深度缺失三种输入情况，冻结原始编码器，并训练带有零初始化特征注入的复制编码器。在NYU-Depth V2和SUN RGB-D上的实验表明，ConD在模态缺失下提升了鲁棒性，甚至在模态完整时也有轻微的性能增益。我们的代码将在论文接收后公开。

</details>


### [66] [Test-Time Training for Modality Order Consistency in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20351)
*Aditi Gupta,Yossi Gandelsman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发现视觉-语言模型在图像和问题的输入顺序上存在敏感性（图像优先优于问题优先），并提出了一种顺序一致的测试时训练方法，不仅消除了顺序差异，还提升了图像优先分支的性能。通过激活修补定位到网络中层的表示分歧，而所提方法可修复这种错位。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 揭示视觉-语言模型中存在一种被忽视的语义无关输入顺序敏感性（模态顺序失效），并探索其成因与缓解方法。

Method: 提出一种顺序一致的测试时训练方法（order-consistent test-time training），通过对两种顺序分支进行一致性约束，在测试阶段微调模型，使其对模态顺序不敏感。

Result: 1) 图像优先提示在所有三个模型和三个基准上一致优于问题优先。2) 所提方法显著缩小了模态顺序差距，并意外地提升了更强的图像优先分支的性能。3) 激活修补将顺序失效定位到网络中层的狭窄区域，测试时训练修复了该错位。

Conclusion: 模态顺序敏感性是VLM中的一个电路级失效，简单的不对称测试时适应可以有效缓解该问题，甚至能提升基线性能。

Original Abstract: We find that vision-language models are sensitive to a specific semantically irrelevant change: the order in which the image and question are presented. Across three models and three benchmarks, image first prompting consistently outperforms question-first prompting, revealing a repeatable modality order failure. We use this gap to design an order-consistent test-time training method. Our method substantially closes the modality-order gap across all evaluated settings. Surprisingly, it also yields consistent improvements in the stronger image-first branch over the baseline, hence bootstrapping both orderings toward mutual consistency. Activation patching localizes the ordering failure to a narrow mid-network region where representations diverge sharply between prompt orders. We find that the test-time training method repairs this misalignment across layers. Together, our results identify modality-order sensitivity as a circuit-level failure in VLMs and demonstrate that simple, asymmetric test-time adaptation can effectively mitigate it and even improve performance over the baseline.

中文摘要翻译: 我们发现视觉-语言模型对一种特定的语义无关变化是敏感的：图像和问题呈现的顺序。在三个模型和三个基准上，图像优先提示始终优于问题优先提示，揭示了一种可重复的模态顺序失效。我们利用这一差距设计了一种顺序一致的测试时训练方法。该方法在所有评估设置中显著缩小了模态顺序差距。令人惊讶的是，它在更强的图像优先分支上也产生了相对于基线的一致改进，从而将两种顺序都引导至相互一致。激活修补将顺序失效定位到一个狭窄的网络中层区域，在该区域中不同提示顺序的表示急剧分化。我们发现测试时训练方法修复了跨层的这种错位。综合来看，我们的结果表明模态顺序敏感是VLM中的一种电路级失效，并证明简单的、非对称的测试时适应可以有效缓解该问题，甚至能提升基线性能。

</details>


### [67] [Look Less, Think Faster: Joint Token-Compute Adaptation for Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20357)
*Pengcheng Wang,Zhiquan Wang,Jayoung Lee,Zhuoyan Xu,Ran Xu,Saurabh Bagchi,Yin Li,Somali Chaterji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SmartVL提出了一种统一的自适应推理框架，通过联合控制视觉token数量和LLM计算能力，在多种计算预算下实现精度与效率的最佳平衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法独立优化视觉token数量或LLM计算深度（如跳过层或注意力头），忽视了这些维度之间存在的根本耦合关系——可用计算资源需要根据输入内容在所有维度上动态分配。

Method: SmartVL包含视觉端token控制器（动态选择信息量大的视觉token）和LLM端计算控制器（自适应调整LLM计算量），两者通过共享预算编码和可微分的延迟估计器进行端到端联合训练，实现跨阶段分配策略。

Result: 在多个MLLM基准测试中，SmartVL的联合调度方法一致优于先前的自适应方法，实现了更优的精度-效率帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 联合调度多个计算维度比独立优化单一维度更有效，能够根据输入复杂度和运行时计算约束自适应分配资源，从而提升MLLM的推理效率。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have recently demonstrated strong performance across vision-language tasks. However, their high inference cost, arising from both the large number of input visual tokens and the heavy computation of the large language model (LLM), remains a key barrier to practical deployment. Recent work attempts to reduce the cost by adaptively optimizing individual dimensions, e.g., pruning redundant visual tokens or skipping LLM layers and heads. Nonetheless, prior approaches typically treat these dimensions independently and overlook a fundamental coupling: the available compute resources must be dynamically allocated across all dimensions based on the input content. To bridge the gap, we propose SmartVL, a unified adaptive inference framework that jointly controls vision token number and model compute capability in response to varying input contents and compute budgets. SmartVL introduces a vision-side token controller that dynamically selects informative visual tokens and an LLM-side compute controller that adaptively adjusts LLM computation. Importantly, these controllers are trained to coordinate with each other so that the overall inference cost satisfies a target budget. To allow this joint scheduling, we connect the controllers using a shared budget encoding and leverage a differentiable latency estimator for end-to-end training. This design enables SmartVL to learn cross-stage allocation strategies that adapt to both input complexity and runtime compute constraints. Experiments across multiple MLLM benchmarks demonstrate that, with joint scheduling, SmartVL consistently outperforms prior adaptive methods and achieves superior accuracy-efficiency Pareto frontiers. Project page: https://www.schaterji.io/publications/2026/jointtokencompute.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）近期在视觉-语言任务中展现出强大的性能。然而，其高昂的推理成本——源于大量的输入视觉token以及大语言模型（LLM）的繁重计算——仍然是实际部署的关键障碍。近期的工作尝试通过自适应地优化单个维度来降低成本，例如修剪冗余的视觉token或跳过LLM的层和注意力头。然而，先前的方法通常将这些维度独立处理，忽略了一个根本性的耦合关系：可用的计算资源必须根据输入内容在所有维度上动态分配。为弥补这一差距，我们提出了SmartVL，一个统一的自适应推理框架，能够根据变化的输入内容和计算预算，联合控制视觉token数量和模型计算能力。SmartVL引入了视觉端的token控制器，动态选择信息性强的视觉token，以及LLM端的计算控制器，自适应调整LLM的计算量。重要的是，这些控制器经过协同训练，使得整体推理成本满足目标预算。为了实现这种联合调度，我们通过共享预算编码连接控制器，并利用可微分的延迟估计器进行端到端训练。该设计使SmartVL能够学习跨阶段的分配策略，同时适应输入复杂度和运行时计算约束。在多个MLLM基准上的实验表明，通过联合调度，SmartVL一致优于先前的自适应方法，并实现了更优的精度-效率帕累托前沿。项目页面：https://www.schaterji.io/publications/2026/jointtokencompute。

</details>


### [68] [Self Gradient Forcing: Native Long Video Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20368)
*Junhao Zhuang,Shiyi Zhang,Yuxuan Bian,Yaowei Li,Yawen Luo,Yijun Liu,Weiyang Jin,Songchun Zhang,Xianglong He,Xuying Zhang,Haoran Li,Haoyang Huang,Zeyue Xue,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自梯度强迫（SGF）训练策略，通过两遍训练为自回归视频扩散模型提供缺失的历史上下文梯度监督信号，在仅5秒训练窗口下实现数分钟的长视频外推，显著优于自强迫（Self Forcing）方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自回归视频扩散方法基于自强迫（Self Forcing）训练，学生模型使用自身rollout生成的历史而非真实视频上下文进行训练。虽然减少了曝光偏差，但历史KV缓存仅作为冻结的rollout状态被未来帧使用，导致未来损失无法监督早期生成的潜变量如何编码为对后续视频生成更有用的键和值，这称为历史上下文-梯度差距（historical context-gradient gap）。

Method: 提出自梯度强迫（Self Gradient Forcing, SGF），一种两遍训练策略。第一遍：执行无梯度的自回归rollout匹配推理阶段，在采样的去噪退出步记录自生成上下文和输入模型的噪声潜变量。第二遍：对记录的退出步执行并行上下文梯度重建，将生成上下文作为停止梯度的干净潜变量输入，模型重新计算上下文KV表示和未来到上下文的因果注意力，从而在原生自回归训练目标中提供缺失的记忆写入监督。

Result: 在多种初始化条件下的长程逐帧和逐块实验中，SGF在主体身份保持、背景/布局一致性、时间稳定性等指标上均优于Self Forcing，使用仅5秒训练窗口即可外推至数分钟长度的视频。

Conclusion: SGF通过恢复历史上下文梯度信号有效弥合了上下文-梯度差距，显著提升了自回归视频扩散模型的长视频外推能力。

Original Abstract: Recent autoregressive video diffusion methods are increasingly built upon Self Forcing, where the student is trained on histories produced by its own rollout rather than ground-truth video contexts. This reduces exposure bias, but the historical key-value cache is still used by future frames only as frozen rollout state. As a result, future losses cannot supervise how earlier generated latents should be written into more useful keys and values for later video-latent generation. We call this the historical context-gradient gap. We propose Self Gradient Forcing (SGF), a two-pass training strategy that restores this missing supervision signal without backpropagating through the full serial rollout. Pass 1 performs a no-gradient autoregressive rollout matching inference and, at a sampled denoising exit step, records both the self-generated context and the noisy latents fed to the model. Pass 2 performs parallel context-gradient reconstruction for the recorded exit step. The generated context is used as stop-gradient clean-latent input, while the model recomputes the context KV representations and future-to-context causal attention. Thus, SGF provides the missing memory-writing supervision within the native autoregressive training objective, using losses on future video latents to train the model to encode context into more effective causal memory. Across extensive long-horizon frame-wise and chunk-wise experiments under different initializations, SGF achieves stronger native long-video extrapolation than Self Forcing, especially in subject identity, background/layout consistency, and temporal stability. Remarkably, using only a 5-second training window, SGF can extrapolate to videos lasting several minutes. Code and models will be released to advance research on autoregressive video generation.

中文摘要翻译: 近期自回归视频扩散方法越来越多地建立在自强迫（Self Forcing）机制上，即学生模型使用自身rollout生成的历史而非真实视频上下文进行训练。这减少了曝光偏差，但历史键值缓存仍然仅作为冻结的rollout状态被未来帧使用。因此，未来损失无法监督早期生成的潜变量如何被写入对后续视频潜变量生成更有用的键和值中。我们称之为历史上下文-梯度差距（historical context-gradient gap）。我们提出自梯度强迫（Self Gradient Forcing, SGF），一种两遍训练策略，无需通过完整的串行rollout反向传播即可恢复缺失的监督信号。第一遍执行无梯度的自回归rollout以匹配推理阶段，并在采样的去噪退出步记录自生成上下文和输入模型的噪声潜变量。第二遍对记录的退出步执行并行上下文梯度重建。生成上下文作为停止梯度的干净潜变量输入，而模型重新计算上下文KV表示和未来到上下文的因果注意力。因此，SGF在原生自回归训练目标内提供了缺失的记忆写入监督，利用未来视频潜变量上的损失训练模型将上下文编码为更有效的因果记忆。在多种初始化条件下进行的广泛长程逐帧和逐块实验中，SGF在主体身份保持、背景/布局一致性和时间稳定性方面实现了比Self Forcing更强的原生长视频外推能力。值得注意的是，仅使用5秒训练窗口，SGF即可外推至持续数分钟的视频。代码和模型将公开发布，以推动自回归视频生成的研究。

</details>


### [69] [Persian Pixel: A large-scale synthetic OCR dataset for Persian language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20385)
*Pouria Mahdi,Haq Nawaz Malik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对波斯语OCR因文字复杂性和数据匮乏而发展滞后的挑战，构建了包含超过34.3万高质量图像-文本对的合成数据集Persian Pixel，并用25种以上随机退化模型模拟真实文档伪影，为训练TrOCR、Donut等现代OCR架构提供了可扩展的开放资源。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 波斯语OCR远不如拉丁语系成熟，主要面临两大根本挑战：一是波斯-阿拉伯文字系统固有的复杂性（包括强制连写、上下文相关的字形变化、大量连字、变音符号摆放以及Naskh和Nastaliq等书写风格的风格差异）；二是大规模高质量标注数据集严重匮乏，手动标注成本高昂且劳动密集，形成了持续的数据瓶颈，阻碍了鲁棒OCR系统的开发和波斯文档数字化的进展。

Method: 本文提出Persian Pixel数据集，从精心筛选的七百万词波斯语语料库出发，利用SynthOCR-Gen渲染框架生成涵盖句子、段落和整页文档布局的超过34.3万高保真图像-文本对。生成流程精确建模了波斯脚本的排印特征（上下文字符连接、位置变体、变音符号放置及多种代表性波斯字体）。为弥合合成到真实的域差距，进一步用25种以上随机退化模型模拟真实文档采集伪影，包括墨迹渗散、纸张老化、模糊、光照变化、扫描仪缺陷、压缩伪影和多种噪声过程。

Result: Persian Pixel提供了可扩展的开放资源，成功克服了波斯语OCR标注数据长期匮乏的瓶颈，能够用于训练和微调TrOCR、Donut等基于Transformer的现代OCR架构，为波斯文档分析、历史手稿数字化和端到端文档理解奠定了坚实基础。

Conclusion: 程序化合成数据生成为低资源和文字复杂场景下的OCR发展提供了一种实用、经济且可扩展的替代手动标注的方案，对推进波斯语OCR研究具有重要意义。

Original Abstract: Optical Character Recognition (OCR) for Persian remains substantially less mature than for Latin-script languages despite Persian being spoken by more than 110 million people across multiple countries. This gap arises from two fundamental challenges: the intrinsic complexity of the Perso-Arabic writing system and the limited availability of large-scale, high-quality annotated datasets. Persian script exhibits obligatory cursive connectivity, context-dependent glyph shaping, extensive ligatures, diacritic placement, and stylistic variation across writing forms such as Naskh and Nastaliq, all of which significantly complicate text recognition. At the same time, the high cost and labor-intensive nature of manual annotation have created a persistent data bottleneck, limiting the development of robust OCR systems and slowing progress in Persian document digitization.In this paper, we introduce Persian Pixel, a comprehensive synthetic OCR dataset specifically designed to address these challenges. Comprising over 343,000 high-fidelity image text pairs, the dataset spans sentence, paragraph, and full-page document layouts generated from a carefully curated seven-million-word Persian corpus using the SynthOCR-Gen rendering framework. The generation pipeline faithfully models the typographic characteristics of Persian script, including contextual character joining, positional glyph variants, diacritic placement, and multiple representative Persian typefaces. To bridge the synthetic-to-real domain gap, the rendered images are further enriched with more than twenty-five stochastic degradation models that emulate realistic document acquisition artifacts, including ink bleed, paper aging, blur, illumination variation, scanner imperfections, compression artifacts, and multiple noise processes.By overcoming the long-standing scarcity of annotated Persian OCR data, Persian Pixel provides a scalable and openly available resource for training and fine-tuning modern OCR architectures, including transformer-based models such as TrOCR and Donut. The dataset establishes a strong foundation for research in Persian document analysis, historical manuscript digitization, and end-to-end document understanding, while demonstrating that programmatic synthetic data generation offers a practical, cost-effective, and scalable alternative to manual annotation for advancing OCR in low-resource and typographically complex scripts.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管波斯语在多个国家有超过1.1亿使用人口，其光学字符识别（OCR）技术远不如拉丁语系语言成熟。这一差距源于两个根本性挑战：波斯-阿拉伯文字系统固有的复杂性，以及大规模高质量标注数据集的匮乏。波斯文字表现出强制连写、上下文相关的字形变化、大量连字、变音符号摆放以及Naskh和Nastaliq等书写形式之间的风格差异，这些都极大地增加了文本识别的难度。同时，手动标注的高成本和劳动密集型特性造成了持续的数据瓶颈，限制了鲁棒OCR系统的开发，也阻碍了波斯文档数字化的进展。本文提出Persian Pixel——一个专为应对这些挑战而设计的综合性合成OCR数据集。该数据集包含超过34.3万对高保真图像-文本对，涵盖从精心筛选的七百万词波斯语语料库出发，利用SynthOCR-Gen渲染框架生成的句子、段落和整页文档布局。生成流程精确建模了波斯脚本的排印特征，包括上下文字符连接、位置字形变体、变音符号放置以及多种代表性波斯字体。为弥合合成到真实的域差距，渲染后的图像进一步经过25种以上随机退化模型的增强，模拟真实的文档采集伪影，包括墨迹渗散、纸张老化、模糊、光照变化、扫描仪缺陷、压缩伪影和多种噪声过程。通过克服波斯语OCR标注数据长期匮乏的瓶颈，Persian Pixel为训练和微调现代OCR架构（包括基于Transformer的模型如TrOCR和Donut）提供了可扩展的开放资源。该数据集为波斯文档分析、历史手稿数字化和端到端文档理解研究奠定了坚实基础，同时证明了程序化合成数据生成为推进低资源和文字复杂脚本的OCR发展提供了一种实用、经济且可扩展的手动标注替代方案。

</details>


### [70] [PercepCap: Video Captioner with Structured Spatio-Temporal Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20389)
*Yifan Xu,Zihao Wang,Zhixiao Wang,Jiaming Zhang,Yichun Yang,Desen Meng,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PercepCap提出了一种感知感知的视频字幕框架，通过先显式生成包含物体轨迹和时间事件的时空感知轨迹，再基于此生成最终字幕，并采用两阶段训练策略（先感知后描述监督微调 + 感知锚定强化学习）和相应的数据构建方法，显著提升了视频字幕质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在视频字幕生成中缺乏对感知证据的显式暴露，导致时空感知错误仅体现在最终字幕中，难以直接识别和定位底层感知错误。

Method: 提出PercepCap框架，遵循"感知-描述"生成链：先产生包含物体轨迹和时间事件的时空感知轨迹，再基于此生成字幕。采用两阶段训练：1）先感知后描述监督微调（SFT），使模型从纯字幕生成适应感知-描述链；2）感知锚定强化学习（RL），通过联合奖励优化感知轨迹和字幕质量。同时提出字幕锚定感知数据构建管道，通过生成纯字幕、提取其中物体和事件、在视频中定位框和时间戳来构建与字幕对齐的感知训练数据。

Result: 在直接字幕生成和字幕到问答的评估中，PercepCap相较于Qwen3-VL基线持续改进，并展现出领先的字幕质量。

Conclusion: PercepCap通过显式化感知证据并采用两阶段训练策略，有效提升了视频字幕的时空感知准确性，验证了感知-描述生成链在视频理解任务中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Video captioning requires fine-grained spatio-temporal understanding of videos, including spatial perception of where objects are located and temporal perception of when events occur. Existing MLLMs usually generate captions directly from video inputs without exposing the perceptual evidence behind descriptions. As a result, mistakes in spatiotemporal perception are only observed in the final caption, making it difficult to identify the underlying perceptual errors directly. To address these issues, we present PercepCap, a perception-aware video captioning framework that makes perceptual evidence explicit before producing the final caption. Specifically, PercepCap follows a perceive-describe generation chain, where the model first produces a spatiotemporal perception trace comprising object trajectories and temporal events, and then generates the final caption conditioned on the perceived evidence. To support this, we design a two-stage training strategy. Perceive-then-Describe Supervised Fine-tuning adapts the model from caption-only generation to the proposed perceive-describe chain, while Perception-Grounded Reinforcement Learning optimizes perception trace and caption quality with joint rewards over perception chain and the final caption. To support our two-stage training, we introduce Caption-Anchored Perception Data Construction. This pipeline builds the SFT and RL training data by first generating a caption-only description, extracting the objects and events it mentions, and grounding them back in the video with boxes and timestamps. This yields caption-aligned perception data that provides solid training ground truth, ensuring that the explicit perception trace and final caption refer to the same objects and events. Across direct caption and caption-to-QA evaluation, PercepCap consistently improves upon the Qwen3-VL baseline and demonstrates leading caption quality.

中文摘要翻译: 视频字幕生成需要对视频进行细粒度的时空理解，包括物体所在位置的空间感知和事件发生时间的时间感知。现有的多模态大语言模型通常直接从视频输入生成字幕，而不暴露描述背后的感知证据。因此，时空感知中的错误仅能在最终字幕中被观察到，导致难以直接识别底层的感知错误。为解决这些问题，我们提出了PercepCap，一个感知感知的视频字幕框架，在生成最终字幕之前显式地呈现感知证据。具体而言，PercepCap遵循"感知-描述"生成链，模型首先产生包含物体轨迹和时间事件的时空感知轨迹，然后基于所感知的证据生成最终字幕。为支持这一过程，我们设计了一种两阶段训练策略。"先感知后描述"监督微调使模型从纯字幕生成适应到所提出的感知-描述链，而感知锚定强化学习通过联合奖励优化感知轨迹和字幕质量，同时奖励感知链和最终字幕。为支持两阶段训练，我们引入了字幕锚定感知数据构建管道。该管道首先生成纯字幕描述，提取其提及的物体和事件，并在视频中通过边界框和时间戳进行定位，从而构建SFT和RL训练数据。这产生了与字幕对齐的感知数据，提供了可靠的训练真值，确保显式感知轨迹和最终字幕指向相同的物体和事件。在直接字幕生成和字幕到问答评估中，PercepCap相较于Qwen3-VL基线持续改进，并展现了领先的字幕质量。

</details>


### [71] [ATSplat: Compact Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting with Adaptive Token Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20417)
*Cho In,Jeonghwan Cho,Mijin Yoo,Gim Hee Lee,Seon Joo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ATSplat提出了一种前馈式3D高斯泼溅框架，通过自适应3D令牌恢复场景自适应的原语分配能力，在减少5.7倍高斯数量的同时达到SOTA渲染质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有前馈式3DGS方法采用像素对齐的回归方式，导致原语数量和位置取决于图像分辨率和输入视角而非场景复杂度，产生密集且冗余的高斯集合，丧失了3DGS优化中场景自适应的容量分配能力。

Method: ATSplat首先从粗粒度块级深度和相机线索中提取稀疏的3D锚点令牌，形成场景的紧凑骨架；每个令牌通过可学习的3D偏移回归为局部高斯，解耦原语放置与输入图像网格；自适应令牌扩展模块根据渲染误差图监督的令牌级不确定性分数，选择性扩展高不确定性令牌。

Result: 在RealEstate10K和DL3DV数据集上，ATSplat在减少5.7倍以上高斯数量的同时达到SOTA渲染质量；从12张512×960分辨率输入图像出发，在单GPU上不到1秒完成重建，以仅311K高斯实现1136 FPS的高质量新视角渲染。

Conclusion: ATSplat通过稀疏到自适应的建模方式，有效恢复了3DGS优化中场景自适应的容量分配能力，在保持紧凑表示的同时集中原语于具有挑战性的区域，实现了效率与质量的双重提升。

Original Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieves high-quality novel-view synthesis by optimizing freely placed primitives in 3D and adaptively densifying them in under-reconstructed regions. However, this scene-adaptive capacity allocation is largely lost in existing feed-forward 3DGS methods, which commonly regress Gaussians at input pixels and lift them along camera rays. Such pixel-aligned formulations make the number and placement of primitives depend on image resolution and input viewpoints rather than scene complexity, resulting in dense and often redundant Gaussian sets. We present ATSplat, a feed-forward 3DGS framework that restores the adaptive allocation capability of 3DGS optimization through Adaptive 3D Tokens. ATSplat first lifts coarse patch-level depth and camera cues into sparse 3D anchor tokens, forming a compact scaffold of the scene. Each token is then regressed into local Gaussians with learnable 3D offsets, decoupling primitive placement from input image grids. An Adaptive Token Expansion module predicts a token-level uncertainty score, supervised by rendering error maps, and selectively expands high-uncertainty tokens through learnable expansion layers. This sparse-to-adaptive formulation enables ATSplat to concentrate primitives in challenging regions while maintaining a compact representation. Experiments on two representative datasets, RealEstate10K and DL3DV, show that ATSplat achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality while reducing the number of Gaussians by more than $5.7\times$ compared with dense feed-forward 3DGS methods. From 12 input images at $512 \times 960$ resolution, ATSplat completes reconstruction in less than a second using a single commercial GPU, and renders high-quality novel views at 1136 FPS ($512 \times 960$) with only 311K Gaussians.

中文摘要翻译: 3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）通过在3D空间中优化自由放置的原语并自适应地在欠重建区域进行致密化，实现了高质量的新视角合成。然而，这种场景自适应容量分配能力在现有前馈式3DGS方法中基本丧失——这些方法通常将高斯回归到输入像素上并沿相机射线提升。这种像素对齐的公式使得原语的数量和位置依赖于图像分辨率和输入视角而非场景复杂度，导致生成密集且往往冗余的高斯集合。本文提出ATSplat，一种前馈式3DGS框架，通过自适应3D令牌恢复了3DGS优化的自适应容量分配能力。ATSplat首先将粗粒度的块级深度和相机线索提升为稀疏的3D锚点令牌，形成场景的紧凑骨架。每个令牌随后通过可学习的3D偏移回归为局部高斯，从而将原语放置与输入图像网格解耦。自适应令牌扩展模块预测一个由渲染误差图监督的令牌级不确定性分数，并通过可学习的扩展层选择性地扩展高不确定性令牌。这种稀疏到自适应的公式使ATSplat能够在保持紧凑表示的同时将原语集中在具有挑战性的区域。在两个代表性数据集RealEstate10K和DL3DV上的实验表明，ATSplat在实现最先进渲染质量的同时，相比密集前馈式3DGS方法减少了5.7倍以上的高斯数量。从12张512×960分辨率的输入图像出发，ATSplat使用单块商用GPU在不到一秒内完成重建，并以仅311K高斯在512×960分辨率下以1136 FPS的速率渲染高质量的新视角。

</details>

### [72] [Beyond Confidence in AI-Assisted Colonoscopy: A Spatial, Temporal and Quality-Aware Audit Framework for Endoscopic AI Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19372)
*Roberto Alcaraz Machado,Ana Lucía Rodríguez Blanco*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出EndoExplain，一个轻量级、可复现的内镜AI审计框架，通过分离分类置信度、病灶分割、归因图、质量指标和时间上下文等多维度信号，弥补传统计算机辅助检测系统仅依赖帧级置信度的不足，实验验证了其可审计性和临床可读性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有AI辅助结肠镜系统通常仅报告帧级置信度，但置信度本身无法判断预测在空间上是否合理、在时间上是否持续、或在图像质量退化时是否可靠，需要更全面的审计框架来分离这些常被混淆的信号。

Method: EndoExplain集成了分类置信度、病灶分割、CAM风格视觉归因、归因-掩膜对齐、帧质量指标和时间事件摘要，通过多方法归因审计（top-20%）、外部冻结模型检查、人类评审的剪辑级时间基准和临床专家外部合理性评估来验证框架有效性。

Result: EfficientNet-B0在HyperKvasir十类分类上准确率0.9280、ROC-AUC 0.9969；U-Net++ EfficientNet-B1分割Dice 0.9318、IoU 0.8826；Eigen-CAM归因-掩膜一致性最强，Grad-CAM++最弱；外部数据上归因方法排序保持不变但重叠度有数据集依赖性；视频事件F1为0.8081（阈值0.85，重叠≥1秒）。

Conclusion: 将置信度、定位、归因、质量元数据和时间上下文分离为可审计的输出信号，具有临床可读性，但本研究为回顾性研究原型，非临床验证的医疗器械。

Original Abstract: AI-assisted colonoscopy systems commonly report frame-level confidence scores, but confidence alone does not show whether a prediction is spatially plausible, temporally persistent or reliable under degraded image quality. We propose EndoExplain, a lightweight and reproducible audit framework for endoscopic AI review. The framework integrates classification confidence, lesion segmentation, CAM-style visual attribution, attribution-mask alignment, frame-quality indicators and temporal event summarisation, with the aim of separating signals that are often conflated in computer-aided detection pipelines. On HyperKvasir, the selected EfficientNet-B0 classifier reaches 0.9280 test accuracy over ten endoscopic classes and 0.9969 ROC-AUC for the polyp_family versus rest view. The selected U-Net++ EfficientNet-B1 segmenter reaches Dice 0.9318 and IoU 0.8826 on the segmented test split. A strict top-20% multi-method attribution audit shows that attribution method strongly changes explanation-mask agreement: Eigen-CAM gives the strongest overlap, whereas Grad-CAM++ remains weakly aligned. A frozen-model external sanity check on ETIS-LaribPolypDB and CVC-ClinicDB preserves this attribution-method ranking while showing dataset-dependent overlap. A human-reviewed clip-level temporal benchmark over 60 HyperKvasir videos reaches event F1 0.8081 at the pre-specified threshold 0.85 using one-to-one event matching with overlap >= 1 s. A clinician-informed external plausibility review supported the clinical readability of separating confidence, localisation, attribution, quality metadata and temporal context. The resulting cockpit-style review layer presents these signals as distinct auditable outputs. This is a retrospective research prototype and not a clinically validated medical device.

中文摘要翻译: AI辅助结肠镜系统通常报告帧级置信度分数，但仅凭置信度无法判断预测在空间上是否合理、在时间上是否持久或在图像质量退化时是否可靠。我们提出了EndoExplain，一个轻量级且可复现的内镜AI审计框架。该框架整合了分类置信度、病灶分割、CAM风格的视觉归因、归因-掩膜对齐、帧质量指标和时间事件摘要，旨在分离计算机辅助检测流程中常被混淆的信号。在HyperKvasir数据集上，所选的EfficientNet-B0分类器在十类内镜分类上达到0.9280的测试准确率，在息肉家族与其余类别的二分类上达到0.9969的ROC-AUC。所选的U-Net++ EfficientNet-B1分割器在分割测试集上达到Dice 0.9318和IoU 0.8826。严格的前20%多方法归因审计显示，归因方法显著改变了解释-掩膜一致性：Eigen-CAM产生的重叠最强，而Grad-CAM++仍保持弱对齐。在ETIS-LaribPolypDB和CVC-ClinicDB上的冻结模型外部健全性检查保持了这种归因方法的排序，同时显示出数据集依赖的重叠度。在60个HyperKvasir视频上进行的、经人工评审的剪辑级时间基准测试中，使用一对一时间匹配（重叠≥1秒）在预设阈值0.85下达到了事件F1为0.8081。经临床医生参与的外部合理性评估支持将置信度、定位、归因、质量元数据和时间上下文分离的临床可读性。由此产生的驾驶舱式审查层将这些信号呈现为不同的可审计输出。这是一个回顾性研究原型，而非临床验证的医疗器械。

</details>


### [73] [Group-of-Latents: Perceptual Video Compression at Extreme Bitrates via Masked Latent Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19437)
*Shaokang Wang,Jinchang Xu,Peidong Jia,Zhijian Hao,Siyuan Qian,Fei Zhao,Rui Ma,Xiaozhu Ju,Jian Tang,Xiaodong Xie,Shanghang Zhang,Huizhu Jia*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于扩散Transformer先验的统一生成式框架，在极低比特率（<0.005 bpp）下实现高感知质量视频压缩，通过潜空间分组策略和深度压缩模块保持感知锚点，并利用DiT潜变量去噪模块零代价重建时序动态。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频压缩算法在极低比特率下感知质量优化不足，该场景尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 1) 在因果分词器潜空间中提出灵活的分组潜变量(GoL)策略，将潜流划分为帧内I-潜变量和帧间P-潜变量；2) 深度压缩模块(I-DCM)编码关键I-潜变量，以最小开销保留感知锚点；3) 基于DiT的统一潜变量去噪模块(U-LDM)细化帧内纹理并从噪声合成P-潜变量，零额外比特率重建时序动态。

Result: 在极低比特率（<0.005 bpp）下达到最先进的感知保真度，具有丰富的空间细节和鲁棒的时序一致性。

Conclusion: 提出的统一生成式框架有效解决了极低比特率视频压缩中的感知质量挑战，代码将开源。

Original Abstract: Most existing video compression algorithms follow a paradigm of transformation and quantization, optimizing the trade-off between distortion and bitrate. However, extremely low-bitrate compression remains an underexplored frontier where perceptual quality optimization under severely constrained coding resources has not been adequately addressed. In this paper, we propose a unified generative framework that leverages pre-trained Diffusion Transformer (DiT) priors to achieve high perceptual quality at extremely low bitrates. We first introduce a flexible Group-of-Latents (GoL) strategy within the latent space of a causal tokenizer, explicitly partitioning the latent stream into intra $I$-latents and inter $P$-latents. The Deep Compression Module (I-DCM) then encodes key $I$-latents to preserve perceptual anchors with minimal overhead. Building upon these anchors, the DiT-based Unified Latent Denoising Module (U-LDM) refines intra-frame textures and synthesizes $P$-latents from noise, reconstructing temporal dynamics at zero additional bitrate cost. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method uniquely operates in the extreme-low-bitrate regime (e.g., (<0.005) bpp), achieving state-of-the-art perceptual fidelity with rich spatial details and robust temporal consistency. The code will be made publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: 大多数现有的视频压缩算法遵循变换与量化的范式，优化失真与比特率之间的权衡。然而，极低比特率压缩仍是一个尚未充分探索的前沿领域，在编码资源严重受限的情况下，感知质量优化尚未得到充分解决。本文提出一个统一的生成式框架，利用预训练的扩散Transformer(DiT)先验，在极低比特率下实现高感知质量。我们首先在因果分词器的潜空间中引入灵活的分组潜变量(GoL)策略，将潜流显式划分为帧内I-潜变量和帧间P-潜变量。深度压缩模块(I-DCM)对关键I-潜变量进行编码，以最小开销保留感知锚点。基于这些锚点，基于DiT的统一潜变量去噪模块(U-LDM)细化帧内纹理并从噪声中合成P-潜变量，以零额外比特率成本重建时序动态。大量实验表明，我们的方法独特地在极低比特率（例如<0.005 bpp）下运行，实现了具有丰富空间细节和鲁棒时序一致性的最先进感知保真度。代码将公开提供。

</details>


### [74] [BLUE: Semantics-Preserving Video Compression for Efficient Vision-Language Surveillance Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19515)
*Shubham Baid,Akash James,Sahil Chachra,Nishant Sinha,Kunal Kislay*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: BLUE是一种面向固定摄像头的监控视频压缩方法，通过抑制静态背景冗余并保留前景活动，在不降低VLM语义推理质量的前提下，显著减少存储、传输和推理成本。实验表明，BLUE在VIRAT和CHAD数据集上与原始H.265的VLM评分几乎无差异，且可减少约53%的VLM调用次数。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 连续监控视频给企业视频分析系统带来了日益增长的存储、传输和推理负担。传统编解码器（如H.265）压缩视频时可能损害下游计算机视觉性能，且无法减少语义视频理解所需的VLM推理调用次数。

Method: BLUE是一种针对固定摄像头监控场景的压缩方法，核心思路是抑制静态背景冗余同时保留前景活动信息。将BLUE压缩后的H.265视频与原始H.265视频在VIRAT（106个片段、227个配对事件样本）和CHAD（54个人类活动异常片段）两个监控数据集上进行比较。对每个配对样本，使用相同的帧索引通过VLM字幕生成流程进行评估，并采用盲评协议将输出与人工标注的ground truth进行评分。

Result: 1) VIRAT数据集上，BLUE与原始H.265的VLM平均评分几乎无差异（差值约-0.01，满分10分）。2) CHAD数据集上，原始H.265和BLUE的评分分别为4.31和4.26，结果接近等效。3) VIRAT上压缩节省与VLM评分变化的相关系数r=0.004，表明更高压缩比不预示语义质量损失。4) BLUE将CHAD上skip-heavy P帧占比从1.4%提升至53.2%，估计可减少53%的VLM调用次数。

Conclusion: BLUE可作为一种面向监控视频的机器中心压缩层，在保持VLM语义性能的同时，有效降低带宽和推理成本。

Original Abstract: Continuous surveillance video creates a growing storage, transmission, and inference burden for enterprise video analytics systems. While modern codecs such as H.265 reduce bitrate for human-viewable video, aggressive compression can degrade downstream computer-vision performance and does not necessarily reduce the number of vision-language model (VLM) inference calls required for semantic video understanding. This paper evaluates BLUE, a fixed-camera surveillance compression approach that suppresses static-background redundancy while preserving foreground activity, for its effect on VLM-based event and anomaly understanding. We compare raw H.265 and BLUE-compressed H.265 video on two surveillance datasets: VIRAT, comprising 227 paired event samples from 106 clips, and CHAD, comprising 54 human-activity anomaly clips. For each pair, the same frame index is evaluated using a VLM captioning pipeline, and outputs are scored against annotation-derived ground truth using a blind judging protocol. The results show no measurable degradation in semantic inference quality. On VIRAT, the mean VLM score remains effectively unchanged between raw H.265 and BLUE, with a mean difference of approximately -0.01 on a 0-10 scale. On CHAD, raw H.265 and BLUE obtain near-equivalent mean scores of 4.31 and 4.26, respectively. Compression saving is also uncorrelated with VLM score change on VIRAT (r = 0.004), indicating that higher BLUE compression does not predict semantic quality loss. Beyond storage reduction, BLUE increases the share of skip-heavy P-frames on CHAD from 1.4% to 53.2%, enabling an estimated 53% reduction in VLM calls through packet-size-based frame skipping. These findings suggest that BLUE functions as a machine-centric compression layer for surveillance video, reducing bandwidth and inference cost while preserving VLM semantic performance.

中文摘要翻译: 连续监控视频给企业级视频分析系统带来了日益增长的存储、传输和推理负担。虽然现代编解码器（如H.265）可以降低供人观看的视频的比特率，但激进的压缩可能会损害下游计算机视觉性能，并且不一定能减少语义视频理解所需的视觉语言模型（VLM）推理调用次数。本文评估了BLUE——一种针对固定摄像头监控场景的压缩方法，该方法抑制静态背景冗余同时保留前景活动——对基于VLM的事件和异常理解的影响。我们在两个监控数据集上比较了原始H.265和经BLUE压缩后的H.265视频：VIRAT数据集包含来自106个片段的227个配对事件样本，CHAD数据集包含54个人类活动异常片段。对于每个配对样本，使用相同的帧索引通过VLM字幕生成流程进行评估，并采用盲评协议将输出与标注导出的真实标签进行评分。结果显示，语义推理质量没有出现可衡量的下降。在VIRAT上，原始H.265和BLUE的VLM平均评分几乎保持不变，在0-10分尺度上平均差异约为-0.01。在CHAD上，原始H.265和BLUE获得的平均评分接近等效，分别为4.31和4.26。压缩节省与VIRAT上的VLM评分变化也不相关（r = 0.004），表明更高的BLUE压缩比并不预示语义质量损失。除了存储减少外，BLUE将CHAD上skip-heavy P帧的占比从1.4%提升至53.2%，通过基于数据包大小的帧跳帧机制，估计可减少53%的VLM调用次数。这些发现表明，BLUE可用作监控视频的机器中心压缩层，在降低带宽和推理成本的同时，保持VLM的语义性能。

</details>


### [75] [PRISM-DR: Per-lesion Retinal Inference with Specialist Models for Diabetic Retinopathy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19864)
*Zübeyr Özeren,Tansel Uyar*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM-DR，一种为糖尿病视网膜病变的四种病灶分别训练专用检测器的流水线，采用病灶特异性YOLO检测器、贝叶斯优化增强、逐病灶集成和病灶间抑制策略，在IDRiD数据集上达到mAP50 0.527和F1 0.529，证明了针对每个病灶单独检测相比单一多类模型更实用有效。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 糖尿病视网膜病变的早期病灶（微动脉瘤、出血、硬性渗出、软性渗出）在尺寸、颜色、形态和患病率上差异显著，单一多类模型容易偏向常见且易检测的类别，忽略罕见且困难的病灶。因此需要一种病灶特异性的检测流水线来处理这种异质性。

Method: PRISM-DR流水线包含：感兴趣区域裁剪、眼底图像特异性预处理、四个并行的YOLO单类检测器（每个病灶一个）、图像分块（tiling）、五折交叉验证的逐病灶集成、以及基于病灶物理尺寸和临床优先级而非置信度的病灶间抑制步骤。每个病灶独立选择最优的YOLO生成版本，并通过贝叶斯优化调整数据增强策略。

Result: 在IDRiD数据集上采用分层五折交叉验证，测试集mAP50为0.527，F1得分为0.529，硬性渗出的AP50最高达0.561。模型在成像尺度接近IDRiD的数据集上零样本迁移良好，但当视野和分辨率差异较大时性能下降。整体绝对结果中等，反映了训练集规模小且任务难度大。

Conclusion: 将每种病灶作为独立的检测问题来处理，是替代单一多类模型的一种实用选择。尽管受限于小规模单源训练集导致绝对性能中等，但病灶特异性方法能更好地应对病灶间的异质性，且具有一定的迁移能力。

Original Abstract: Diabetic retinopathy is a leading cause of preventable blindness; its early lesions are small, low contrast, and easily missed in manual screening. Most automated detectors handle the four non-proliferative DR lesions: microaneurysms, hemorrhages, hard exudates, and soft exudates, with a single multi-class model, even though these lesions differ sharply in size, color, morphology, and prevalence, so a shared model favors common, easy classes over rare, difficult ones. We present PRISM-DR, a lesion-specific pipeline that trains one single-class detector per lesion, each with its own configuration. From a raw fundus image, the pipeline applies region of interest cropping, fundus-specific preprocessing, four parallel YOLO detectors, tiling, per-lesion ensembling of five cross-validation folds, and an inter-lesion suppression step that resolves overlaps by physical lesion size and clinical priority rather than confidence. Per lesion, the best of five YOLO generations is selected, and augmentation is tuned by Bayesian optimization. Trained on IDRiD with stratified five-fold cross-validation, the system reaches a test mAP50 of 0.527 and F1 of 0.529, highest AP50 on hard exudates with 0.561. Without fine-tuning, the models transfer well where the imaging scale is close to IDRiD and degrade as field of view and resolution depart. These modest absolute results reflect a small single-source training set and a difficult task; however, treating each lesion as a separate detection problem is a practical alternative to a single multi-class model.

中文摘要翻译: 糖尿病视网膜病变是可预防性失明的主要原因；其早期病灶微小、对比度低，在人工筛查中容易漏诊。大多数自动检测器使用单个多类模型来处理四种非增殖性糖尿病视网膜病变病灶——微动脉瘤、出血、硬性渗出和软性渗出，尽管这些病灶在尺寸、颜色、形态和患病率上差异显著，因此共享模型会偏向常见且易检测的类别，忽略罕见且困难的类别。我们提出PRISM-DR，一种病灶特异性流水线，为每个病灶训练一个单类检测器，每个检测器拥有独立的配置。从原始眼底图像出发，该流水线依次执行感兴趣区域裁剪、眼底特异性预处理、四个并行的YOLO检测器、图像分块、五折交叉验证的逐病灶集成，以及基于病灶物理尺寸和临床优先级而非置信度的病灶间抑制步骤。每个病灶独立选择最优的YOLO生成版本，并通过贝叶斯优化调整数据增强策略。在IDRiD数据集上采用分层五折交叉验证训练，系统在测试集上达到mAP50为0.527，F1得分为0.529，硬性渗出的AP50最高达0.561。在无需微调的情况下，模型在成像尺度接近IDRiD的数据集上迁移良好，而当视野和分辨率差异较大时性能下降。这些中等水平的绝对结果反映了训练集规模小且任务难度大；然而，将每种病灶作为独立的检测问题来处理，是替代单一多类模型的一种实用选择。

</details>

### [76] [Native Multi-Dimensional Subquadratic Operators via Input Dependent Long Convolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19378)
*David R. Wessels,Farhad Ramezanghorbani,David W. Romero,Alireza Moradzadeh,Olivia Viessmann,Maksim Zhdanov,John St. John,Ken Janik,David M Knigge,Yucheng Tang,Erik J Bekkers,Saee Gopal Paliwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyenaND是一种次二次、全局、输入依赖的多维数据算子，通过隐式参数化的全局多维卷积核直接作用于数据原生几何结构，在基因组学、计算机视觉、医学成像和PDE建模等多个领域达到或超越注意力基线性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有次二次替代方案在处理多维数据时需要妥协：标准卷积缺乏全局感受野和输入依赖性，而循环模型需要将图像、体积等数据栅格化为违反空间结构的1D扫描顺序。因此需要一种能直接作用于多维数据原生几何结构、兼具全局感受野和输入依赖性的高效算子。

Method: 提出HyenaND算子，通过隐式参数化的全局、输入依赖的多维卷积核，直接对多维数据的原生几何结构进行卷积操作。同时开发了CUDA实现nSubQ，融合FFT-卷积路径，将HyenaND的O(L log L)复杂度转化为实际加速。

Result: 在长上下文基因组学、计算机视觉、医学成像和PDE建模等多个任务上，纯HyenaND堆叠可匹配强注意力基线的准确率；混合配置（交错HyenaND与注意力层）则优于纯注意力和基于循环的强混合基线。

Conclusion: HyenaND作为一种次二次、全局、输入依赖的多维算子，能够有效替代注意力机制处理多维数据，在保持高效计算的同时达到或超越注意力方法的性能，尤其混合配置展现了更优的潜力。

Original Abstract: Subquadratic alternatives to attention require compromises when applied to multi-dimensional data: standard convolutions lack global receptive fields and input dependency, while recurrent models require rasterizing data such as images, volumes, and partial differential equation (PDE) into an ad-hoc $1\rm D$ scan order that violates their spatial structure. We introduce \textit{HyenaND}, a subquadratic, global, input-dependent operator that acts directly on the native geometry of multidimensional data through convolutions with implicitly parametrized global, input-dependent multi-dimensional convolutional kernels. Our CUDA implementation, \texttt{nSubQ}, fuses the FFT-convolution path to turn HyenaND's $\mathcal{O}(L \log L)$ scaling into wall-clock speedups. Across long-context genomics, computer vision, medical imaging, and PDE modeling, pure HyenaND stacks match the accuracy of strong attention baselines, while hybrid configurations that interleave HyenaND and attention layers outperform both pure attention and strong recurrence-based hybrids.

中文摘要翻译: 当应用于多维数据时，注意力的次二次替代方案需要做出妥协：标准卷积缺乏全局感受野和输入依赖性，而循环模型需要将图像、体积和偏微分方程（PDE）等数据栅格化为违反其空间结构的特定1D扫描顺序。我们提出了\textit{HyenaND}，一种次二次、全局、输入依赖的算子，它通过隐式参数化的全局、输入依赖的多维卷积核，直接作用于多维数据的原生几何结构上进行卷积。我们的CUDA实现\texttt{nSubQ}融合了FFT-卷积路径，将HyenaND的$\mathcal{O}(L \log L)$缩放转化为实际墙钟加速。在长上下文基因组学、计算机视觉、医学成像和PDE建模中，纯HyenaND堆叠可匹配强注意力基线的准确率，而交错混合HyenaND与注意力层的混合配置则优于纯注意力和基于循环的强混合基线。

</details>


### [77] [Bayesian Wind Tunnels for Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19379)
*Siddhartha R Dalal,Vishal Misra,Abhay Parekh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer是否能在贝叶斯模型选择中识别正确的假设类别。通过构建“贝叶斯风洞”可控环境，发现小规模Transformer（2.8M参数）在纯关系任务（对合函数）上能精确匹配贝叶斯最优（0.01-bit熵差异），但在需要算术运算的任务上，整数token成功而符号token完全失败，且该边界在112倍参数缩放下依然存在。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 以往工作证明Transformer能在固定假设类内进行精确贝叶斯滤波，但能否进行更高层次的贝叶斯模型选择——即从数据中识别正确的假设类别——尚不清楚。本文旨在探究Transformer在这一能力上的表现及其局限性。

Method: 提出“模型选择贝叶斯风洞”这一可控实验环境，其中假设类别上的真实后验概率可闭式求解。利用“无不动点对合”（满足f(f(x))=x的纯关系函数）作为测试任务，比较整数token与不透明符号（含义每episode变化）下的表现。进一步扩展到非嵌套比较（对合vs三循环），并引入算术运算条件（模加/模乘）以测试感知访问条件。

Result: 1）2.8M参数Transformer在纯关系任务上达到0.01-bit熵差异的贝叶斯最优匹配；2）非嵌套比较（对合vs三循环）的类后验MAE低于0.001；3）算术条件（模加/模乘）下，整数token成功但符号token完全失败，该边界在2.8M到316M参数缩放下持续存在；4）固定重标号的不透明符号成功（0.009-bit MAE），表明稳定语义是关键；5）头部子任务诊断将失败定位在头部反转与算术的组合而非头部解析本身；6）前沿大语言模型呈现定性贝叶斯行为但校准差距大（~55x）。

Conclusion: Transformer具备超出简单性/子集偏见的真正贝叶斯模型选择能力，但存在尖锐的感知访问条件：当判别统计量需要算术运算时，模型选择成功与否取决于token是否具有稳定语义（整数身份）。稳定语义使电路编译成为可能，而纯关系学习在无稳定语义时也能成功。该能力边界在参数大幅缩放后仍不改变，表明这是Transformer架构的固有限制。

Original Abstract: Prior work has shown that transformers can perform exact Bayesian filtering within a fixed
  hypothesis class. Can they also perform Bayesian model selection -- identifying the correct
  hypothesis class from data? We introduce model-selection Bayesian wind tunnels: controlled
  environments where ground-truth posteriors over hypothesis classes are available in closed
  form. Using fixed-point-free involutions -- whose defining property f(f(x))=x is purely
  relational -- a 2.8M-parameter transformer achieves 0.01-bit entropy agreement with the
  Bayesian optimum (3 seeds), with both integer tokens and opaque symbols whose meanings
  change every episode. This extends to non-nested comparisons: involutions vs. 3-cycles
  (where neither class is a subset of the other) achieve class-posterior MAE under 0.001,
  demonstrating genuine model selection beyond simplicity/subset bias. We then identify a
  sharp perceptual access condition: when the discriminative statistic requires arithmetic --
  modular addition (rotations) or multiplication (f(x)=cx mod p) -- model selection succeeds
  with integer tokens but fails completely with opaque symbols, and this boundary persists
  under 112x scaling (2.8M to 316M parameters). A stationarity control confirms the operative
  factor: opaque tokens with a fixed relabeling succeed (0.009-bit MAE), showing that stable
  semantics, not integer identity, enable circuit compilation. Header subtask diagnostics
  localize the failure to the composition of header inversion with arithmetic rather than
  header parsing itself. Probing frontier LLMs on the same tasks shows qualitative Bayesian
  behavior but a large calibration gap (~55x), measured through lossy probes and therefore
  directional rather than exact.

中文摘要翻译: 已有工作表明，Transformer可以在固定假设类别内执行精确的贝叶斯滤波。那么，它们是否也能进行贝叶斯模型选择——即从数据中识别出正确的假设类别？我们引入了模型选择贝叶斯风洞：一种可控环境，其中假设类别的真实后验概率可以闭式求解。利用无不动点对合（其定义性质f(f(x))=x纯属关系性），一个2.8M参数的Transformer实现了与贝叶斯最优解的0.01-bit熵一致性（3个随机种子），无论是在整数token还是在每episode含义发生变化的不透明符号上均成立。这一结果扩展到了非嵌套比较：对合与三循环（其中任一类都不是另一类的子集）的类别后验MAE低于0.001，展示了超越简单性/子集偏见的真正模型选择能力。随后，我们识别出一个尖锐的感知访问条件：当判别统计量需要算术运算时——模加法（旋转）或模乘法（f(x)=cx mod p）——模型选择在整数token上成功，但在不透明符号上完全失败，并且这一边界在112倍参数缩放（从2.8M到316M参数）下持续存在。平稳性控制实验确认了关键因素：使用固定重标号的不透明符号能够成功（0.009-bit MAE），表明使电路编译成为可能的是稳定语义，而非整数身份本身。头部子任务诊断将失败定位在头部反转与算术的组合上，而非头部解析本身。在相同任务上探测前沿大语言模型，显示出定性的贝叶斯行为，但存在较大的校准差距（约55倍），由于是通过有损探针测量，该结果指示方向而非精确数值。

</details>


### [78] [CruiseBench: A Real-Flight-Aligned N-CMAPSS Benchmark for Engine RUL Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19380)
*Pu Cheng,Qiang Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CruiseBench，一个基于N-CMAPSS数据集的巡航阶段RUL基准，通过CPM-N-CMAPSS掩码提取巡航段数据，并采用固定协议实现可复现的RUL模型对比。实验表明TSMixer取得最佳性能，消融研究验证了飞行阶段选择等因素对结果的影响。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: N-CMAPSS数据集虽然更真实地模拟了航空发动机的退化过程，但其完整的飞行记录增加了数据量，并将退化特征与运行状态变化混淆在一起，导致预处理选择复杂化，使得RUL建模性能的直接比较变得困难。因此需要构建一个可控、标准化的子基准来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出CruiseBench基准，核心是CPM-N-CMAPSS掩码工具，通过common-altitude方法识别并存储每个循环内的巡航阶段区间，对九个子数据集进行掩码处理。采用固定协议：使用场景描述符和实测传感器作为输入，排除虚拟传感器、健康参数和辅助元数据，保留原始分辨率窗口，应用数据集级RUL上限。

Result: 在CruiseBench-eta5-W256-S10设置下，TSMixer取得最低平均RMSE（3.4±1.71）和Saxena分数（(2.50±2.99)×10^4）。消融研究表明飞行阶段选择、时间降采样方法和RUL上限阈值会影响报告结果。

Conclusion: CruiseBench通过固定的巡航阶段协议，为受控的RUL模型比较提供了可复现的子基准；CPM-N-CMAPSS则为未来的迁移学习和领域自适应研究提供了阶段特定的数据基础。

Original Abstract: Remaining useful life (RUL) prediction estimates how long an engine can continue safe operation and is central to maintenance planning. N-CMAPSS extends C-MAPSS by simulating run-to-failure aero-engine trajectories using recorded real-flight profiles and retaining complete within-flight time series rather than cycle-level snapshots. However, this added realism reduces evaluation control because full-flight records increase data volume and entangle degradation cues with operating-regime variation, complicating preprocessing choices and direct comparisons of RUL modeling performance. To mitigate this issue, this paper proposes CruiseBench, a cruise-stage RUL benchmark derived from N-CMAPSS. It introduces CPM-N-CMAPSS (Cruising-Period Mask for N-CMAPSS), a mask artifact that stores cycle-local cruising intervals identified by the common-altitude method for the nine accessible subdatasets. CruiseBench applies a fixed protocol to the masked rows, using scenario descriptors and measured sensors as inputs while excluding virtual sensors, health parameters, and auxiliary metadata from the feature tensor, preserving native-resolution windows, and applying dataset-wise RUL caps. Experiments with LSTM, GRU, TCN, and TSMixer provide baseline results for this setting. Under CruiseBench-eta5-W256-S10, TSMixer obtains the lowest average RMSE, $3.4\pm1.71$, and Saxena score, $(2.50\pm2.99)\times 10^{4}$. Ablation studies show that flight-stage selection, temporal downscaling method, and RUL-cap threshold affect reported results. With its fixed cruise-stage protocol, CruiseBench provides a reproducible sub-benchmark for controlled RUL model comparison and CPM-N-CMAPSS provides a stage-specific data foundation for future transfer-learning and domain-adaptation studies.

中文摘要翻译: 剩余使用寿命（RUL）预测用于估计发动机能够继续安全运行的时间，是维护计划的核心。N-CMAPSS通过使用记录的飞行剖面模拟航空发动机的从运行到失效的轨迹，并保留完整的飞行内时间序列而非循环级快照，对C-MAPSS进行了扩展。然而，这种增加的现实性降低了评估的可控性，因为完整的飞行记录增加了数据量，并将退化线索与运行状态变化纠缠在一起，使得预处理选择复杂化，并使得RUL建模性能的直接比较变得困难。为缓解这一问题，本文提出了CruiseBench，一个源自N-CMAPSS的巡航阶段RUL基准。它引入了CPM-N-CMAPSS（巡航时段掩码），一种掩码工件，存储了通过通用高度方法为九个可访问子数据集识别的循环局部巡航区间。CruiseBench对掩码行应用固定协议，使用场景描述符和实测传感器作为输入，同时从特征张量中排除虚拟传感器、健康参数和辅助元数据，保留原始分辨率窗口，并应用数据集级RUL上限。使用LSTM、GRU、TCN和TSMixer进行的实验为该设置提供了基线结果。在CruiseBench-eta5-W256-S10下，TSMixer取得了最低的平均RMSE，即3.4±1.71，以及Saxena分数，即（2.50±2.99）×10^4。消融研究表明，飞行阶段选择、时间降尺度方法和RUL上限阈值会影响报告结果。凭借其固定的巡航阶段协议，CruiseBench为受控的RUL模型比较提供了可复现的子基准，而CPM-N-CMAPSS为未来的迁移学习和领域自适应研究提供了阶段特定的数据基础。

</details>


### [79] [Air Quality Arena: A Large-Scale Multi-Region Ground Monitoring Dataset and Benchmark for Air Quality Forecasting with Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19381)
*Rishi Bharadwaj,Manik Gupta,Pandarasamy Arjunan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Air Quality Arena (AQA)，一个大规模多国多污染物空气质量基准数据集和评测平台，系统评估了11个时间序列基础模型在短期空气质量预测上的性能，发现这些模型在零样本场景下优于传统基线，其中跨模态架构（结合视觉基础模型）表现最佳。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有空气质量预测基准在地理范围和污染物覆盖面上过于狭窄，且未能评估最新一代时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在真实大规模数据上的表现，因此需要更全面的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了覆盖7个国家4大洲、6种主要污染物、超过14,000个站-污染物序列的AQA数据集，并在该数据集上对11个领先的时间序列基础模型和经典基线方法进行短期空气质量预测任务的系统评测。

Result: 时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）作为零样本预测器表现有效，且始终优于经典基线方法；其中表现最佳的模型采用了跨模态架构，利用视觉基础模型进行时间序列预测。

Conclusion: 时间序列基础模型在空气质量预测任务上展现了强大的零样本能力，跨模态架构（融合视觉基础模型）为该领域提供了新的性能突破方向，AQA可作为未来空气质量预测研究的标准化基准。

Original Abstract: Air pollution causes an estimated 7.9 million premature deaths annually, making accurate forecasting a critical public health priority. Machine learning is increasingly being applied to forecast air pollution levels, yet existing benchmarks remain narrow in both geographic scope and pollutant coverage, and fail to evaluate the latest generation of time series foundation models (TSFMs) on real world, large scale data. We present Air Quality Arena (AQA), a large scale multi-country and multi-pollutant dataset (AQA-Data) and benchmark (AQA-Bench) to address this gap. AQA covers 6 major pollutants over a three year period across 7 diverse countries and 4 continents, with more than 14,000 station-pollutant series, aiming to provide a comprehensive benchmark for air quality tasks. We benchmark this dataset across 11 leading time series foundation models and classical baselines to assess performance on short-term air quality forecasting. Our results demonstrate that TSFMs are effective zero-shot forecasters and consistently outperform classical baselines, with our top-performing model employing a cross-modal architecture that leverages a vision foundation model for time series forecasting. AQA is publicly released at AirQualityArena.github.io

中文摘要翻译: 空气污染每年导致约790万人过早死亡，因此准确的预测成为公共卫生的关键优先事项。机器学习越来越多地被应用于预测空气污染水平，然而现有的基准在地理范围和污染物覆盖面上仍然狭窄，并且未能评估最新一代时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在真实大规模数据上的表现。我们提出了Air Quality Arena (AQA)，这是一个大规模的多国多污染物数据集（AQA-Data）和基准（AQA-Bench），以填补这一空白。AQA覆盖了7个不同国家和4大洲的6种主要污染物，时间跨度三年，包含超过14,000个站-污染物序列，旨在为空气质量任务提供全面的基准。我们在该数据集上对11个领先的时间序列基础模型和经典基线方法进行了基准测试，评估其在短期空气质量预测上的性能。结果表明，时间序列基础模型作为零样本预测器表现有效，且始终优于经典基线方法；其中表现最佳的模型采用了跨模态架构，利用视觉基础模型进行时间序列预测。AQA已在AirQualityArena.github.io公开发布。

</details>


### [80] [Challenges of Explainability in Continual Learning for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19382)
*Quentin Besnard,Emmanuel Doumard,Nicolas Labroche,Nicolas Ragot,Nicolas Ringuet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究可解释性在基于经验回放的持续学习框架中用于自适应时间序列预测的作用，通过在真实压电水位时间序列上分析注意力传播和梯度归因方法，揭示模型和采样行为随时间的演变规律，为非平稳预测中的数据选择和适应策略提供指导。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 深度学习模型在时间序列预测中表现出潜力，但在真实环境监测中因非平稳动态和有限可解释性而面临部署挑战。本文旨在将可解释性作为理解持续学习和自适应时间序列预测的核心工具。

Method: 采用PatchMixer、PatchTST和DLinear等神经预测架构，结合基于注意力的采样机制以支持模型随时间自适应。利用注意力传播（Attention Rollout）和基于梯度的归因方法（Grad-CAM）分析预测行为和采样策略，在持续学习框架下进行可解释性分析。

Result: 在呈现异质模式和机制转变的真实压电水位时间序列上，实验结果表明分析模型和采样行为能为持续学习框架的动态提供有价值的见解。揭示了归因模式随时间的演化规律，以及这些模式如何为非平稳预测场景中的数据选择和适应策略提供信息。

Conclusion: 可解释性不仅能用于理解持续学习行为，还能指导非平稳预测场景中的数据选择和适应策略，但同时也面临着相应的挑战和机遇。

Original Abstract: Deep learning models have shown strong potential for time series forecasting, yet their deployment in real-world environmental monitoring remains challenging due to non-stationary dynamics and limited explainability. In this work, we investigate explainability as a central tool for understanding continual learning in adaptive time series forecasting, with Experience Replay strategies. We study neural forecasting architectures such as PatchMixer, PatchTST and DLinear, augmented with attention-based sampling mechanisms to support model adaptation over time. Explainability is leveraged through attention rollout and gradient-based attribution methods (Grad-CAM) to analyze both predictive behavior and sampling strategies within a continual learning framework. Experiments conducted on real-world piezometric time series exhibiting heterogeneous patterns and regime shifts show that analyzing model and sampling behaviors provides valuable insights into the dynamics of the continual learning framework. Beyond predictive performance, our results highlight the challenges and opportunities of using explainability to understand continual learning behaviors, revealing how attribution patterns evolve over time and how they can inform data selection and adaptation strategies in non-stationary forecasting scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 深度学习模型在时间序列预测中展现出强大的潜力，但由于非平稳动态和有限的可解释性，它们在真实环境监测中的部署仍然面临挑战。本研究将可解释性作为理解持续学习在自适应时间序列预测中作用的核心工具，并结合经验回放策略进行探究。我们研究了PatchMixer、PatchTST和DLinear等神经预测架构，这些架构通过基于注意力的采样机制增强，以支持模型随时间自适应。通过注意力传播和基于梯度的归因方法（Grad-CAM），我们在持续学习框架内分析预测行为和采样策略。在呈现异质模式和机制转变的真实压电水位时间序列上进行的实验表明，分析模型和采样行为能为持续学习框架的动态提供有价值的见解。除了预测性能，我们的结果还凸显了利用可解释性理解持续学习行为的挑战与机遇，揭示了归因模式如何随时间演化，以及这些模式如何为非平稳预测场景中的数据选择和适应策略提供信息。

</details>


### [81] [SUM: Unified Geometric Surgery on Spatio-Temporal Adaptation Vectors for Federated Class Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19384)
*Jaeik Kim,Jaeyoung Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SUM框架，通过在服务器端对时空适应向量进行几何手术，统一解决联邦类增量学习中的空间干扰（异构客户端）和时间干扰（顺序任务）导致的灾难性遗忘问题，无需额外客户端开销。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有FCIL方法通常用分离机制分别处理空间和时间干扰，导致额外客户端计算或通信开销，且聚合过程中更新的方向性交互未受调控。需要一种统一方法同时解决两种耦合干扰。

Method: 将FCIL重新解释为统一的多任务学习问题，提出SUM（Spatio-Temporal Adaptation Vectors的统一几何手术）框架。空间SUM在每轮聚合中缓解客户端级干扰；因果在线时间SUM随时间移除跨任务干扰。所有操作仅在服务器端进行，不增加客户端计算、通信或存储开销。

Result: 在多种视觉和语言基准上，SUM相比现有FCIL方法提升高达22%，且对不可靠客户端鲁棒，保持计算效率。

Conclusion: SUM作为一种纯服务器端框架，通过几何手术统一解决了FCIL中时空耦合干扰问题，显著提升了性能且不引入额外客户端开销，具有实用性和可扩展性。

Original Abstract: Real-world intelligent systems often require both distributed collaboration across data-isolated clients and continual adaptation to evolving tasks. This setting naturally gives rise to Federated Class Incremental Learning (FCIL), which combines Federated Learning (FL) and Continual Learning (CL). However, their combination introduces two coupled sources of interference: spatial interference from heterogeneous clients and temporal interference from sequential tasks, jointly leading to Spatial-Temporal Catastrophic Forgetting (ST-CF). Existing approaches typically address spatial and temporal interference with separate mechanisms, often incurring additional client-side computation or communication, while leaving directional interactions among updates during aggregation unregulated. In this paper, we reinterpret FCIL as a unified multi-task learning problem, where both client and task updates are represented as adaptation vectors in a shared parameter space. Based on this view, we propose Unified Geometric Surgery on Spatio-Temporal Adaptation Vectors (SUM), a purely server-side framework that performs geometric surgery on adaptation vectors during aggregation. Spatial SUM mitigates client-level interference within each round, while causal online temporal SUM removes cross-task interference over time without additional client-side computation, communication, or memory beyond standard federated training. Empirically, SUM achieves up to 22% improvement over prior FCIL methods across diverse vision and language benchmarks while remaining robust to unreliable clients and maintaining computational efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 现实世界的智能系统通常需要跨数据隔离客户端的分布式协作，以及对不断演变的任务的持续适应。这种设置自然地引出了联邦类增量学习（FCIL），它结合了联邦学习（FL）和持续学习（CL）。然而，它们的结合引入了两种耦合的干扰源：来自异构客户端的空间干扰和来自顺序任务的时间干扰，共同导致了时空灾难性遗忘（ST-CF）。现有方法通常使用分离机制分别处理空间和时间干扰，往往带来额外的客户端计算或通信开销，同时未能调控聚合过程中更新之间的方向性交互。在本文中，我们将FCIL重新解释为一个统一的多任务学习问题，其中客户端和任务更新都被表示为共享参数空间中的适应向量。基于这一观点，我们提出了时空适应向量的统一几何手术（SUM），这是一个纯服务器端框架，在聚合过程中对适应向量执行几何手术。空间SUM缓解了每轮内的客户端级干扰，而因果在线时间SUM随时间消除了跨任务干扰，且无需额外的客户端计算、通信或内存开销，与标准联邦训练一致。实验表明，在多种视觉和语言基准上，SUM相比现有FCIL方法实现了高达22%的性能提升，同时对不可靠客户端保持鲁棒并维持计算效率。

</details>


### [82] [STN-TGAT: Top-K Portfolio Construction via Prior-Guided Graph Attention with Learnable Soft-Threshold Sparsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19385)
*Haoran Guo,Yutong Lu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STN-TGAT模型，融合时序Transformer和图注意力网络，结合NMI先验图与软阈值稀疏化机制进行股票排名和投资组合构建，在考虑交易成本等现实因素下优于基准模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有股票排名和投资组合构建方法未能同时有效建模时间动态性和横截面依赖关系，且缺乏对实际交易条件（如交易成本、权重分配等）的考虑，导致模型评估与实际投资表现存在差距。

Method: 提出STN-TGAT模型：1）时序Transformer捕获长程序列模式；2）图注意力网络建模动态股票间关系；3）基于NMI的先验图结合软阈值稀疏化机制，去噪同时保留有效连接；4）投资组合构建采用Top-50中选Top-5、显式权重分配和交易成本调整等实际策略。

Result: 在真实数据上，STN-TGAT在预测准确性和投资盈利能力（以投资组合回报衡量）方面持续优于多个基准模型。

Conclusion: 将决策对齐训练与自适应关系建模相结合，为数据驱动的投资组合构建提供了一致且实际有效的框架。

Original Abstract: This paper tackles the problem of stock ranking and portfolio construction under realistic investment settings by jointly modeling temporal dynamics and cross-sectional dependencies. We propose the Soft-Threshold NMI-prior Transformer Graph Attention Network (STN-TGAT), which integrates a temporal Transformer with a Graph Attention Network to capture long-horizon sequential patterns and dynamic inter-stock relationships. An NMI-based prior graph combined with a soft-threshold sparsification mechanism enhances structural robustness by mitigating noisy correlations while preserving informative connections. The portfolio formation process incorporates practical considerations, including Top-5 selection within the Top-50 $S\&P$ 500 constituents, explicit weight allocation, and transaction cost adjustment, thereby aligning the evaluation with real-world trading conditions. Empirical results on real-world data demonstrate that STN-TGAT consistently outperforms benchmark models from predictive accuracy and investment profitability measured by portfolio returns. These findings suggest that combining decision-aligned training with adaptive relational modeling provides a coherent and practically effective framework for data-driven portfolio construction.

中文摘要翻译: 本文探讨在现实投资环境下通过联合建模时间动态性和横截面依赖关系来解决股票排名与投资组合构建问题。我们提出了软阈值NMI先验Transformer图注意力网络（STN-TGAT），该网络融合了时序Transformer与图注意力网络，以捕获长期序列模式及动态股票间关系。基于NMI的先验图结合软阈值稀疏化机制，通过抑制噪声相关性同时保留信息连接来增强结构鲁棒性。投资组合构建过程纳入了实际考虑因素，包括在标普500前50成分股中选取前5只、显式权重分配以及交易成本调整，从而使评估更贴合真实交易环境。真实数据上的实验结果表明，STN-TGAT在预测准确性和以投资组合回报衡量的投资盈利能力方面持续优于基准模型。这些发现表明，将决策对齐训练与自适应关系建模相结合，为数据驱动的投资组合构建提供了一致且实际有效的框架。

</details>


### [83] [Building Fast, Evaluating Slow: Pipeline Choices Dominate Autointerpretability Score Variance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19386)
*Sinie van der Ben,Neele Roch,Anna Hedström,Mennatallah El-Assady*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地揭示了稀疏自编码器（SAE）可解释性评估中的自动可解释性评分存在严重的方法论方差问题，导致跨论文比较可能反映的是评估流水线差异而非架构差异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的SAE可解释性跨论文比较依赖自动可解释性评分，但这种比较有效的前提是评分反映特征的稳定属性而非评估流水线的混淆因素。本文旨在检验这一前提是否成立。

Method: 通过四项指标（simulation、detection、fuzzing、purity）、两个模型（Pythia-160M、Apertus-8B）以及四个方法学变化维度进行系统性实验，分析方法论方差与架构方差的相对大小。

Result: R1) 方法论方差在所有指标和模型上均超过架构方差；R2) 各指标稳定性不同，detection最稳定，fuzzing在所有条件下均不可靠；R3) top-k特征排序在语料库和采样条件下不一致，稳定的均值掩盖了逐特征的不稳定性。

Conclusion: 基于自动可解释性评分的跨论文比较可能反映的是流水线差异而非架构差异，这对SAE实用性的持续争论具有重要意义。不可靠的评估会阻碍可解释性研究进展。本文还贡献了方差分解方法、稳定性检查工具和最低报告清单。

Original Abstract: Cross-paper comparison of sparse autoencoder (SAE) interpretability often relies on autointerpretability scores. In this evaluation pipeline, a language model (LM) explains each feature, and another LM scores the explanation. For these comparisons to be meaningful, scores must reflect stable properties of the features rather than confounding aspects of the evaluation pipeline. Through systematic experiments across four metrics (simulation, detection, fuzzing, purity), two models (Pythia-160M, Apertus-8B), and four axes of methodological variation, we show that this assumption does not hold. Specifically, we find that R1) methodological variance collectively exceeds architectural variance across all metrics and tested models; R2) each metric exhibits a distinct instability profile, with detection being the most stable and fuzzing unreliable across all conditions; R3) top-k feature rankings do not stay consistent across corpus and draw conditions, masking per-feature instability behind stable mean scores; a failure that cannot be detected by monitoring explanation similarity alone. These findings suggest that cross-paper comparisons based on autointerpretability scores may reflect pipeline differences rather than architectural differences, with implications for the ongoing debate on SAE utility. More broadly, unreliable evaluation slows progress in interpretability research at a time when reliable tools for understanding AI systems are needed. To support evaluation, we contribute a variance decomposition approach, a Stability Check, and a Minimum Reporting Checklist.

中文摘要翻译: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）可解释性的跨论文比较通常依赖自动可解释性评分。在该评估流程中，一个语言模型（LM）解释每个特征，另一个LM对解释进行评分。要使这些比较具有意义，评分必须反映特征的稳定属性，而非评估流程中的混淆因素。通过在四项指标（模拟、检测、模糊测试、纯度）、两个模型（Pythia-160M、Apertus-8B）以及四个方法学变化维度上进行系统性实验，我们表明这一假设并不成立。具体而言，我们发现：R1）在所有指标和测试模型上，方法论方差总体超过架构方差；R2）每个指标具有不同的不稳定特征，其中检测指标最为稳定，而模糊测试在所有条件下均不可靠；R3）top-k特征排序在语料库和采样条件下不一致，稳定的均值掩盖了逐特征的不稳定性——这种失败无法通过仅监控解释相似性来检测。这些发现表明，基于自动可解释性评分的跨论文比较可能反映的是流水线差异而非架构差异，这对有关SAE实用性的持续争论具有重要意义。更广泛而言，在需要可靠工具来理解AI系统的关键时期，不可靠的评估会阻碍可解释性研究的进展。为支持评估，我们贡献了方差分解方法、稳定性检查工具以及最低报告清单。

</details>


### [84] [Scale-Aware Learning of Chaotic Dynamics on Unstructured Meshes via Binned Spectral Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19387)
*Kanad Sen,Romit Maulik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图拉普拉斯频带的谱损失函数，用于非结构化网格上非线性动力系统代理模型的长期预测，通过Chebyshev多项式滤波器和多级图架构（GLEAM）实现了可扩展的频带功率匹配，显著提升了湍流预测的长期保真度和统计不变性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高维非线性混沌动力系统的代理建模需要保留物理场的尺度相关结构，但现有基于傅里叶频带的谱损失函数仅适用于结构化网格；非结构化网格上缺乏规范傅里叶基，导致无法直接应用频带谱功率监督，亟需一种适用于非结构化网格的谱损失函数来提升代理模型的长程预测质量。

Method: 将傅里叶频带替换为图拉普拉斯频带，提出图拉普拉斯频带谱功率损失；通过Chebyshev多项式图滤波器近似精确频带投影器，避免显式特征分解；并提出多级图架构下的GLEAM（Graph Laplacian Energy Alignment for Meshes）方法，在图层级间施加尺度感知的保留子空间正则化。

Result: 在非结构化网格湍流预测任务上，所提谱损失函数相比确定性基线方法显著提升了长程滚动预测的保真度，并更好地保持了统计不变量。

Conclusion: 基于图拉普拉斯频带的谱损失函数能有效推广到非结构化网格代理建模，Chebyshev和多级近似方法使得该框架可扩展，为复杂几何上的非线性动力系统预测提供了新的正则化手段。

Original Abstract: Surrogate modeling for high-dimensional nonlinear dynamical systems that exhibit chaos requires mechanisms that preserve not only pointwise accuracy but also the scale-dependent structure of physical fields. Bandwise spectral power losses, such as the binned spectral loss function, provide such supervision on structured grids, where Fourier modes define a standard frequency decomposition. On irregular meshes, however, no canonical Fourier basis exists, and spectral representations must be constructed from graph operators induced by mesh connectivity and geometry. In this study, we extend the binned spectral power loss for application to unstructured-mesh surrogate modeling of nonlinear dynamical systems. This is obtained by replacing Fourier bands with graph-Laplacian frequency bands, and we provide scalable Chebyshev and multilevel approximations for improving long-horizon rollout fidelity. In its full-spectrum form, our approach uses graph Laplacian eigenspaces to provide a graph analogue of Fourier band-power matching, but incurs the high cost of spectral decomposition. As a scalable approximation, we replace exact band projectors with sparse Chebyshev polynomial graph filters, avoiding explicit eigendecomposition. When utilizing multilevel graph architectures, we introduce Graph Laplacian Energy Alignment for Meshes (GLEAM), which applies retained-subspace scale-aware supervision across graph hierarchies so that coarse and fine representations are regularized during autoregressive rollout. Our results show that the proposed spectral losses improve long-horizon rollout fidelity and preserve statistical invariants for the forecasting of turbulent flows on unstructured meshes, compared to deterministic baselines.
