To address this limitation, we propose a Neural-Collapse-Inspired Prioritization (NCIP) framework that replaces absolute confidence with cross-checkpoint prediction variability in the terminal training regime, where model geometry becomes highly structured. NCIP introduces two key components. First, it selects an NC-guided representative subset of training checkpoints using an equiangularity score of classifier weights, quantified as the standard deviation of pairwise cosine similarities among class weight vectors. Second, it prioritizes test inputs by their prediction variability across the selected checkpoints, surfacing boundary-adjacent and failure-prone samples that are unstable under checkpoint-induced decision boundary shifts. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets and architectures show that NCIP achieves strong performance in early fault discovery compared with competitive baselines, with 1.5 to 16.6 percent RAUC-ALL gains and 4.9 to 20.6 percent RAUC-500 gains under the same testing budget. NCIP further attains the best average performance across all dataset-model pairs.

中文摘要翻译: 随着深度神经网络（DNN）在安全关键领域的广泛部署，在有限测试预算下降低模型验证成本变得越来越重要。现有的测试用例优先级排序技术通常依赖于从输出概率导出的单一检查点置信度信号。然而，DNN可能高置信度地犯错，并且预测类与竞争类之间的置信度差距通常很小，这削弱了早期故障发现能力。为解决这一限制，我们提出了一种神经坍缩启发优先级排序（NCIP）框架，该框架在终端训练阶段（此时模型几何结构变得高度结构化）用跨检查点预测变异性替代绝对置信度。NCIP引入两个关键组件。首先，它使用分类器权重的等角性评分（量化为类权重向量间成对余弦相似度的标准差）来选择NC引导的训练检查点代表性子集。其次，它根据测试输入在所选检查点上的预测变异性对其进行优先级排序，从而暴露那些在检查点导致的决策边界偏移下不稳定的边界邻近且易失效的样本。跨多个数据集和架构的大量实验表明，在相同测试预算下，NCIP相比竞争基线在早期故障发现方面取得了强劲性能，RAUC-ALL增益为1.5%至16.6%，RAUC-500增益为4.9%至20.6%。NCIP还在所有数据集-模型组合上取得了最佳平均性能。

</details>


### [142] [Evaluating and Mitigating Gender Bias in Pre-trained Embeddings for ML-based Recruitment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20073)
*Farnaz Faramarzi Lighvan,Lynn Houthuys*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了九种预训练嵌入模型在合成简历数据集上的性别偏差，发现显式性别擦除不能完全消除性别泄露，而多任务对抗学习虽能提升公平性，但只能作为文本级去偏的补充策略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基于AI的简历筛选系统可能放大社会偏见，尤其在非结构化简历文本中，预训练语言模型的嵌入即使在移除显式性别指标后仍可能推断出敏感属性。本文旨在系统评估不同嵌入模型的性别泄露程度，并探索有效的去偏策略。

Method: 在合成FairCVdb数据集上评估九种预训练嵌入模型，分析其在原始和性别擦除简历上的申请评分信息性和性别泄露情况；采用基于梯度反转的多任务对抗学习框架在抑制性别信息的同时预测申请者适配度；使用基于帕累托前沿的多目标模型选择以平衡预测效用与公平性。

Result: 显式性别擦除虽能大幅减少但无法完全消除性别泄露；对抗学习主要改善了原始简历上的公平性，但仅能作为文本级去偏的补充策略，而非替代方案。

Conclusion: 仅靠模型层面的对抗学习不足以彻底消除简历筛选中的性别偏差，必须结合文本级别的显式去偏处理，两者互补才能更有效地实现公平性。

Original Abstract: AI-based recruitment systems that rely on machine learning models trained on historical CV data, risk perpetuating and amplifying social biases. A key challenge arises in unstructured CV text, where pre-trained language model embeddings may infer sensitive attributes such as gender even after explicit indicators are removed. In this paper, we evaluate nine pre-trained embedding models on the synthetic FairCVdb dataset, analyzing the informativeness of their embeddings for applicant scoring and their susceptibility to gender leakage, on both original and gender-scrubbed biographies. We further use a multi-task adversarial learning framework with gradient reversal to predict applicant suitability while suppressing gender information from learned representations. Finally, we use a multi-objective Pareto-front-based model selection to balance predictive utility and fairness. Our experimental results show that explicit gender scrubbing substantially reduces but does not eliminate gender leakage, while adversarial learning improves fairness mainly on original biographies and acts as a complementary strategy rather than a substitute for text-level debiasing.

中文摘要翻译: 基于AI的招聘系统依赖在历史简历数据上训练的机器学习模型，存在延续和放大社会偏见的风险。一个关键挑战出现在非结构化简历文本中：即使移除了显式性别指标，预训练语言模型的嵌入仍可能推断出性别等敏感属性。本文在合成FairCVdb数据集上评估了九种预训练嵌入模型，分析了它们的嵌入对申请者评分的信息性以及在原始和性别擦除简历上的性别泄露情况。我们进一步采用基于梯度反转的多任务对抗学习框架，在抑制学习表示中性别信息的同时预测申请者适配度。最后，我们使用基于帕累托前沿的多目标模型选择来平衡预测效用与公平性。实验结果表明，显式性别擦除虽能大幅减少但无法完全消除性别泄露，而对抗学习主要改善了原始简历上的公平性，它作为文本级去偏的补充策略而非替代方案。

</details>


### [143] [Co-Evolving LLM Evaluators and Policies via DynamicRubric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20083)
*Beining Wang,Weihang Su,Hongtao Tian,Hao Kong,Tao Yang,Ting Yao,Qingyi Pan,Yueyue Wu,Qingyao Ai,Min Zhang,Yiqun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出DynamicRubric框架，通过响应集条件化的评估器-策略协同进化，在策略优化中保留相对评分差距信号，提升大语言模型的监督效果，并在微信搜索AI问答场景中全量部署验证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随着策略改进，采样响应质量趋近，导致评估器评分差距坍缩，产生弱或误导性的策略监督信号，形成策略优化的瓶颈。需从理论层面厘清评分差距为何重要，并设计能保留有效信号的方法。

Method: 通过概率分配视角理论证明评估器评分差距恰好是策略更新的方向性增益；提出DynamicRubric框架，为每个候选响应集生成加权的二元评分项，并聚合为响应级分数，实现评估器与策略的协同进化。

Result: 在8B骨干模型上，DynamicRubric优于使用70B奖励模型或235B静态评分生成器的基线；在可验证推理和编码任务上表现提升；全量部署于微信搜索AI问答场景，日均服务数千万请求，关键在线指标改善。

Conclusion: 评估器引导的后训练应遵循一条原则：评估器应随着其所监督的策略一同进化。

Original Abstract: Post-training with evaluator feedback on policy-induced samples serves as a major mechanism for improving large language models. As policies improve, these sampled responses become close in quality. These close candidates create a bottleneck for policy optimization: collapsed relative evaluator score gaps yield weak or misleading policy supervision. We theoretically characterize why these gaps matter through a probability allocation view, showing that the directional gain of shifting probability mass from one response to another is exactly the evaluator score gap between them. This identifies relative score gaps as the policy optimization signals that guide updates. Motivated by this view, we propose DynamicRubric, a response-set-conditioned evaluator--policy co-evolution framework that generates weighted binary rubric items for each candidate set and aggregates the resulting judgments into response-level scores. In our experiments with 8B backbones, DynamicRubric improves evaluator performance and provides stronger policy supervision than baselines using a 70B reward model or a 235B static rubric generator. DynamicRubric-optimized policies also show gains on verifiable reasoning and coding tasks. A DynamicRubric-optimized model is fully deployed in WeChat Search's AI answering scenario, where it serves all online traffic across tens of millions of requests per day and improves key online metrics. These results suggest a principle for evaluator-guided post-training: evaluators should evolve with the policies they supervise.

中文摘要翻译: 在策略诱导的样本上使用评估器反馈进行后训练，是改进大语言模型的主要机制。随着策略的改进，这些采样响应的质量逐渐趋近。这些接近的候选响应给策略优化带来了瓶颈：相对评估器评分差距的坍缩会产生微弱或误导性的策略监督信号。我们通过概率分配的视角从理论上阐明了这些差距为何重要，表明将概率质量从一个响应转移到另一个响应的方向性增益恰好等于它们之间的评估器评分差距。这揭示了相对评分差距正是指导策略更新的策略优化信号。受此观点启发，我们提出了DynamicRubric——一种基于响应集条件的评估器-策略协同进化框架，该框架为每个候选集生成加权的二元评分项，并将所得判断聚合为响应级分数。在使用8B骨干网络的实验中，DynamicRubric提升了评估器性能，并提供了比使用70B奖励模型或235B静态评分生成器的基线更强的策略监督。经DynamicRubric优化的策略在可验证推理和编码任务上也表现出提升。一个经DynamicRubric优化的模型已全面部署于微信搜索的AI问答场景中，每天为数千万次请求的全部在线流量提供服务，并改善了关键在线指标。这些结果表明了评估器引导后训练的一个原则：评估器应随着其所监督的策略一同进化。

</details>


### [144] [Autonomous Collaborative Learning Among an Ensemble of Tsetlin Machines with Consensus-Based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20124)
*Yehuda Rudin,Osnat Keren,Michal Yemini,Alexander Fish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于共识推理的垂直特征划分场景下Tsetlin机器去中心化协同学习范式，各智能体维护私有TM模型，通过共识融合实现分布式协作，在网格和连通图拓扑上取得与集中式模型相当的分类精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文献中分布式和去中心化的Tsetlin Machine学习研究较少，需要进一步探索去中心化协作学习范式。

Method: 提出一种垂直特征划分设置下的去中心化协作学习范式，每个智能体维护自己的私有Tsetlin Machine模型，不交换原始数据；推理阶段通过共识机制融合各智能体模型的预测结果，形成全局共识。

Result: 在二维网格和连通图网络拓扑上的实验表明，所提去中心化方法的分类精度与集中式模型相当。

Conclusion: 所提去中心化范式能够支持异构TM智能体协作，适用于多模态感知等场景，在分类性能上可媲美集中式模型。

Original Abstract: Tsetlin Machine (TM) is a rule-based machine-learning algorithm comprising collectives of two-action Tsetlin Automata (TAs) that cooperatively form conjunctive logical clauses from Boolean inputs through stochastic feedback. Although few recent studies have examined TM Federated Learning, the broader area of distributed and decentralized TM learning has not received much attention in the existing literature and warrants further exploration. In this work, we propose a paradigm for decentralized collaborative learning under a vertical feature-partitioning setting among an ensemble of Tsetlin Machines using consensus-based inference. Within this decentralized paradigm, each agent maintains its own private TM model, and there is no exchange of raw data among agents. Inference combines individual agents model predictions into a global consensus. The paradigm accommodates heterogeneous TM-based agents with differing data acquisition means, local data distributions, or computational resources, thereby facilitating the integration and fusion of information in settings such as multi-modal sensing environments. Experiments conducted using two-dimensional grid and connected graph network topologies demonstrate that the classification accuracies achieved are comparable to those of centralized models.

中文摘要翻译: Tsetlin Machine (TM) 是一种基于规则的机器学习算法，由两动作Tsetlin自动机集体组成，通过随机反馈从布尔输入中协作形成合取逻辑子句。尽管近期有少量研究探讨了TM联邦学习，但分布式和去中心化TM学习这一更广泛的领域在现有文献中尚未得到充分关注，值得进一步探索。本文提出了一种在垂直特征划分设置下，基于共识推理的Tsetlin Machine集成去中心化协作学习范式。在该去中心化范式中，每个智能体维护其自身的私有TM模型，智能体之间不交换原始数据。推理阶段将各智能体模型的预测结果融合为全局共识。该范式能够容纳具有不同数据采集手段、本地数据分布或计算资源的异构TM智能体，从而促进多模态感知环境等场景中的信息集成与融合。在二维网格和连通图网络拓扑上开展的实验表明，所提方法取得的分类精度与集中式模型相当。

</details>


### [145] [CURED: Creating, Understanding, and Repairing Errors Demonstrator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20140)
*Nicholas Chandler,Sebastian Jäger,Philipp Jung,Felix Bießmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于机器学习的表格数据错误检测与清洗的统一演示系统，允许用户上传表格数据、注入真实数据依赖错误并使用现代ML方法进行清洗和错误分析。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 表格数据中的错误检测与清洗是数据密集型应用的前提，现有研究虽然展示了统计学习算法的潜力，但缺乏一个能够连接理论进展与实践洞察的统一演示工具。

Method: 构建了一个Web应用程序，允许用户上传表格数据、用真实数据依赖错误扰动数据，并应用现代机器学习方法进行错误清洗和错误机制理解。

Result: 该演示系统成功弥合了表格数据错误模型与清洗算法在理论进展与直观实践洞察之间的差距。

Conclusion: 该演示工具有效连接了基于ML的数据清洗理论与实际应用，帮助用户更好地理解和清洗表格数据中的错误。

Original Abstract: Detecting and cleaning errors in tabular data is a prerequisite for data intense software applications. Recent research at the intersection of Machine Learning (ML) and Database Management Systems (DBMS) highlights the potential of statistical learning algorithms for error detection and cleaning. This paper combines our recent work on ML-based data cleaning and error models in a unified demonstrator. The web application allows users to upload tabular data, perturb the data with realistic data dependent errors and use modern ML methods to clean and understand error mechanisms in data. Our demonstrator helps to bridge the gap between theoretical advancements and intuitive practical insights in the context of error models and data cleaning algorithms for tabular data. The demonstrator is available at https://cured.demo.calgo-lab.de/

中文摘要翻译: 检测和清理表格数据中的错误是数据密集型软件应用程序的先决条件。最近在机器学习（ML）与数据库管理系统（DBMS）交叉领域的研究凸显了统计学习算法在错误检测和清理方面的潜力。本文在我们近期基于ML的数据清洗和错误模型相关工作基础上，构建了一个统一的演示系统。该Web应用程序允许用户上传表格数据，使用真实的数据依赖错误对数据进行扰动，并利用现代ML方法进行数据清洗和理解数据中的错误机制。我们的演示系统有助于弥合表格数据错误模型和数据清洗算法的理论进展与直观实践洞察之间的差距。该演示系统可通过 https://cured.demo.calgo-lab.de/ 访问。

</details>


### [146] [Active Inference as a Convex Markov Decision Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20152)
*Nikola Milosevic,Nicolás Hinrichs,Nico Scherf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明主动推理（AIF）中的期望自由能（EFE）最小化可形式化为凸马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），其中 pragmatic 项等价于潜在MDP中的奖励最大化，而 epistemic 项引入非线性成分。通过镜像下降算法局部线性化目标，使EFE最小化与actor-critic方法和动态规划兼容，并将世界模型学习与策略优化耦合，建立了主动推理与现代强化学习理论之间的联系。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 将主动推理（AIF）框架中的期望自由能（EFE）最小化纳入策略优化视角，阐明其与标准强化学习的关系，并为EFE最小化提供收敛性分析和策略改进保证的理论基础。

Method: 将EFE最小化形式化为凸马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），分析有限时域、折扣和平均奖励三种EFE公式，推导出镜像下降（MD）算法，该算法在当前状态边际附近局部线性化目标，产生与actor-critic方法和动态规划兼容的策略依赖奖励。

Result: 1) 证明EFE最小化可形式化为凸MDP；2) 揭示epistemic价值作为策略依赖（表现型）奖励；3) 推导出兼容actor-critic和动态规划的镜像下降算法；4) 论证世界模型学习与策略优化耦合使主动推理具有表现型强化学习结构。

Conclusion: 主动推理可以通过凸MDP和镜像下降算法与现代强化学习和优化理论对接，为主动推理提供收敛性分析和策略改进保证的理论路径。

Original Abstract: Active Inference (AIF) frames adaptive behavior as the minimization of expected free energy (EFE), combining epistemic and pragmatic objectives within a single variational principle. We frame AIF as policy optimization and show that, for closed-loop control policies, EFE minimization can be formulated as a convex Markov decision process (MDP). In this formulation, the pragmatic terms are linear in the predictive state marginals and therefore equivalent to reward maximization in a latent MDP, while the epistemic value introduces a nonlinear component that distinguishes EFE minimization from standard reinforcement learning. This perspective further reveals the epistemic drive of active inference as a policy-dependent (performative) reward. We analyze finite-horizon, discounted, and average-reward formulations of EFE and derive a mirror descent (MD) algorithm that locally linearizes the objective around the current state marginals, yielding a policy-dependent reward that is compatible with actor-critic methods and dynamic programming. Finally, we argue that coupling world-model learning with policy optimization gives active inference the structure of performative reinforcement learning, providing a route toward grounding active inference within modern reinforcement learning and optimization theory, including convergence analysis and principled policy improvement guarantees.

中文摘要翻译: 主动推理（Active Inference, AIF）将适应性行为框架化为期望自由能（Expected Free Energy, EFE）的最小化，在单一变分原理下结合了认知目标与实用目标。本文将主动推理置于策略优化框架下，并证明对于闭环控制策略，EFE最小化可以形式化为一个凸马尔可夫决策过程（Markov Decision Process, MDP）。在该形式化中，实用项在预测状态边际上是线性的，因此等价于潜在MDP中的奖励最大化，而认知价值引入了一个非线性分量，将EFE最小化与标准强化学习区分开来。该视角进一步揭示了主动推理的认知驱动力是一种策略依赖（表现型）的奖励。本文分析了有限时域、折扣和平均奖励三种EFE公式，并推导出一种镜像下降（Mirror Descent, MD）算法，该算法在当前状态边际附近局部线性化目标，产生一种与actor-critic方法和动态规划兼容的策略依赖奖励。最后，本文论证了将世界模型学习与策略优化耦合使得主动推理具备表现型强化学习（Performative Reinforcement Learning）的结构，为在现代强化学习和优化理论（包括收敛性分析和原则性策略改进保证）中夯实主动推理的基础提供了一条路径。

</details>


### [147] [Local Stability and Gaussian Smoothing of Quantized Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20153)
*Sergey Salishev,Anton Makarov,Oleg Granichin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了高斯平均化作为量化神经模型的平滑替代方法，通过有界局部振荡推导了|f-g|的局部维度相关界，将高斯平滑与不连续网络的稳定性分析联系起来，并给出了ReLU和符号激活函数的高斯平均闭式解，在高维二元感知器上展示了机制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 为量化神经网络（具有不连续激活函数）提供一种平滑的替代方法，从而支持稳定性分析和基于梯度的训练。

Method: 利用高斯平均化（Gaussian averaging）对量化神经模型进行平滑近似；在有界局部振荡假设下推导|f-g|的维度相关边界；计算ReLU和符号激活函数的高斯平均闭式解；在高维二元感知器上通过显式量化噪声替代进行层预激活聚合。

Result: 推导了局部维度相关的误差界，得到了ReLU和符号激活函数的高斯平均闭式解，并展示了高斯包络（Gaussian envelope）在推理侧平滑和训练侧平滑替代梯度中的双重作用。

Conclusion: 高斯平均化为量化神经模型提供了一种有效的平滑替代方案，能够桥接不连续网络稳定性分析与实际训练/推理需求。

Original Abstract: We study Gaussian averaging as a smooth surrogate for quantized neural models. Under bounded local oscillation, we derive a local dimension-dependent bound on |f-g|, linking Gaussian smoothing to the stability analysis of discontinuous networks. We compute closed-form Gaussian averages of the rectified linear unit (ReLU) and sign activation functions, and illustrate the mechanism on a high-dimensional binary perceptron, where layer-preactivation aggregation under an explicit quantization-noise surrogate yields the Gaussian envelope used in inference-side smoothing and training-side smooth surrogate gradients.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究高斯平均化作为量化神经模型的平滑替代方法。在有界局部振荡条件下，我们推导了关于|f-g|的局部维度相关界，将高斯平滑与不连续网络的稳定性分析联系起来。我们计算了整流线性单元（ReLU）和符号激活函数的高斯平均闭式解，并在高维二元感知器上阐述了该机制：在显式量化噪声替代下的层预激活聚合产生了推理侧平滑和训练侧平滑替代梯度中所使用的高斯包络。

</details>


### [148] [Self-organizing Architecture of Receptron Units: a Hardware-Aware Framework for Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20162)
*Stefano Radice,Ludovico Casaccia,Riccaro Emanuele Beccalli,Bruno Paroli,Paolo Milani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Receptron模型的类神经形态分类器，可在资源受限的物联网边缘MCU上实现非线性决策边界和在线自适应，性能接近标准机器学习方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 物联网边缘设备的MCU计算和内存资源极度有限，传统深度学习方法难以部署；需要一种轻量级、可解释且支持在线自适应的分类模型。

Method: 基于Receptron模型设计类神经形态分类器，采用单单元架构实现非线性可分离决策边界，无需多层网络，可直接部署在中端MCU上并支持持续在线自适应。

Result: 在基础数据集基准测试上的交叉验证准确率与标准机器学习方法基线相当。

Conclusion: Receptron可作为资源受限的类神经形态边缘系统中一种可行且可解释的替代方案，特别适用于动态非平稳环境。

Original Abstract: The growing demand for intelligent processing at the edge of IoT networks is constrained by the severe computational and memory limitations of microcontroller units, which render impractical conventional deep learning approaches. We propose a neuromorphicinspired classifier based on the Receptron model, a single-unit architecture capable of implementing non-linearly separable decision boundaries, without resorting to multi-layer networks. The model is designed for direct deployment on mid-range MCUs, while supporting continuous on-device adaptation. Experimental evaluation on basic dataset benchmarks yields cross-validated accuracies compatible with standard machine learning method baselines. These results position the Receptron as a viable and interpretable alternative for resource-constrained neuromorphic edge systems operating in dynamic, non-stationary environments.

中文摘要翻译: 物联网边缘智能处理的需求日益增长，但微控制器单元严重的计算和内存限制使传统深度学习方法难以实际部署。我们提出了一种基于Receptron模型的类神经形态分类器，该模型采用单单元架构，无需多层网络即可实现非线性可分离决策边界。该模型设计用于直接部署在中端MCU上，并支持连续的在设备自适应。在基础数据集基准测试上的实验评估显示，其交叉验证准确率与标准机器学习方法基线相当。这些结果表明，Receptron可作为在动态、非平稳环境中运行的资源受限类神经形态边缘系统中一种可行且可解释的替代方案。

</details>


### [149] [Instance Hardness-Based Relevance for Imbalanced Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20173)
*Vitor M. Leitao,Juscimara G. Avelino,George D. C. Cavalcanti,Rafael M. O. Cruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对传统相关性函数在双峰分布等复杂场景下难以准确识别罕见实例的问题，提出了一种基于实例硬度的相关性函数（InHaR），通过结合学习难度与目标分布来推断稀有性，在双峰分布下显著提升了重采样策略的预测性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统回归任务中处理不平衡问题依赖相关性函数，但该方法在双峰分布等复杂场景下仅根据目标值分配固定相关值，无法区分真正罕见的实例与正常实例，导致稀有性识别不准确。

Method: 提出实例硬度相关性函数（InHaR），该函数不仅考虑目标变量的分布，还引入实例对学习算法的难度（学习难度），从而综合推断实例的稀有性。

Result: 实验表明，InHaR在双峰分布下能正确识别稀有区域；当用于引导随机过采样（RO）和高斯噪声（GN）等重采样策略时，相比传统基于相关性的方法，预测性能有显著提升。

Conclusion: InHaR有效克服了传统相关性函数在复杂分布场景下的局限性，为不平衡回归中的稀有实例识别提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Imbalanced regression problems arise when the target variable has an asymmetric distribution, resulting in underrepresented value ranges in the dataset. Traditional approaches for identifying rare instances rely on a relevance function that assigns higher importance to specific regions of the target distribution. However, the effectiveness of imbalance-aware learning methods depends strongly on how relevance is defined. In more complex scenarios, such as bimodal distributions, traditional relevance functions struggle to capture rarity, as they assign fixed relevance values based solely on target values, thereby compromising the distinction between truly rare and normal instances. To address these limitations, this study proposes an Instance Hardness-based relevance function (InHaR) for identifying rare instances in regression problems. Unlike traditional relevance functions, the proposed approach incorporates learning difficulty, allowing rarity to be inferred not only from the target distribution but also from the difficulty of instances for the learning algorithm. This property is particularly important in bimodal scenarios, where rarity cannot be accurately inferred from target values alone. Experimental results demonstrate that the InHaR correctly identifies rare regions under bimodal distributions and, when used to guide resampling strategies such as Random Oversampling (RO) and Gaussian Noise (GN), leads to significant improvements in predictive performance compared to traditional relevance-based approaches. The code, dataset, and further details about the proposed method are publicly available at https://github.com/VitorLeitao/instance-hardness-Imbalanced-regression.

中文摘要翻译: 当目标变量呈现不对称分布时，会出现不平衡回归问题，导致数据集中某些取值范围的实例代表性不足。传统的稀有实例识别方法依赖于相关性函数，该函数为目标分布的特定区域赋予更高的重要性。然而，不平衡感知学习方法的有效性在很大程度上取决于相关性的定义方式。在更复杂的场景中，例如双峰分布，传统的相关性函数难以捕捉稀有性，因为它们仅根据目标值分配固定的相关值，从而混淆了真正稀有实例与正常实例之间的区别。为解决这些局限性，本研究提出了一种基于实例硬度的相关性函数（InHaR），用于识别回归问题中的稀有实例。与传统相关性函数不同，所提出的方法引入了学习难度，使得稀有性不仅可以从目标分布中推断，还可以从实例对学习算法的难度中推断。这一特性在双峰分布场景中尤为重要，因为仅凭目标值无法准确推断稀有性。实验结果表明，InHaR能够正确识别双峰分布下的稀有区域，并且当用于引导随机过采样（RO）和高斯噪声（GN）等重采样策略时，与传统的基于相关性的方法相比，能够显著提升预测性能。本研究的代码、数据集及所提方法的更多细节已在https://github.com/VitorLeitao/instance-hardness-Imbalanced-regression上公开。

</details>


### [150] [On Optimization Complexity of Second-Order Certified Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20192)
*Nikita Doikov,Anastasia Koloskova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从优化视角研究机器遗忘问题的算法复杂度，利用均匀凸正则化器推导初始模型与遗忘模型间距离的新上界，并提出一种基于各向异性高斯机制的二阶遗忘算法，在线性模型下实现了认证遗忘的快速收敛率，证明了二阶信息相比一阶方法的优势。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有机器遗忘算法缺乏对认证遗忘与优化精度之间关系的理论理解，且一阶遗忘方法收敛较慢，亟需从优化角度分析遗忘问题的算法复杂度并开发更高效的方法。

Method: 1) 利用均匀凸正则化器提出新的泛化误差替代量，推导初始模型与遗忘模型间的距离上界；2) 提出基于各向异性高斯机制的二阶遗忘算法；3) 针对拟自协方差损失下的线性模型证明认证遗忘的快速收敛率。

Result: 理论上证明了若遗忘数据能被遗忘模型良好预测，则对应优化问题简单；提出的二阶遗忘算法达到了全局收敛的最新水平，在逻辑回归和指数回归中相比一阶方法具有可证明的优势。

Conclusion: 二阶信息对机器遗忘有显著益处，所提方法在认证遗忘与优化精度之间实现了更好的平衡，为机器遗忘的优化理论基础提供了新见解。

Original Abstract: We study machine unlearning: the removal of memorized training data from a trained model. Specifically, we investigate the algorithmic complexity of certified unlearning from an optimization perspective. We formalize the goal of an unlearning algorithm as simultaneously achieving certified unlearning and optimization accuracy. Utilizing the notion of uniformly convex regularizers, we prove new bounds on the distance between initial and unlearned models using a novel substitute for generalization error. Thus we theoretically demonstrate that if the removed data is well-predicted by the unlearned model, the corresponding optimization problem is simple. Furthermore, we develop a new second-order unlearning algorithm with an anisotropic Gaussian mechanism and state-of-the-art global convergence. We prove fast rates for our method in achieving certified unlearning for linear models with quasi-self-concordant losses. As a direct application, our theory covers unlearning for logistic and exponential regressions and shows a provable benefit of utilizing second-order information compared to first-order unlearning methods.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究机器遗忘：即从已训练模型中移除被记忆的训练数据。具体而言，我们从优化角度探讨认证遗忘的算法复杂度。我们将遗忘算法的目标形式化定义为同时实现认证遗忘与优化精度。利用均匀凸正则化器的概念，我们通过一种新颖的泛化误差替代量，证明了初始模型与遗忘模型之间距离的新上界。因此，我们从理论上证明：若被移除的数据能被遗忘模型良好预测，则相应的优化问题将变得简单。此外，我们开发了一种新的基于各向异性高斯机制的二阶遗忘算法，实现了全局收敛的最新水平。我们证明了该方法在拟自协方差损失的线性模型下实现认证遗忘的快速收敛率。作为直接应用，我们的理论涵盖了逻辑回归和指数回归的遗忘问题，并证明了利用二阶信息相比一阶遗忘方法具有可验证的优势。

</details>


### [151] [OLEDLM: A Unified Language Model for OLED Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20194)
*Fukang Wen,Yuchong Tang,Jingyuan Li,Beichen Wang,Yixuan Jiang,Xiaoyi Jiang,Yaxuan Liu,Shunyu Wang,Zuoqiang Shi,Yi Zhu,Yanan Zhu,Pipi Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于因果语言模型的逆向分子设计框架，用于在巨大的化学空间中高效生成满足特定光电性质要求的OLED分子，通过多阶段策略（基础语言模型、属性预测器微调、强化学习、DFT验证）实现了高结构有效性和优化性质。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: OLED材料开发面临化学空间巨大、量子化学约束严格、标注数据稀缺等挑战，现有通用分子生成模型难以有效针对OLED这一特定领域进行训练，亟需一种专门面向OLED的逆向分子设计方法。

Method: 采用多阶段策略：(1) 基于LLaMA风格的Transformer架构构建OLED基础化学语言模型；(2) 在大规模OLED数据集上预训练BERT模型，微调属性预测器；(3) 利用属性预测器作为奖励信号，对微调后的模型进行强化学习以优化SMILES生成；(4) 通过DFT验证评估生成分子的有效性。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架能够高效导航OLED化学空间，生成具有高结构有效性和优化光电性质的新型候选分子，并通过DFT验证证实了生成分子的可靠性。

Conclusion: 本文首次成功将大规模语言模型适配到OLED领域，填补了通用分子生成与光电材料严格结构要求之间的空白，为OLED材料的逆向设计提供了高效可行的新范式。

Original Abstract: The development of organic light-emitting diode (OLED) materials faces the compounded challenges of an astronomically large chemical space, stringent quantum-chemical constraints, and a scarcity of labeled data. Although the question of OLED generation is important, few models have been trained effectively for this specific domain. We propose an inverse molecular design framework based on causal language models: given target optoelectronic properties (e.g., excitation energy, oscillator strength), our model directly generates OLED SMILES sequences satisfying the specified constraints. We employ a multi-stage strategy: first, we establish a foundational chemical language model using a LLaMA-style transformer architecture. To the best of our knowledge, this represents the first successful adaptation of LLMs specifically for the OLED domain, bridging the gap between generic molecular generation and the stringent structural requirements of optoelectronic materials. Second, we fine-tune property predictors based on a BERT model pre-trained on our large-scale OLED dataset. Then, we perform Reinforcement Learning on our fine-tuned model, leveraging our property predictor, for better SMILES generation. Finally, through DFT verification, we demonstrate that our framework can efficiently navigate the OLED chemical space, generating novel candidates with high structural validity and optimized optoelectronic properties.

中文摘要翻译: 有机发光二极管（OLED）材料的开发面临着化学空间极其庞大、量子化学约束严格以及标注数据稀缺等多重挑战。尽管OLED分子生成问题十分重要，但很少有模型能够针对这一特定领域进行有效训练。我们提出了一种基于因果语言模型的逆向分子设计框架：给定目标光电性质（例如激发能、振子强度），我们的模型直接生成满足指定约束条件的OLED SMILES序列。我们采用多阶段策略：首先，使用LLaMA风格的Transformer架构构建基础化学语言模型。据我们所知，这是首次成功将大规模语言模型适配到OLED领域，填补了通用分子生成与光电材料严格结构要求之间的空白。其次，基于在大规模OLED数据集上预训练的BERT模型，微调属性预测器。然后，利用属性预测器对微调后的模型进行强化学习，以优化SMILES生成。最后，通过DFT验证，我们证明了该框架能够高效导航OLED化学空间，生成具有高结构有效性和优化光电性质的新型候选分子。

</details>


### [152] [The Quadrilateral Loss: Additivity as a Measurable Behavior of Dense Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20201)
*Antonio Di Cecco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出四边形损失（quadrilateral loss），一种可微分的惩罚项，将加性模型中的可加性从架构约束转为可测量的行为指标，通过二阶混合差分量化特征交互。实验表明大多数交互可几乎无成本移除，适度惩罚能同时提升精度和可加性，并揭示了多种实现精确可加性路径的优劣与陷阱。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统加性模型通过架构约束（如禁止特征交互）实现可解释性，但这种方式过于刚性。作者希望将可加性视为一种可测量的行为属性而非硬性架构约束，从而更灵活地控制模型中的特征交互程度，并揭示不同实现路径的隐蔽失败模式。

Method: 提出四边形损失（quadrilateral loss），通过计算训练样本对交换单一坐标后的二阶混合差分来量化特征交互，该损失在分段线性网络中仍保持信息量，且期望值等价于干预Shapley-GAM的每坐标交互质量。该损失将可加性变为可调节的"旋钮"和在线可观测指标。

Result: 大多数学习到的交互可几乎无成本移除；在小型数据集上适度惩罚可同时改善精度和可加性；预正则化交互量几乎无法预测正则化后保留的交互；行为约束优于权重空间约束；不同数据规模下方法排名发生反转；不同路径收敛到的形状函数一致；发现三种静默失败模式。

Conclusion: 将可加性作为可测量行为而非架构约束来对待更为灵活有效；不同实现路径（结构掩码、行为惩罚、权重衰减、回拟、共享区模型、装袋提升树桩等）存在显著差异，在数据规模间排名反转；行为约束应先于结构约束；三种静默失败模式共享同一解剖学特征：将保证条件引入到静默使其前提失效的环境中。

Original Abstract: Additive models buy interpretability by forbidding feature interactions, a constraint that neural instantiations enforce architecturally. We introduce the quadrilateral loss, a differentiable penalty that treats additivity as a measurable behavior instead: a second-order mixed difference on pairs of training points swapping one coordinate, which vanishes if and only if the coordinate carries no interaction, remains informative for piecewise-linear networks, and equals in expectation the per-coordinate interaction mass of the interventional Shapley-GAM. The loss turns additivity into a dial - most learned interactions prove removable almost for free, and on small datasets a moderate penalty improves accuracy and additivity simultaneously - and into an online observable: its per-feature surrender curves show, across seeds and datasets, that pre-regularization interaction magnitude barely predicts what a regularized model retains, undermining post-hoc interaction rankings. Against this instrument we compare routes to exact additivity, spanning structural masks, behavioral penalties (optionally crystallized into exact structure), weight decay, backfitting, the shared-section model, and bagged boosted stumps: constraining behavior before structure dominates weight-space constraints, rankings reverse between data regimes, and converging routes agree on the shape functions themselves. Three silent failure modes we document share one anatomy: guarantees imported into settings that quietly void their preconditions.

中文摘要翻译: 加性模型通过禁止特征交互来实现可解释性，神经网络的实例化则通过架构强制这一约束。我们引入了四边形损失，这是一种可微的惩罚项，将可加性视为一种可测量的行为：即对训练样本对交换单一坐标后的二阶混合差分，该差值为零当且仅当该坐标不携带交互信息，对分段线性网络仍保持有效性，且在期望上等价于干预Shapley-GAM的每坐标交互质量。该损失将可加性变成一个可调节的旋钮——大多数学习到的交互几乎可以无成本地移除，在小型数据集上适度的惩罚能同时提升精度和可加性——并且成为一个在线可观测指标：其逐特征投降曲线显示，跨不同种子和数据集，预正则化交互量几乎无法预测正则化模型所保留的交互，从而削弱了事后交互排名的可靠性。借助这一工具，我们比较了实现精确可加性的多种路径，涵盖结构掩码、行为惩罚（可选择固化为精确结构）、权重衰减、回拟、共享区模型以及装袋提升树桩：在结构之前约束行为优于权值空间约束，排名在不同数据规模之间发生反转，且收敛路径在形状函数本身达成一致。我们记录了三类静默失败模式，它们共享同一解剖特征：将保证条件引入到静默使其前提条件失效的环境中。

</details>


### [153] [ELSAA: Efficient Low-Rank and Sparse Attention Approximation for Training Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20214)
*Mahdi Heidari,Mohammad Mahdi Rahimi,Jaekyun Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ELSAA提出了一种低秩与稀疏混合的注意力近似方法，在不构造完整二次型注意力分数矩阵的情况下，通过稀疏分支捕捉高相似度交互、低秩分支汇总全局信息，并引入分母感知融合项解决两分支归一化尺度差异问题，以支持更长序列的Transformer训练。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Transformer中N×N的二次型注意力分数矩阵是将其扩展到更长输入长度的核心障碍。现有方法要么施加稀疏性（每个查询只关注少量键），要么使用低秩/核方法压缩全局交互，但两者各有局限。ELSAA旨在同时利用稀疏和低秩的优势，以更高效的方式近似注意力算子本身，从而在不牺牲模型表达能力的前提下支持更长上下文。

Method: ELSAA不对Transformer的投影或输出矩阵进行稀疏/低秩分解，而是在密集投影得到Q、K、V之后，直接近似注意力分数算子。方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 稀疏分支，捕获选定的高相似度交互；(2) 低秩分支，汇总弥散的全局交互；(3) 分母感知融合项，根据稀疏分支相对于低秩分支的估计注意力质量，对稀疏分支进行缩放，解决两分支因支持集差异导致的归一化分母质量不同的问题。

Result: 本文未在摘要中明确给出具体实验结果，但指出该方法构建了一个实用的框架，可以在不实例化完整二次型分数矩阵的情况下生成低秩与稀疏混合的注意力输出，旨在实现更长上下文的训练，同时保留尖锐的token级交互和广泛的上下文混合。

Conclusion: ELSAA通过引入分母感知融合的低秩-稀疏混合注意力近似框架，为扩展Transformer处理更长序列提供了一种新的实用方案，能够在不构造完整二次型注意力矩阵的情况下同时保留局部精细交互与全局上下文信息。

Original Abstract: The quadratic $N\times N$ attention score matrix remains a central obstacle to extending Transformers to longer input lengths. Existing efficient attention methods usually reduce this bottleneck by either imposing sparsity, so that each query attends to only a small subset of keys, or by using low-rank/kernel sketches, so that global interactions are compressed into a lower-dimensional representation. We propose \emph{ELSAA}, an efficient low-rank and sparse approximation of attention. Importantly, ELSAA does \emph{not} decompose the learned projection or output matrices of the Transformer into sparse and low-rank factors. Instead, after dense projections produce $Q,K,V$, ELSAA approximates the induced attention score operator itself: a sparse branch captures selected high-similarity interactions, while a low-rank branch summarizes diffuse global interactions. Since the two branches can be normalized over supports with very different denominator mass, ELSAA introduces a denominator-aware fusion term that scales the sparse branch according to its estimated attention mass relative to the low-rank branch. This gives a practical framework for constructing low-rank and sparse attention outputs without materializing the full quadratic score matrix, aiming to enable longer-context training while preserving both sharp token-level interactions and broad contextual mixing.

中文摘要翻译: 二次型的N×N注意力分数矩阵仍然是扩展Transformer以处理更长输入长度的核心障碍。现有的高效注意力方法通常通过施加稀疏性（使每个查询仅关注少量键）或使用低秩/核方法（将全局交互压缩到低维表示）来缓解这一瓶颈。我们提出了ELSAA，一种高效的低秩与稀疏注意力近似方法。重要的是，ELSAA并不对Transformer的学习投影矩阵或输出矩阵进行稀疏和低秩分解。相反，在密集投影生成Q、K、V之后，ELSAA直接近似诱导出的注意力分数算子本身：稀疏分支捕获选定的高相似度交互，而低秩分支汇总弥散的全局交互。由于两分支可以在支持集差异很大的情况下进行归一化，ELSAA引入了一个分母感知融合项，根据稀疏分支相对于低秩分支的估计注意力质量来缩放稀疏分支。这提供了一个实用的框架，用于在不实例化完整二次型分数矩阵的情况下构建低秩和稀疏注意力输出，旨在实现更长上下文的训练，同时保留尖锐的token级交互和广泛的上下文混合。

</details>


### [154] [User-Centric Modeling of Transactional Sequences with Explainable State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20228)
*Ivan Palagin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合对比表示学习（CoLES）与状态空间模型（Mamba）的混合方法用于交易事件序列的用户中心建模。通过在Mamba中注入CoLES嵌入作为用户先验，模型在三个公开数据集上均优于独立Mamba和CoLES+线性分类器，收敛速度提升2-3倍，并通过可解释性分析揭示了事件筛选机制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 对比学习方法能生成高质量的用户压缩表示，但现有编码器（RNN存在梯度消失问题，Transformer存在二次复杂度问题）均有局限。Mamba作为选择性状态空间模型可高效处理长程依赖，但在个性化用户分析中尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出两种集成策略：(1) 用CoLES嵌入初始化Mamba的隐藏状态；(2) 将投影后的CoLES嵌入作为前缀标记（prefix token）添加到输入序列前。两种方式均在第一步就为模型提供信息丰富的用户先验。

Result: 在Age（多分类年龄组预测）、MBD（多标签产品购买）、Taobao（二分类购买预测）三个公开数据集上，混合模型持续优于独立Mamba和CoLES+线性分类器，收敛速度比纯SSM基线快2-3倍。可解释性分析（离散化步长图和积分梯度）揭示了行为丰富数据集上的选择性事件筛选机制，并识别了最具信息量的交易特征。

Conclusion: 对比表示学习与状态空间模型的混合方法能有效提升用户建模性能与训练效率，并具有良好的可解释性。

Original Abstract: We propose a hybrid approach for user-centric modeling of transactional event sequences that combines contrastive representation learning (CoLES) with State Space Models (SSMs). While contrastive methods yield high-quality compressed user representations, existing encoders -- RNNs and Transformers -- suffer from vanishing gradients or quadratic complexity, respectively. Mamba, a selective SSM, efficiently handles long-range dependencies but remains underexplored for personalized user analysis. We investigate two integration strategies: (1)~initializing the Mamba hidden state with a CoLES embedding, and (2)~prepending the projected CoLES embedding as a prefix token to the input sequence. Both approaches supply the model with an informative user prior from the first step. Experiments on three public datasets -- Age (multiclass age-group prediction), MBD (multi-label product acquisition), and Taobao (binary purchase prediction) -- demonstrate consistent improvements over standalone Mamba and CoLES with a linear classifier, with the hybrid models converging 2--3$\times$ faster than the plain SSM baseline. Explainability analysis via discretization-step maps and Integrated Gradients reveals selective event filtering on behavior-rich datasets and identifies the most informative transaction features.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出一种面向交易事件序列的用户中心建模混合方法，该方法将对比表示学习（CoLES）与状态空间模型（SSM）相结合。虽然对比方法能生成高质量的压缩用户表示，但现有编码器——RNN和Transformer——分别存在梯度消失或二次复杂度问题。Mamba作为一种选择性状态空间模型，能高效处理长程依赖，但在个性化用户分析中尚未被充分探索。我们研究了两种集成策略：(1) 用CoLES嵌入初始化Mamba的隐藏状态，(2) 将投影后的CoLES嵌入作为前缀标记预置到输入序列前。两种方法均在第一步就为模型提供信息丰富的用户先验。在三个公开数据集（Age——多分类年龄组预测、MBD——多标签产品购买、Taobao——二分类购买预测）上的实验表明，混合模型持续优于独立的Mamba和CoLES+线性分类器，且收敛速度比纯SSM基线快2-3倍。通过离散化步长图和积分梯度进行的可解释性分析揭示了行为丰富数据集上的选择性事件过滤机制，并识别了最具信息量的交易特征。

</details>


### [155] [PIER: Physics-Informed Environmental Retrieval for Time-Series Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20230)
*Shiyuan Luo,Runlong Yu,Chonghao Qiu,Yue Qin,Rahul Ghosh,Robert Ladwig,Paul C. Hanson,Yiqun Xie,Xiaowei Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理信息环境检索（PIER）框架，通过结合嵌入检索与物理感知流（基于通量-响应一致性评分），并自适应平衡两路权重，提升了多系统环境建模的准确性与泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 环境系统建模受限于观测数据不足和物理动态多样性，现有检索增强方法依赖嵌入相似性但无法保证物理过程一致性，因此需要一种能融合物理知识约束的检索框架。

Method: PIER框架包含双流检索：嵌入检索流和物理感知流（利用局部验证器基于物理导出的通量特征对候选进行通量-响应一致性评分）；通过权重调整机制基于诊断特征自适应平衡两路权重。

Result: 在美国中西部356个湖泊跨越41年的实验中，PIER在水温和溶解氧预测任务上持续优于基线方法，并可作为通用增强策略适用于多种骨干模型。

Conclusion: PIER通过引入物理一致性约束有效提升了环境检索的可靠性，是一种模型无关的通用增强框架。

Original Abstract: Accurate modeling of environmental systems is fundamental to scientific understanding and decision-making, yet remains challenging because observations are limited and physical dynamics vary across systems. Retrieval-augmented approaches offer a natural path to transfer knowledge across systems, but standard embedding-based retrieval does not guarantee consistency of underlying physical processes, since scenarios with similar embeddings may arise from different underlying mechanisms. We propose Physics-Informed Environmental Retrieval (PIER), a model-agnostic framework that augments embedding-based retrieval with a physics-aware stream that scores candidates by flux-response consistency with the target, using local verifiers trained on physics-derived flux features. A weight adjustment mechanism then learns per-scenario weights that adaptively balance the two retrieval streams based on diagnostic features summarizing physics-stream reliability. Experiments on 356 lakes across the Midwestern United States spanning 41 years show that PIER consistently outperforms baselines for water temperature and dissolved oxygen prediction, and serves as a general augmentation strategy across diverse backbones.

中文摘要翻译: 环境系统的精确建模是科学理解与决策的基础，但由于观测数据有限且不同系统中的物理动态各异，这一任务仍然充满挑战。检索增强方法为跨系统知识迁移提供了自然路径，但标准基于嵌入的检索无法保证底层物理过程的一致性，因为具有相似嵌入的场景可能源于不同的物理机制。我们提出物理信息环境检索（PIER），一个模型无关的框架，它在嵌入检索的基础上增加了一个物理感知流：该流使用基于物理导出通量特征训练的局部验证器，根据候选与目标之间的通量-响应一致性进行评分。随后，权重调整机制根据描述物理流可靠性的诊断特征，学习每个场景的自适应权重，从而平衡两个检索流。在美国中西部356个湖泊跨越41年的实验表明，PIER在水温和溶解氧预测任务上持续优于基线方法，并且可作为适用于多种骨干模型的通用增强策略。

</details>


### [156] [PhaseAware: Interpretable Human-in-the-Loop Rehabilitation Scoring with Boundary Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20237)
*Yankai Zheng,Yuhe Liu,Yuxin Ma,Tianci Xue,Jiayuan Tian,Yu Fu,Yuxuan Hu,Jianing Wang,Zichun Xiao,Junya Mu,Shaohui Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PhaseAware 是一个结合时间骨干网络与相位/身体分组描述符的轻量级康复评估框架，通过门控残差机制实现高精度评分与结构化临床审查线索生成，在 UI-PRMD 和 KIMORE 数据集上表现优异，支持资源受限场景下的可解释康复质量评估。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有康复评分系统缺乏可解释性，难以在临床工作流中被医生有效审查和解读。作者希望开发一个既保持高精度又能够生成结构化审查线索的框架，以支持临床医生的人工审核、边界案例监控和人机协同分诊。

Method: 提出 PhaseAware 框架，结合时序骨干网络（temporal backbone）与相位描述符（phase descriptors）及身体分组描述符（body-group descriptors），通过骨干条件门控残差通路（backbone-conditioned gated residual pathway）稳定特征表示，并基于相位和身体级敏感性生成结构化审查线索。

Result: 在 UI-PRMD 深蹲协议上取得 0.0230 的 RMSE，相比已有基线降低 88.9%；在 KIMORE 深蹲子集上表现良好，表明相位感知设计在不同深蹲协议间具有可迁移性。

Conclusion: PhaseAware 提供了一种实用且可解释的康复评估方法，能够在保持临床医生监督的前提下，将自动评估整合到信息系统中，避免完全自主决策，适用于资源受限环境。

Original Abstract: Rehabilitation scoring systems are most useful when their outputs can be reviewed and interpreted within clinical workflows. This study presents PhaseAware, a compact framework for continuous rehabilitation quality assessment that combines a temporal backbone with phase- and body-group descriptors through a backbone-conditioned gated residual pathway. The model was evaluated on the UI-PRMD deep-squat protocol and further tested on the KIMORE squatting subset. On UI-PRMD, PhaseAware achieved an RMSE of 0.0230, corresponding to an 88.9% reduction relative to the accepted baseline. It also maintained favorable performance on KIMORE, suggesting that the phase-aware design transfers across related squatting protocols. In addition to score prediction, PhaseAware generates structured review cues based on phase- and body-level sensitivity, highlighting the movement stages and body regions most relevant to each prediction. The architecture employs a backbone-conditioned gated residual mechanism to stabilize feature representation, supporting use in resource-constrained settings. These cues are intended to support clinician review, boundary-case monitoring, and human-in-the-loop triage rather than autonomous decision-making. Overall, PhaseAware offers a practical and interpretable approach to rehabilitation scoring that may help integrate automated assessment into information systems while preserving clinician oversight.

中文摘要翻译: 康复评分系统在其输出能够在临床工作流中被审查和解读时最为有用。本研究提出了 PhaseAware，一个用于连续康复质量评估的轻量级框架，它通过骨干条件门控残差通路将时序骨干网络与相位描述符及身体分组描述符相结合。该模型在 UI-PRMD 深蹲协议上进行了评估，并进一步在 KIMORE 深蹲子集上进行了测试。在 UI-PRMD 上，PhaseAware 取得了 0.0230 的均方根误差（RMSE），相较已有基线降低了 88.9%。在 KIMORE 上同样保持了良好的性能，表明这种相位感知设计能够在相关深蹲协议间迁移。除评分预测外，PhaseAware 还基于相位和身体级敏感性生成结构化审查线索，突出显示与每个预测最相关的运动阶段和身体区域。该架构采用骨干条件门控残差机制来稳定特征表示，支持在资源受限环境中使用。这些线索旨在支持临床医生审查、边界案例监控以及人在回路分诊，而非自主决策。总体而言，PhaseAware 提供了一种实用且可解释的康复评分方法，有助于在保持临床医生监督的同时，将自动评估整合到信息系统中。

</details>


### [157] [Breaking the $T^{3/4}$ Barrier for Regret Minimization With Bi-Dimensional CDFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20258)
*Matteo Castiglioni,Anna Lunghi,Alberto Marchesi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类CDF相关目标函数的遗憾最小化问题，提出了一个遗憾界为\(\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{7/10})\)的算法，优于此前最优的\(\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{3/4})\)，并应用于双边贸易中的利润最大化问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有算法在\[0,1\]^2上学习形如\(g(x)\cdot\mathbb{P}(X\le x)\)的目标函数时，遗憾上界为\(\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{3/4})\)，且存在\(\Omega(T^{2/3})\)的下界。本文试图缩小这一差距，探究能否部分打破此类目标学习中的维度诅咒。

Method: 设计了一种新算法，通过更高效地利用部分累积信息（CDF结构）和二元反馈，实现了比现有方法更优的遗憾上界。具体技术细节需参考原文。

Result: 算法达到\(\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{7/10})\)的遗憾界，优于此前\(\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{3/4})\)的最优结果，但仍未达到\(\Omega(T^{2/3})\)的下界。在重复双边贸易固定价格利润最大化问题中也得到了相同的遗憾界。

Conclusion: 本文表明对于CDF相关目标函数，维度诅咒可被部分缓解，但最优遗憾界仍未闭合，\(\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{7/10})\)与下界\(\Omega(T^{2/3})\)之间仍存在差距。

Original Abstract: We study regret minimization for learning CDF-related objectives of the form \[ g(x)\cdot\mathbb{P}_{X\sim\mathcal{D}}(X\le x), \] over $[0,1]^2$, where $g$ is a known Lipschitz function and $\mathcal{D}$ is an unknown distribution. At each round $t$, the learner selects a point $x_t$ and observes the binary feedback $\mathbb{I}(X_t\le x_t)$, where $X_t\sim\mathcal{D}$. We design an algorithm achieving regret $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{7/10})$, improving over the previous best-known bound of $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{3/4})$ and showing that the curse of dimensionality can be at least partially lifted for this class of objectives, though a gap remains with the $Ω(T^{2/3})$ lower bound. As an application, our techniques yield the same $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{7/10})$ regret bound for profit maximization in repeated bilateral trade with fixed prices.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究在\([0,1]^2\)上学习形如\(g(x)\cdot\mathbb{P}_{X\sim\mathcal{D}}(X\le x)\)的CDF相关目标函数的遗憾最小化问题，其中\(g\)是已知的Lipschitz函数，\(\mathcal{D}\)是未知分布。在每一轮\(t\)，学习器选择一个点\(x_t\)并观测二元反馈\(\mathbb{I}(X_t\le x_t)\)，其中\(X_t\sim\mathcal{D}\)。我们设计了一个遗憾界为\(\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{7/10})\)的算法，优于此前最优的\(\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{3/4})\)界，表明对此类目标函数而言维度诅咒可被部分缓解，尽管与\(\Omega(T^{2/3})\)的下界之间仍存在差距。作为应用，我们的技术在重复双边贸易固定价格设定下，为利润最大化问题同样给出了\(\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{7/10})\)的遗憾界。

</details>


### [158] [Interpretable Fuzzy Rule-Based Regression Extension for Ex-Fuzzy Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20277)
*Cayan Deniz Kucuktopana,Javier Fumanal-Idocin,Richard Pitts,Javier Andreu-Perez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为Ex-Fuzzy库扩展了可解释的Mamdani模糊回归功能，引入基于模糊C均值聚类的目标感知划分初始化策略，在10个数据集上实验表明高斯划分优于均匀梯形划分，能在保持约0.86决定系数的同时生成10-15条可读规则。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 机器学习模型在回归任务中预测精度高，但在安全关键和监管领域缺乏可解释性；模糊规则系统虽具有透明可解释性，但Mamdani型模糊回归在现代机器学习库中仍未被充分支持。

Method: 为Ex-Fuzzy库扩展可解释回归功能，实现从数据直接学习标量后件的Mamdani模糊推理；提出基于模糊C均值聚类的目标感知划分初始化策略，通过增强输入-输出空间生成语言变量以强调特征空间中与输出相关的区域。

Result: 在KEEL库的10个回归数据集上，高斯划分策略一致优于均匀梯形划分策略，平均决定系数约0.86，规则库仅10-15条可读规则。

Conclusion: 所提出的实现为黑箱回归模型提供了透明且有竞争力的替代方案，在保持竞争性预测性能的同时支持实际可解释性。

Original Abstract: Machine learning models achieve high predictive accuracy in regression tasks, but their deployment in safety-critical and regulated domains requires interpretability. While fuzzy rule-based systems offer transparent, linguistically explicit interpretable models, Mamdani-style fuzzy regression remains underrepresented in modern machine learning software libraries. This paper presents an interpretable regression extension for the Ex-Fuzzy library, enabling Mamdani fuzzy inference with scalar consequents learned directly from data. For this, a target-aware partition initialisation strategy based on Fuzzy C-Means clustering is introduced, in which linguistic variables are derived from an augmented input-output space to emphasise output-relevant regions of the feature space. The proposed extension is evaluated on ten regression datasets from the KEEL repository, comparing Gaussian and trapezoidal partition strategies against standard baselines including linear regression, multilayer perceptron, and random forests. Experimental results show that Gaussian partitions consistently outperform uniform trapezoidal partitions, achieving a mean coefficient of determination of approximately 0.86 while producing compact rule bases of 10-15 human-readable rules. The proposed implementation provides a transparent and competitive alternative to black-box regression models, supporting practical interpretability with competitive predictive performance.

中文摘要翻译: 机器学习模型在回归任务中实现了高预测精度，但在安全关键和受监管领域的部署需要可解释性。虽然模糊规则系统提供了透明、语言上明确的可解释模型，但Mamdani型模糊回归在现代机器学习软件库中仍然代表性不足。本文为Ex-Fuzzy库提出了一种可解释的回归扩展，实现了直接从数据学习标量后件的Mamdani模糊推理。为此，引入了一种基于模糊C均值聚类的目标感知划分初始化策略，其中语言变量从增强的输入-输出空间中导出，以强调特征空间中与输出相关的区域。所提出的扩展在来自KEEL库的十个回归数据集上进行了评估，比较了高斯和梯形划分策略与包括线性回归、多层感知机和随机森林在内的标准基线。实验结果表明，高斯划分一致优于均匀梯形划分，平均决定系数约为0.86，同时产生包含10-15条人类可读规则的紧凑规则库。所提出的实现为黑箱回归模型提供了一种透明且有竞争力的替代方案，在保持竞争性预测性能的同时支持实际可解释性。

</details>


### [159] [The Blessing of Dimensionality: How Near-Orthogonality in High-Dimensional Spaces Explains Temporal Portability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20301)
*Abigail Woodring,Adrian Chan,Rana Muhammad Shahroz Khan,Sukwon Yun,Chau-Wai Wong,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过长期实验和理论分析，验证了PortLLM方法在持续预训练多步更新后仍具有时间可迁移性，并揭示了高维向量近正交性是这一现象的关键理论依据。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有PortLLM方法在持续预训练的短期时间可迁移性已被验证，但其长期表现和理论理解仍存在空白，本文旨在填补这两方面的不足。

Method: (1) 在Mistral、Gemma、Qwen三个基座模型上进行10步持续预训练，实证研究PortLLM补丁的长期时间可迁移性；(2) 从理论角度提供两种分析，解释PortLLM简单方法取得竞争性能的原因。

Result: 实证发现可迁移性在长时间跨度下依然保持，无需在基座模型周期性更新时重复微调；理论发现高维向量的近正交性是时间可迁移性的关键解释，并且损失景观的几何视角有助于比较不同适配方案。

Conclusion: PortLLM在持续预训练长期更新中仍具竞争力，高维向量近正交性是其有效性的理论基础。

Original Abstract: Fine-tuning has been widely used to adapt large language models (LLMs) for domain-specific tasks. Parameter efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods such as low-rank adaptation (LoRA) are frequently used to reduce computational costs. PortLLM is a training-free and data-free scheme used to adapt LLMs after continual pretraining. Although the initial PortLLM results show that LoRA patches exhibit short-term temporal portability, the long-term performance of PortLLM across several updates of continual pretraining remains underexplored. Furthermore, the intriguing effectiveness of PortLLM is not well understood from a theoretical standpoint. We address these two open questions by (1) performing an extensive empirical study of the long-term temporal portability of PortLLM patches across 10 continual pretraining steps using base models Mistral, Gemma, and Qwen; and (2) offering two theoretical analyses to explain our observation that the simple PortLLM method achieves competitive performance. We find empirically that the portability persists across longer time duration, indicating that repeated fine-tuning is not required when the base model is periodically updated. We find theoretically that near-orthogonality of high-dimensional vectors is a key justification for temporal portability. Our analyses also demonstrate a geometric perspective of the loss landscape in facilitating the theoretical comparison of different adaptation options.

中文摘要翻译: 微调已被广泛用于适配大规模语言模型以执行特定领域任务。参数高效微调方法（如低秩适配）常被用于降低计算成本。PortLLM是一种无训练且无数据的方案，用于在持续预训练后适配大语言模型。尽管初步的PortLLM结果显示LoRA补丁具有短期的时间可迁移性，但PortLLM在多次持续预训练更新后的长期性能仍未得到充分探索。此外，PortLLM令人瞩目的有效性在理论上也尚未被很好理解。本文通过以下方式解决这两个开放问题：(1) 使用基座模型Mistral、Gemma和Qwen，在10步持续预训练步骤上对PortLLM补丁的长期时间可迁移性进行了广泛的实证研究；(2) 提供两种理论分析来解释我们所观察到的现象，即简单的PortLLM方法能够取得有竞争力的性能。我们通过实证发现，可迁移性在更长时间跨度上仍然存在，表明当基座模型被周期性更新时，无需重复微调。我们从理论上发现，高维向量的近正交性是时间可迁移性的一个关键解释。我们的分析还展示了损失景观的几何视角，有助于对不同适配方案进行理论比较。

</details>


### [160] [Classical Hardware Acceleration of Quantum Autoencoders for Real-Time Anomaly Detection in Collider Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20302)
*Ivan Ge,Sagar Addepalli,Abhilasha Dave,Julia Gonski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了用于对撞机实时异常检测触发的变分量子自编码器模型，在FPGA上实现了首个量子机器学习触发系统，性能媲美经典方法且满足资源与时序约束。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在高能物理实时触发系统中部署量子机器学习，需要能够经典地仿真和编译量子电路，并将其综合到低延迟FPGA硬件上，以利用QML高效表示高维对撞数据中长程高阶相关性的优势。

Method: 采用变分量子自编码器（Variational Quantum Autoencoder）模型，进行量子电路的经典仿真与编译，最终将量子门综合到FPGA硬件加速器上。

Result: 该量子模型在异常检测性能上达到了与最先进经典方法相当的水平；经FPGA综合后，资源使用和时序约束均满足未来对撞机触发应用的要求。

Conclusion: 这项工作提供了首个用于高能物理触发的QML模型的FPGA实现，能够在经典数据采集流水线中部署更高能力的模型，同时推进对撞机实验基础设施的量子就绪度。

Original Abstract: Quantum machine learning (QML) algorithms in high energy physics (HEP) can efficiently represent and leverage long-range, high-order correlations in high-dimensional collider data, potentially with fewer parameters and favorable scaling relative to classical models. Deployment of QML in real-time collider applications such as trigger systems requires the ability to emulate and compile quantum circuits classically, then synthesize the resulting quantum gates onto low-latency hardware accelerators, namely field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs). We present a study of variational quantum autoencoder models for real-time anomaly detection triggers in modern collider experiments. The models achieve performance comparable to state-of-the-art classical approaches and, after FPGA synthesis, satisfy resource usage and timing constraints consistent with trigger applications in future colliders. This work provides one of the first FPGA implementations of QML models for HEP triggers, enabling higher-capability models in today's classical data acquisition pipelines while advancing quantum readiness of collider experiment infrastructure.

中文摘要翻译: 高能物理中的量子机器学习算法能够高效地表示并利用高维对撞数据中的长程、高阶相关性，且相较于经典模型，其参数量更少、扩展性更优。将量子机器学习部署到对撞机实时应用中（如触发系统），需要能够经典地仿真和编译量子电路，然后将生成的量子门综合到低延迟硬件加速器（即现场可编程门阵列）上。本文针对现代对撞机实验中的实时异常检测触发，提出了变分量子自编码器模型的研究。该模型在性能上与最先进的经典方法相当，并且在FPGA综合后，其资源使用和时序约束均满足未来对撞机触发应用的要求。这项工作提供了首个用于高能物理触发的量子机器学习模型的FPGA实现，能够在当今经典数据采集流水线中部署更高能力的模型，同时推进对撞机实验基础设施的量子就绪度。

</details>


### [161] [Multi-modal transformer for signal classification in nanopore blockade experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20323)
*Sandro Kuppel,Julian Hoßbach,Samuel Tovey,Christian Holm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多模态深度学习架构，联合处理原始时间序列、小波图像和静态特征向量三种信号表示，在纳米孔测序中实现了高精度分子识别，在42肽基准测试上超越现有方法10个百分点以上，并在20氨基酸数据集上达到接近完美的准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 纳米孔器件产生的信号高度复杂，将检测到的信号模式可靠地分配至特定分子是当前的主要挑战。

Method: 提出了一种多模态深度学习架构，联合处理多种信号表示：原始时间序列数据、基于小波变换的图像以及静态特征向量，通过注意力机制整合不同模态的互补信息。

Result: 在42肽基准测试中超越现有方法10个百分点以上；在20氨基酸数据集上达到接近完美的准确率；注意力分析表明时间序列和小波图像输入分别关注同一事件的不同特征。

Conclusion: 机器学习的多模态方法能够实现纳米孔传感器鲁棒、高精度的分子识别。

Original Abstract: Nanopore devices have emerged as powerful tools for single-molecule sensing, with potential for rapid, portable diagnostics. They detect changes in ionic current as analytes enter nanometer-scale pores, providing a means of identifying diverse biomarkers from their characteristic signal patterns. However, these signals are highly complex, and reliably assigning them to specific molecules remains a major challenge. Here, we address this by introducing a multi-modal deep learning architecture that jointly processes multiple signal representations, including raw time-series data, wavelet-based images, and static feature vectors. Our approach surpasses existing methods by more than 10 percentage points on a 42-peptide benchmark and transfers to a 20-amino-acid dataset with near-perfect accuracy. The model integrates complementary information from these representations, with attention analysis showing that the time-series and wavelet-image inputs emphasize different features of the same event. Together, these results demonstrate the potential of machine learning to enable robust, high-accuracy molecular identification with nanopore sensors.

中文摘要翻译: 纳米孔器件已成为单分子传感的有力工具，具有快速、便携式诊断的潜力。它们通过检测分析物进入纳米尺度孔道时离子电流的变化来感知分子，从而根据特征信号模式识别多种生物标志物。然而，这些信号高度复杂，如何将信号可靠地归因于特定分子仍是一大挑战。针对这一问题，本文提出了一种多模态深度学习架构，该架构联合处理多种信号表示，包括原始时间序列数据、基于小波变换的图像以及静态特征向量。我们的方法在42肽基准测试上超越现有方法10个百分点以上，并在20氨基酸数据集上实现了接近完美的准确率。该模型通过整合这些表示的互补信息，注意力分析表明时间序列和小波图像输入关注同一事件的不同特征。综合而言，这些结果展示了机器学习在实现纳米孔传感器鲁棒、高精度分子识别方面的巨大潜力。

</details>


### [162] [Interval and fuzzy physics-augmented neural networks (iPANN and fPANN) for uncertainty quantification and propagation in constitutive modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20339)
*Somesh Pratap Singh,Govinda Anantha Padmanabha,Jingye Tan,Steven Yang,Reese E. Jones,D. Thomas Seidl,Nikolaos Bouklas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出区间和模糊物理增强神经网络（iPANNs/fPANNs），用于含噪声超弹性本构建模中的无分布偶然不确定性量化，并通过有限元仿真验证其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在数据稀疏、含噪或异构条件下，本构建模的不确定性处理仍是可靠力学仿真的核心挑战，现有方法缺乏一种紧凑、物理一致且无需分布假设的偶然不确定性量化途径。

Method: 构建iPANN学习稀疏的下界、均值和上界自由能密度分支，通过自动微分获得应力并包络噪声观测；fPANN通过α-截集插值将iPANN嵌入模糊集表示；编码客观性、一致性和多凸性等力学约束，并采用平滑L0正则化促进可解释性；两阶段迁移学习先训练稀疏均值响应，再微调为上下界分支。

Result: 在含异方差噪声、不同随机实现、偏移噪声均值和变化噪声幅度的合成各向同性超弹性数据上，学习到的边界成功包络了含噪应力观测并在测试集上泛化；在有限元设置中验证了不确定性通过均值、上下界预测的传播。

Conclusion: 该框架为超弹性本构建模中无分布偶然不确定性量化及下游有限元仿真中的不确定性传播提供了紧凑、物理一致的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Constitutive modeling under uncertainty remains a central challenge for reliable mechanics simulations, particularly when the available stress-deformation data are sparse, noisy, or heterogeneous. We propose interval and fuzzy physics-augmented neural networks (iPANNs and fPANNs) for uncertainty-aware hyperelastic constitutive modeling. iPANNs learn sparse lower, mean, and upper free energy density branches whose stresses, obtained by automatic differentiation, ultimately enclose noisy stress observations. In contrast to this deterministic interval description, fPANNs embed the learned iPANN branches into a fuzzy-set representation through alpha-cut interpolation, yielding a nested family of admissible responses. iPANNs and fPANNs encode mechanistic constraints - preserving objectivity, consistency and promoting polyconvexity - and smoothed L0 regularization promotes interpretable energy representations. The bound models are trained through a two-stage transfer-learning procedure in which a sparse mean constitutive response is learned first and then fine-tuned into lower and upper energy branches. We evaluate the framework on synthetic isotropic hyperelastic data with heteroscedastic noise, varying random realizations, shifted noise means, and varying noise magnitudes. The results show that the learned bounds enclose noisy stress observations while generalizing to the test set. Further, we examine the propagation of uncertainty through the mean, upper and lower bound predictions of the learned iPANN models in a finite element setting. The proposed framework provides a compact, physics-consistent route for distribution-free aleatoric uncertainty quantification in hyperelastic constitutive modeling, and propagation in downstream finite element simulations.

中文摘要翻译: 不确定性条件下的本构建模仍然是可靠力学仿真的核心挑战，尤其是当可用的应力-变形数据稀疏、含噪或异构时。我们提出了区间物理增强神经网络（iPANNs）和模糊物理增强神经网络（fPANNs），用于不确定性感知的超弹性本构建模。iPANNs学习稀疏的下界、均值和上界自由能密度分支，通过自动微分获得的应力最终包络含噪的应力观测。与这种确定性区间描述不同，fPANNs通过α-截集插值将学到的iPANN分支嵌入到模糊集表示中，从而产生一个嵌套的可接受响应族。iPANNs和fPANNs编码了力学约束——保持客观性、一致性并促进多凸性——同时平滑L0正则化促进了可解释的能量表示。边界模型通过两阶段迁移学习过程进行训练：首先学习稀疏的均值本构响应，然后微调为下界和上界能量分支。我们在含异方差噪声、不同随机实现、偏移噪声均值和变化噪声幅度的合成各向同性超弹性数据上评估了该框架。结果表明，学习到的边界在泛化到测试集的同时包络了含噪的应力观测。此外，我们在有限元环境中考察了不确定性通过所学iPANN模型的均值、上界和下界预测的传播。所提出的框架为超弹性本构建模中的无分布偶然不确定性量化及其在下游有限元仿真中的传播提供了一条紧凑、物理一致的途径。

</details>


### [163] [Variance-reduced Domain Adaptation using Paired Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20367)
*Andrea Napoli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种名为PSDA的随机方差缩减技术，通过对域内和跨域样本配对形成四元组联合采样，解决无监督域自适应中分布对齐损失高方差且缺乏有限和结构的问题，实验表明该方法能降低方差并提升目标域精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 无监督域自适应中广泛使用的分布对齐损失（如相关性对齐和最大均值差异）在小批量优化时存在高方差问题，且这些损失缺乏有限和结构，无法直接应用经典的随机方差缩减方法。

Method: 提出PSDA（Paired Sampling for Domain Adaptation）方法，通过将域内和跨域的观测样本配对形成四元组，在训练时始终联合采样这些四元组。配对设计以最小化期望梯度方差为目标，转化为求解一组线性分配问题。

Result: 仿真实验表明PSDA相比相关方法降低了梯度方差；在三个域偏移数据集上的实验显示目标域准确率得到提升。

Conclusion: PSDA通过配对采样策略有效降低了分布对齐损失的梯度方差，提升了无监督域自适应的性能。

Original Abstract: Correlation alignment and the maximum mean discrepancy are two widely used distribution-matching frameworks for unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA). However, high variance in these losses has been shown to undermine their effectiveness in minibatch optimisation settings. Furthermore, the losses lack finite-sum structure, which renders them incompatible with classical stochastic variance reduction (SVR) methods. This paper proposes Paired Sampling for Domain Adaptation (PSDA), a novel SVR technique tailored to such objectives. PSDA pairs observations both within and across domains, to form quadruplets that are always sampled together during training. The pairings are designed to minimise expected gradient variance, and reduce to solving a set of linear assignment problems. Our simulations demonstrate reduced variance compared to related methods, and experiments on three domain shift datasets show improved target domain accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 相关性对齐和最大均值差异是无监督域自适应中广泛使用的两种分布匹配框架。然而，已有研究表明这些损失在小批量优化环境中存在高方差问题，从而削弱了其有效性。此外，这些损失缺乏有限和结构，使其无法与经典的随机方差缩减方法兼容。本文提出了面向域自适应的配对采样（PSDA），一种针对此类目标函数量身定制的新型随机方差缩减技术。PSDA在域内和跨域之间对观测样本进行配对，形成在训练过程中始终联合采样的四元组。配对的设计旨在最小化期望梯度方差，并可转化为求解一组线性分配问题。我们的仿真实验表明，与相关方法相比，该方法降低了方差；在三个域偏移数据集上的实验显示，目标域准确率得到了提升。

</details>


### [164] [Online Variance Reduction for Domain Adaptation on Streaming Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20374)
*Andrea Napoli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ARROW，首个针对流式数据的在线随机方差缩减算法，用于MMD和CORAL损失函数，通过自适应重加权机制实现了与离线算法相当的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MMD和CORAL损失的离线SVR算法无法兼容在线、分布式或增量学习场景，缺乏面向流式数据的在线方差缩减方法。

Method: 维护对齐统计量的移动平均参考，并自适应地重加权传入的小批量数据，使小批量统计量与参考统计量对齐；同时提出松弛重加权方案以解决权重优化问题的可解性。

Result: 实验和仿真表明，ARROW在运行时间、方差缩减程度和目标域准确率方面与离线算法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: ARROW作为首个在线SVR算法，能够有效处理流式数据下的MMD和CORAL损失方差缩减问题，性能可与离线算法媲美。

Original Abstract: This paper studies the problem of stochastic variance reduction (SVR) for the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) and correlation alignment (CORAL) loss functions. Although various offline SVR algorithms for these losses have been proposed, these are incompatible with online, distributed, or incremental learning settings. This paper presents Adaptive vaRiance Reduction via Online reWeighting (ARROW), the first online SVR algorithm for the MMD and CORAL for streamed data. The method maintains moving average references of the alignment statistics, and adaptively reweights incoming minibatches so that the minibatch and reference statistics are aligned. Further, we propose a relaxed reweighting scheme so that the ensuing weight-optimisation problem is tractable. In experiments and simulations, we show that ARROW performs competitively with offline algorithms in terms of runtime, degree of variance reduction achieved, and target domain accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了最大均值差异（MMD）和相关性对齐（CORAL）损失函数的随机方差缩减（SVR）问题。尽管目前已提出多种针对这些损失的离线SVR算法，但它们无法兼容在线、分布式或增量学习场景。本文提出了自适应重加权在线方差缩减算法（ARROW），这是首个面向流式数据、用于MMD和CORAL损失的在线SVR算法。该方法维护对齐统计量的移动平均参考，并自适应地重加权传入的小批量数据，使小批量统计量与参考统计量对齐。此外，我们提出了一种松弛重加权方案，使后续的权重优化问题具有可解性。实验和仿真结果表明，ARROW在运行时间、方差缩减程度以及目标域准确率方面与离线算法相比具有竞争力。

</details>


### [165] [PG-KINN: A Physics-Informed Petrov-Galerkin Kolmogorov-Arnold Network for Solving Forward and Inverse PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20378)
*Amirhossein Sadr,Nima Soltani,Vahideh Moghtadaiee,Aida Pakniyat,Dara Rahmati,Saeid Gorgin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PG-KINN，一种基于Petrov-Galerkin形式的物理信息KAN，通过将KAN作为试空间、紧支撑分段多项式作为测试空间，克服了传统PINN和KAN的局限性，在计算力学基准上取得了优异性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有物理信息学习方法（MLP基PINN）存在谱偏差和密集参数化问题，KAN虽有所缓解但物理损失函数的选择同样关键：强形式需高阶导数和重加权损失、能量形式限于自伴算子且对参数识别问题失效、边界积分形式需已知基本解。因此需要一种更鲁棒且通用的离散化策略。

Method: 提出PG-KINN，采用Petrov-Galerkin形式：试空间为KAN，测试空间为独立、紧支撑的分段多项式空间，使用Gauss-Legendre求积进行评估。通过分部积分降低微分阶数，保持对一般非自伴、非线性及反问题的适用性，局部化测试函数将全局残差转化为条件数良好的单元弱残差集合。

Result: 在裂纹奇异性、应力集中、Neo-Hookean超弹性、非均匀介质反参数识别及复杂几何等基准测试中，PG-KINN一致优于传统MLP基线和最先进的KAN基强形式/能量形式/反问题公式（PIKAN）。

Conclusion: 将KAN试空间与多项式测试空间的Petrov-Galerkin耦合定位为AI计算力学中一种鲁棒且准确的路径。

Original Abstract: Physics-informed learning of partial differential equations (PDEs) has been dominated by multilayer perceptrons (MLPs), whose spectral bias and dense parameterization limit both accuracy and interpretability. Kolmogorov Arnold Networks (KANs) mitigate these limitations because their learnable spline activations are structurally aligned with the piecewise-polynomial bases of classical discretizations. However, the way a PDE is cast into a loss functional is as decisive as the choice of approximator: strong-form residual minimization requires high-order derivatives and heavily weighted losses, the energy (Bubnov-Galerkin) form is restricted to self-adjoint operators and, as we show, collapses to a trivial solution for parameter-identification problems, and boundary integral forms require a known fundamental solution. We propose PG-KINN, a physics-informed KAN built on a Petrov-Galerkin formulation in which the trial space is a KAN and the test space is an independent, compactly supported, piecewise-polynomial space evaluated with Gauss-Legendre quadrature. Integration by parts lowers the differentiation order while retaining applicability to general non-self-adjoint, nonlinear, and inverse problems; the localized test functions turn the global residual into a set of element-wise weak residuals with favorable conditioning. On a suite of benchmarks spanning crack singularities, stress concentration, Neo-Hookean hyperelasticity, inverse parameter identification in heterogeneous media, and complex geometries, PG-KINN consistently outperforms legacy MLP baselines and state-of-the-art KAN-based strong/energy/inverse formulations (PIKAN). These results position the Petrov-Galerkin coupling of KAN trial spaces and polynomial test spaces as a robust and accurate route for AI-based computational mechanics.

中文摘要翻译: 物理信息学习偏微分方程（PDE）长期以来由多层感知器（MLP）主导，但其谱偏差和密集参数化限制了精度和可解释性。Kolmogorov Arnold网络（KAN）通过可学习的样条激活函数在结构上对齐了经典离散化中的分段多项式基，从而缓解了这些限制。然而，将PDE转化为损失函数的方式与逼近器的选择同样关键：强形式残差最小化需要高阶导数和重加权损失；能量（Bubnov-Galerkin）形式限于自伴算子，且我们证明其对参数识别问题会坍缩为平凡解；边界积分形式则需要已知的基本解。我们提出PG-KINN，一种基于Petrov-Galerkin形式的物理信息KAN，其中试空间为KAN，测试空间为独立、紧支撑的分段多项式空间，使用Gauss-Legendre求积进行评估。分部积分降低了微分阶数，同时保持对一般非自伴、非线性和反问题的适用性；局部化测试函数将全局残差转化为一组条件数良好的单元弱残差。在涵盖裂纹奇异性、应力集中、Neo-Hookean超弹性、非均匀介质反参数识别以及复杂几何的基准测试中，PG-KINN一致优于传统MLP基线和最先进的基于KAN的强形式/能量形式/反问题公式（PIKAN）。这些结果将KAN试空间与多项式测试空间的Petrov-Galerkin耦合定位为AI计算力学中一条鲁棒且准确的路径。

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [ST-DDA: Dynamic Channel Estimation in the Doppler-Delay-Angle Domain via Sparse Subspace Tracking for TDD Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19677)
*Xu Zhu,Tiejun Li*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对跳频TDD系统中全频段信道获取困难的问题，提出ST-DDA框架，通过在Doppler-延迟-角度域进行在线稀疏子空间跟踪，利用历史观测和窗口重叠提升信道重建精度与效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在跳频时分双工（TDD）系统中，每个探测时隙只能观测有限子带，传统单时隙恢复方法无法充分利用历史观测信息，导致全频段信道获取精度受限。

Method: 提出ST-DDA在线稀疏子空间跟踪框架，包含：1) 利用Doppler域能量集中性进行窗口化DDA域稀疏恢复；2) 利用相邻窗口重叠进行热启动加速；3) 采用交替子空间方法在支撑诱导子空间内进行正则化最小二乘更新；4) 结合位置编码卷积重加权以利用局部角度和多普勒结构。

Result: 实验表明，重加权ST-DDA在更长的探测间隔和更大的跳频周期下，比动态压缩感知基线方法实现了更准确可靠的重建，同时保持了相当的单时隙运行时间。

Conclusion: ST-DDA通过在线稀疏子空间跟踪与窗口重叠热启动，有效解决了跳频TDD系统中全频段信道重建的精度与效率平衡问题。

Original Abstract: Accurate full-band channel acquisition in frequency-hopping time-division duplex (TDD) systems is challenging because each sounding slot observes only a limited frequency subband, while conventional single-slot recovery cannot fully exploit historical observations. We propose ST-DDA, an online sparse-subspace tracking framework for latest-slot reconstruction in the Doppler--delay--angle (DDA) domain. We first show that the Doppler-domain representation remains energy-concentrated under moderate channel variation, thereby supporting windowed DDA-domain sparse recovery. A local stability analysis further shows that the substantial overlap between adjacent windows enables the preceding-window estimate to warm-start each window-specific recovery problem, allowing the optimization progress to be carried across slots under a fixed per-slot iteration budget. For computational tractability, ST-DDA employs the alternating subspace method, which restricts the regularized least-squares fidelity updates to support-induced subspaces, together with position-encoded convolutional reweighting that exploits local angular and Doppler structures. Experiments show that reweighted ST-DDA achieves more accurate and reliable reconstruction than dynamic compressed-sensing baselines, particularly for longer sounding intervals and larger frequency-hopping periods, while maintaining comparable per-slot runtime.

中文摘要翻译: 在跳频时分双工（TDD）系统中，由于每个探测时隙仅能观测有限频率子带，实现精确的全频段信道获取具有挑战性；而传统的单时隙恢复方法无法充分利用历史观测信息。为此，我们提出ST-DDA——一种用于Doppler-延迟-角度（DDA）域最新时隙重建的在线稀疏子空间跟踪框架。我们首先证明，在中等信道变化条件下，Doppler域表示仍保持能量集中性，从而支持窗口化DDA域的稀疏恢复。进一步的局部稳定性分析表明，相邻窗口间存在显著的重叠，使得前一个窗口的估计可以为每个窗口特定的恢复问题提供热启动，从而在固定每时隙迭代预算下将优化进度跨时隙传递。为了计算可追踪性，ST-DDA采用交替子空间方法，将正则化最小二乘保真度更新限制在支撑诱导子空间内，并结合位置编码卷积重加权以利用局部角度和多普勒结构。实验表明，与动态压缩感知基线方法相比，重加权ST-DDA在更长的探测间隔和更大的跳频周期下实现了更准确、更可靠的重建，同时保持了相当的单时隙运行时间。

</details>


### [167] [SpiRadar: Radar-Based Non-Contact Spirometry via Sparse Polynomial Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19798)
*Yonathan Eder,Yhonatan Kvich,Naama Golan,Adi Wagerhoff,Safit Levy,Lior Barbash,Ron Berant,Patrick Stafler,Yonina C. Eldar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出SpiRadar，一种基于雷达的非接触式肺功能检测框架，可在无需口嘴或鼻夹的情况下实现肺功能曲线重建、参数估计和支气管舒张反应评估，在39名儿童（6-18岁）的验证中达到临床级性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统肺功能检测（肺活量测定）依赖口嘴和鼻夹，导致患者不适且操作难度大，尤其在儿童人群中配合困难，影响检测质量。现有非接触替代方法性能不足，亟需一种临床可行的无接触肺功能评估方案。

Method: (1) 基于生理动机的预处理实现鲁棒信号提取；(2) 特征依赖的多项式变换，建立雷达测量的胸腔位移与肺活量体积曲线之间的映射；(3) 稀疏优化实现无需个体校准即可泛化到未见受试者。采用受试者级留一法交叉验证进行严格评估。

Result: 在39名儿童（6-18岁，包括健康儿童和哮喘患者）58次肺功能测试中：曲线重建平均RMSE为0.23±0.12 L；关键肺功能参数相关系数超过0.84；支气管舒张反应分类准确率89.5%，敏感性90.9%，特异性87.5%。

Conclusion: 该非接触式肺功能检测方法在临床可行性上得到验证，尤其适用于儿童哮喘监测和远程患者护理。

Original Abstract: Chronic respiratory diseases affect hundreds of millions of people worldwide, with spirometry serving as the gold standard for pulmonary function assessment. However, conventional spirometry's reliance on mouthpieces and nose clips creates discomfort and technical challenges that can compromise test quality, particularly in pediatric populations where cooperation difficulties are amplified. This study presents SpiRadar, a comprehensive radar-based framework for non-contact spirometry that eliminates physical contact requirements while enabling accurate curve reconstruction, clinical parameter estimation, and bronchodilator response (BDR) assessment. Our key contributions include: (1) a methodological framework integrating physiologically-motivated preprocessing for robust signal extraction, a feature-dependent polynomial transformation linking radar-measured thoracic displacement to spirometric volume curves, and sparse optimization enabling generalization to unseen subjects without individual calibration; (2) a rigorous validation on a pediatric cohort of 39 subjects (ages 6-18 years) undergoing 58 spirometry trials, including healthy children and asthma patients tested pre- and post-bronchodilator, using subject-level leave-one-out cross-validation; (3) clinical-grade performance outperforming alternative non-contact methods, achieving accurate curve reconstruction (mean RMSE: 0.23 +- 0.12 L) and strong correlations (above 0.84) for key spirometric parameters. BDR classification achieved 89.5% accuracy with balanced sensitivity (90.9%) and specificity (87.5%). These results establish clinical feasibility for our non-contact spirometry approach, with particular promise for pediatric asthma monitoring and remote patient care.

中文摘要翻译: 慢性呼吸系统疾病影响着全球数亿人口，肺活量测定是肺功能评估的金标准。然而，传统肺活量测定依赖口嘴和鼻夹，不仅造成患者不适，还带来技术挑战，可能损害检测质量，尤其在配合困难的儿童人群中问题更为突出。本研究提出SpiRadar——一个基于雷达的综合框架，用于非接触式肺功能检测，无需物理接触即可实现准确的曲线重建、临床参数估计和支气管舒张反应评估。我们的主要贡献包括：(1) 一个方法论框架，整合了基于生理动机的预处理以实现鲁棒信号提取、将雷达测量的胸腔位移映射到肺活量体积曲线的特征依赖多项式变换，以及通过稀疏优化实现无需个体校准即可泛化到未见受试者的能力；(2) 在39名受试者（年龄6-18岁）组成的儿童队列中进行的严格验证，该队列进行了58次肺功能测试，包括健康儿童和哮喘患者在支气管舒张剂使用前后的测试，采用受试者级留一法交叉验证；(3) 临床级性能超越了其他非接触方法，实现了准确的曲线重建（平均均方根误差：0.23 ± 0.12 L）以及关键肺功能参数的强相关性（均高于0.84）。支气管舒张反应分类达到了89.5%的准确率，具有平衡的敏感性（90.9%）和特异性（87.5%）。这些结果确立了我们的非接触式肺功能检测方法的临床可行性，在儿童哮喘监测和远程患者护理方面具有特别的应用前景。

</details>


### [168] [WARA: A Closed-Loop Multi-Agent Framework for Wireless Optimization Autoresearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19822)
*Yuan Guo,Yilong Chen,Chao Hu,Xianghao Yu,Liang Hong,Jie Xu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向无线领域的端到端自动研究框架WARA，采用闭环多智能体系统，将初始研究主题转化为完整研究包，并通过工件验证与修复机制确保研究质量，性能接近已接收的同行评审论文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大语言模型智能体在工具使用、代码执行和迭代修订方面展现出的能力为自动化科学研究提供了新机遇，但目前缺乏面向无线通信领域的端到端自动研究框架，特别是在无线资源分配优化这一关键领域。

Method: 提出WARA（Wireless AutoResearch Agent）闭环多智能体系统，将研究工作流组织为三个阶段：1）研究缺口识别与问题提出，2）优化建模、算法设计与实验，3）研究交付物构建。每个阶段采用工件中介过程，由控制器管理的门控机制验证工件并维护一致性，验证失败时仅修复受影响的工件而非重启整个流程。另设计了基于LLM的ScoringAgent评估手稿级研究有效性。

Result: WARA显著优于一次性LLM生成方法，其质量接近近期已接收的同行评审论文水平。这表明闭环工件控制策略在端到端LLM辅助无线优化研究中具有实际潜力。

Conclusion: 闭环工件控制机制能够有效保障自动化科研流程的一致性和质量，WARA框架证明了LLM在无线资源分配优化领域进行端到端自动化研究的可行性和有效性。

Original Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have shown growing capabilities in tool use, code execution, artifact inspection, and iterative revision, creating new opportunities for automating scientific research. To the best of our knowledge, this paper presents the first end-to-end autoresearch framework for the wireless domain, with a particular focus on wireless resource allocation optimization, an essential area for characterizing the fundamental performance limits of wireless systems and enhancing their practical performance under dynamic channel and network conditions. Specifically, we propose the Wireless AutoResearch Agent (WARA), a closed-loop multi-agent system that transforms an initial research topic into a complete research package. WARA organizes the research workflow into three phases: 1) research gap identification and problem proposal, 2) optimization modeling, algorithm design, and experimentation, and 3) research deliverable construction. Each phase follows an artifact-mediated process, in which structured upstream artifacts are consumed to generate downstream outputs. Controller-managed gates validate these artifacts and maintain consistency among problem formulations, algorithms, experiments, and research claims. When validation fails, WARA repairs only the affected artifact instead of restarting the entire workflow. We further design an LLM-based ScoringAgent to evaluate manuscript-level research validity. Comparative results show that WARA substantially outperforms one-shot LLM generation and approaches the quality profile of recently accepted peer-reviewed papers. These results demonstrate the potential of closed-loop artifact control for end-to-end LLM-assisted wireless optimization research. The source code is available at https://github.com/guoyuan-dotcom/WARA_CUHKSZ

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型（LLM）智能体在工具使用、代码执行、工件检查和迭代修订方面展现出日益增长的能力，为自动化科学研究创造了新的机遇。据我们所知，本文提出了首个面向无线领域的端到端自动研究框架，特别聚焦于无线资源分配优化——这是刻画无线系统基本性能极限并在动态信道与网络条件下提升其实际性能的关键领域。具体而言，我们提出了WARA（Wireless AutoResearch Agent），一个闭环多智能体系统，能够将初始研究主题转化为完整的研究成果包。WARA将研究工作流组织为三个阶段：1）研究缺口识别与问题提出，2）优化建模、算法设计与实验，3）研究交付物构建。每个阶段遵循工件中介过程，其中结构化的上游工件被消费以生成下游输出。控制器管理的门控机制验证这些工件，并维护问题表述、算法、实验和研究声明之间的一致性。当验证失败时，WARA仅修复受影响的工件，而非重启整个工作流。我们进一步设计了基于LLM的ScoringAgent来评估手稿级别的研究有效性。对比结果表明，WARA显著优于一次性LLM生成方法，并且其质量接近近期已接收的同行评审论文水平。这些结果证明了闭环工件控制在端到端LLM辅助无线优化研究中的潜力。源代码可在 https://github.com/guoyuan-dotcom/WARA_CUHKSZ 获取。

</details>


### [169] [Optimal Sensor Placement via Graph-constrained Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19831)
*Feng Ji,Jingyang Dai,Wee Peng Tay,Sirajudeen Gulam Razul*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文将传感器最优放置问题重新建模为连续空间上的生成式建模问题，利用流匹配模型学习最优传感器配置的分布，从而避免传统图信号处理方法中依赖离散图组合优化的高计算成本，并实现任意连续坐标的传感器部署。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统图信号处理方法将传感器放置问题建模为离散图上的组合优化，计算成本高，且传感器位置被限制在图的顶点上，无法在连续空间中灵活部署。

Method: 先通过离线GSP优化生成最优传感器配置的训练样本，再用流匹配模型学习其分布；在推理阶段，利用学习到的速度场直接生成连续传感器坐标，无需在线组合优化；此外还开发了置换不变的条件生成框架以支持固定锚点传感器场景。

Result: 在真实无线电传播模拟器上的实验验证了所提框架在传感器放置任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于流匹配的连续空间生成式方法能有效解决传感器最优放置问题，克服了传统方法的离散限制和高计算开销。

Original Abstract: Optimal sensor placement is a fundamental problem in graph signal processing (GSP), where a limited number of sensors are deployed to reconstruct a continuous signal field. Existing GSP methods rely on combinatorial optimization over discretized graphs, resulting in high computational cost and sensor locations restricted to graph vertices. We reformulate sensor placement as a continuous-space generative modeling problem. An offline GSP optimization routine first generates training samples of optimal sensor configurations, from which a flow matching (FM) model learns their distribution. At inference, the learned velocity field directly generates continuous sensor coordinates, eliminating online combinatorial optimization. We further develop a permutation-invariant conditional generation framework for deployment with fixed anchor sensors. Experiments on a realistic radio propagation simulator demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework for sensor placement.

中文摘要翻译: 传感器最优放置是图信号处理中的一个基本问题，目标是用有限数量的传感器部署来重建连续信号场。现有的图信号处理方法依赖于在离散化图上的组合优化，导致计算成本高，且传感器位置受限于图的顶点。本文将传感器放置重新建模为连续空间上的生成式建模问题。首先通过离线GSP优化流程生成最优传感器配置的训练样本，然后利用流匹配模型学习其分布。在推理阶段，学习到的速度场直接生成连续的传感器坐标，无需在线组合优化。我们进一步开发了置换不变的条件生成框架，以支持固定锚点传感器的部署场景。在真实无线电传播模拟器上的实验证明了所提框架在传感器放置中的有效性。

</details>


### [170] [A Covert Precision Satellite Communication Framework Assisted by Cooperative IRSs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19883)
*Haoyang Wu,Yunfan Bai,Mei Shen,Yuwen Qian,Guangji Chen,Long Shi,Feng Shu,Jun Li*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种多智能反射面（IRS）协作辅助卫星隐蔽精确无线通信（CPWC）系统，结合OFDM随机子载波选择（RSCS）方法，在相对熵与检测错误概率约束下，通过交替优化与序列二次规划算法最大化隐蔽速率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 卫星通信链路固有开放性使其易遭窃听，存在严重安全隐患，现有方案在隐蔽性和精确性上仍有不足。

Method: 构建多IRS协作辅助的卫星隐蔽精确无线通信系统；提出基于OFDM的随机子载波选择方法集中信号能量；推导基于相对熵和检测错误概率的闭式隐蔽约束；采用交替优化的半定松弛迭代算法优化速率，再以序列二次规划算法精化波束赋形器。

Result: 所提CPWC系统有效；基于检测错误概率的方案在隐蔽速率上优于SOCP基准、随机相位设计、1-bit IRS量化方案以及无精确通信的SDR基线。

Conclusion: 多IRS协作与随机子载波选择可有效提升卫星通信的隐蔽性与精确性，为卫星隐蔽通信提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Satellite communication (SatCom), as an effective complement to terrestrial networks, has attracted considerable attention from both academia and industry owing to its wide coverage and high flexibility. However, the inherent openness of satellite links renders them highly vulnerable to eavesdropping, thereby posing significant security challenges. In this paper, we propose a satellite covert precision wireless communication (CPWC) system, where multiple intelligent reflecting surfaces (IRSs) cooperate to assist satellite transmissions, ensuring that confidential information is delivered to legitimate users while remaining undetectable to wardens. To further enhance covertness, an orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM)-based random subcarrier selection (RSCS) method is developed to concentrate the signal energy at the intended receiver. Under a practical satellite-terrestrial channel model, we derive closed-form covertness constraints for the CPWC system based on relative entropy and detection error probability. Under the relative-entropy constraint and the satellite power constraint, we maximize the covert rate by an alternating-optimization (AO) based semidefinite relaxation (SDR) iterative algorithm and obtain a high-quality feasible solution. Using this solution as a warm start, we further impose the detection-error-probability constraint and refine the beamformer through a sequential quadratic programming (SQP) based algorithm. Numerical results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed CPWC system, where the detection-error-probability-based scheme outperforms the second-order cone programming (SOCP) benchmark, the random-phase-shift design, the one-bit IRS quantized scheme, and the SDR baseline without precise communication (PC) in terms of covert rate.

中文摘要翻译: 卫星通信作为地面网络的有效补充，因其广覆盖和高灵活性而受到学术界和工业界的广泛关注。然而，卫星链路固有的开放性使其极易遭受窃听，从而带来重大的安全挑战。本文提出一种卫星隐蔽精确无线通信（CPWC）系统，其中多个智能反射面（IRS）协作辅助卫星传输，确保机密信息传递给合法用户的同时，对监控者保持不可检测。为进一步增强隐蔽性，开发了一种基于正交频分复用（OFDM）的随机子载波选择（RSCS）方法，将信号能量集中到目标接收端。在实用的卫星-地面信道模型下，我们基于相对熵和检测错误概率推导了CPWC系统的闭式隐蔽约束。在相对熵约束和卫星功率约束下，我们通过基于交替优化（AO）的半定松弛（SDR）迭代算法最大化隐蔽速率，并获得高质量可行解。以此解作为热启动，进一步施加检测错误概率约束，通过基于序列二次规划（SQP）的算法精化波束赋形器。数值结果表明了所提CPWC系统的有效性，其中基于检测错误概率的方案在隐蔽速率上优于二阶锥规划（SOCP）基准、随机相位设计、单比特IRS量化方案以及无精确通信（PC）的SDR基线。

</details>


### [171] [Graph Distribution-valued Signals in Wasserstein Spaces: Theory and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20008)
*Yanan Zhao,Feng Ji,Xingchao Jian,Wee Peng Tay*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种将图信号表示为Wasserstein空间中概率测度（即图分布值信号，GDS）的图信号处理（GSP）框架，克服了传统向量GSP需要完全同步观测和严格时序对应等局限，并支持信号依赖的图结构建模与不确定性量化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统向量化GSP需要所有顶点同步观测才能得到完整的图信号，且要求观测的滤波器输入-输出对在时间上严格对应，这些假设在实际应用中往往难以满足。此外，真实世界中的图结构常依赖于信号本身，而传统方法缺乏对图拓扑不确定性的显式建模。

Method: 将图信号建模为Wasserstein空间中的概率分布（GDS），而非传统的向量表示；将图结构建模为以信号实现为条件的条件分布，从而同时处理信号依赖图结构和拓扑不确定性。在此基础上建立GSP基本概念与GDS对应概念的映射关系，并推导GDS变换的理论连续性，保证对输入扰动和分布近似下的稳定性。

Result: 在理论上证明了GDS变换的连续性，提供了稳定性保证；在图滤波器学习和异常检测等示例应用中展示了方法的有效性，并通过实证研究验证了其性能。

Conclusion: 该框架在严格推广传统图信号（可视为Dirac delta测度）的同时，内在地捕捉了不确定性和随机性。经典GSP公式作为该框架的特例自然浮现，为处理不确定图信号和信号依赖图结构提供了统一且理论完备的解决方案。

Original Abstract: We introduce a framework for graph signal processing (GSP) in which signals are represented as graph distribution-valued signals (GDSs), i.e., probability measures in a Wasserstein space. This perspective addresses fundamental limitations of classical vector-based GSP, including the requirement for complete synchronous observations across vertices and the need for strict temporal correspondence in observed filter input--output pairs. Furthermore, by modeling the graph structure as a distribution conditioned on signal realizations, we provide a principled approach to signal-dependent graph structures, which are common in real-world applications, while explicitly encoding uncertainty in graph topology. Our framework inherently captures uncertainty and stochasticity while strictly generalizing traditional graph signals, which can be interpreted as Dirac delta measures. We develop a systematic correspondence between foundational GSP concepts and their GDS analogs, showing that classical formulations emerge as special cases of our framework. We establish theoretical continuity results for GDS transforms, providing stability guarantees for input perturbations and distribution approximations. We demonstrate the utility of this approach through example applications, including graph filter learning and anomaly detection, and validate its effectiveness through empirical studies.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种图信号处理（GSP）框架，其中信号被表示为图分布值信号（GDS），即Wasserstein空间中的概率测度。这一视角解决了传统基于向量的GSP的根本性局限，包括需要在所有顶点上进行完全同步观测，以及观测到的滤波器输入-输出对需严格时间对应的要求。此外，通过将图结构建模为以信号实现为条件的条件分布，我们为信号依赖的图结构（这在真实世界应用中很常见）提供了原则性的处理方法，同时显式编码了图拓扑中的不确定性。我们的框架内在地捕捉了不确定性和随机性，同时严格推广了传统图信号——后者可被解释为Dirac delta测度。我们建立了基础GSP概念与其GDS对应概念之间的系统对应关系，表明经典公式作为我们框架的特例自然涌现。我们为GDS变换建立了理论连续性结果，为输入扰动和分布近似提供了稳定性保证。我们通过示例应用（包括图滤波器学习和异常检测）展示了该方法的实用性，并通过实证研究验证了其有效性。

</details>


### [172] [Drift-Aware RL-based Wavelet Denoising for Network-Traffic Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20011)
*Priyalakshmi Sheela,Indrakshi Dey*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种漂移感知的自适应小波去噪框架，将去噪作为预处理层优化异常检测和容量估计任务，通过强化学习策略选择小波配置，优于传统去噪方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 网络监测中的流量测量受加性噪声和统计漂移（均值、方差、分布形状或尾部行为的时变变化）污染。静态小波去噪在独立同分布高斯假设下校准，在漂移存在时失配，且在中高信噪比下过度抑制有用结构，导致监测决策退化。

Method: 提出漂移感知框架，将自适应小波去噪作为预处理层，针对两个下游任务（异常检测和容量估计）进行优化。采用四检测器门控（Page-Hinkley、方差比、Jensen-Shannon、Anderson-Darling）判断何时调用学习策略，Proximal Policy Optimization智能体在混合离散-连续动作空间中选择每窗口的小波配置。奖励函数基于下游任务效用而非重构保真度。

Result: 针对每种漂移类型和输入信噪比，将所提去噪器与低通移动平均滤波器、VisuShrink、SureShrink、BayesShrink和Wiener滤波器进行基准对比。异常目标定义在干净信号上，漂移门控定义在污染上，确保两阶段非循环。

Conclusion: 通过将去噪优化与下游任务效用直接关联，并引入漂移感知门控机制，所提方法在漂移环境下能更好地恢复多尺度瞬态负载突发和估计运营所需容量C95，克服了传统静态去噪方法的局限性。

Original Abstract: Traffic-utilisation measurements for network monitoring are corrupted by additive noise and statistical drift: time-dependent change in the signal's mean, variance, distributional shape, or tail behaviour. Static wavelet denoising, calibrated under stationary independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) Gaussian assumptions, becomes mismatched under drift and, at moderate-to-high signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), over-suppresses useful structure and degrades monitoring decisions. We propose a drift-aware framework treating adaptive wavelet denoising as a preprocessing layer optimised for two tasks: anomaly detection, recovering the multi-scale transient load bursts that noise and drift obscure, and capacity estimation, recovering the operational required capacity $C_{95}$ (95th percentile of utilisation). Because localised bursts are multi-scale structure a wavelet preserves but a low-pass filter removes, detection discriminates denoiser families. A four-detector gate (Page-Hinkley, variance-ratio, Jensen-Shannon, Anderson-Darling) determines when to invoke a learned policy, and a Proximal Policy Optimization agent selects a per-window wavelet configuration over a mixed discrete-continuous action space. Unlike prior work, the reward is downstream task utility, not reconstruction fidelity. The denoiser is benchmarked, per drift type and input SNR, against a low-pass moving-average filter, VisuShrink, SureShrink, BayesShrink, and a Wiener filter. Defining the anomaly target on the clean signal and the drift gate on the corruption keeps both stages non-circular.
