中文摘要翻译: 网络监测中的流量测量受到加性噪声和统计漂移（即信号均值、方差、分布形状或尾部行为的时变变化）的污染。在平稳独立同分布高斯假设下校准的静态小波去噪方法在漂移存在时会失配，并且在中等至高信噪比下会过度抑制有用结构，从而降低监测决策的质量。本文提出一种漂移感知框架，将自适应小波去噪作为预处理层，针对两项任务进行优化：异常检测，恢复被噪声和漂移掩盖的多尺度瞬态负载突发；以及容量估计，恢复运营所需容量C95（利用率的第95百分位数）。由于局部化的突发是小波能够保留而低通滤波器会移除的多尺度结构，异常检测能够区分不同去噪器族。一个四检测器门控机制（Page-Hinkley检验、方差比检验、Jensen-Shannon散度、Anderson-Darling检验）决定何时调用学习策略，Proximal Policy Optimization智能体在混合离散-连续动作空间中为每个窗口选择小波配置。与先前工作不同，奖励函数基于下游任务效用而非重构保真度。针对每种漂移类型和输入信噪比，将该去噪器与低通移动平均滤波器、VisuShrink、SureShrink、BayesShrink和Wiener滤波器进行基准对比。将异常目标定义在干净信号上，漂移门控定义在污染信号上，确保两个阶段保持非循环性。

</details>


### [173] [SNR-Dependent Mismatched Filtering for Bistatic OFDM Ranging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20012)
*Ying Zhang,Fan Liu,Yifeng Xiong,Jie Yang,Xinyi Wang,Shi Jin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了采用OFDM的双基地ISAC系统在存在解调误差时的测距性能，推导了BPSK、QPSK和方形QAM调制下失配滤波响应中期望旁瓣电平与平均主瓣电平的闭式表达式，揭示了SNR对延迟域旁瓣结构的定量影响。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在双基地OFDM-ISAC系统中，接收机利用本地解调的符号序列进行相关测距，但由于解调误差的存在，测距处理器工作于失配滤波而非理想匹配滤波状态，导致延迟域相关响应的旁瓣结构依赖于信噪比（SNR）。现有文献缺乏对这种SNR驱动的符号判决误差如何定量重塑延迟域旁瓣行为的理论分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对频率平坦衰落信道，推导了BPSK、QPSK及一般方形QAM星座下失配测距响应的期望旁瓣电平（ESL）和平均主瓣电平的闭式表达式，从理论上定量刻画了SNR驱动的符号判决误差对延迟域旁瓣行为的影响。

Result: 给出了不同调制方式下期望旁瓣电平和平均主瓣电平的解析表达式，揭示了SNR对旁瓣结构的缩放行为；仿真结果验证了理论推导的准确性。

Conclusion: 本文为双基地OFDM-ISAC系统中SNR依赖的测距性能缩放行为提供了理论分析工具，推导的闭式结果能够准确刻画解调误差导致的失配滤波对延迟域响应的影响，仿真验证了分析的正确性。

Original Abstract: This paper investigates the ranging performance of a bistatic integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) system employing orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM), in which an ISAC transmitter emits a communication waveform carrying random data symbols, and a separate receiver performs ranging by correlating the received signal with a locally demodulated symbol sequence. Owing to inevitable demodulation errors, the ranging processor operates under mismatched filtering rather than ideal matched filtering, resulting in a delay-domain correlation response whose sidelobe structure explicitly depends on the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). Focusing on frequency-flat fading channels, we derive closed-form expressions for the expected sidelobe level (ESL) and the average mainlobe level of the resulting mismatched ranging response for BPSK, QPSK, and general square QAM constellations. The analysis quantitatively characterizes how SNR-driven symbol decision errors reshape the delay-domain sidelobe behavior, thereby providing analytical insight into the SNR-dependent scaling behavior of ranging performance in bistatic OFDM-based ISAC systems. Simulation results validate the theoretical derivations and confirm the accuracy of the proposed analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了采用正交频分复用（OFDM）的双基地集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统的测距性能。在该系统中，ISAC发射机发射携带随机数据符号的通信波形，独立的接收机通过将接收信号与本地解调的符号序列进行相关处理来实现测距。由于不可避免的解调误差，测距处理器工作在失配滤波而非理想匹配滤波状态下，导致延迟域相关响应的旁瓣结构显式依赖于信噪比（SNR）。针对频率平坦衰落信道，我们推导了BPSK、QPSK及一般方形QAM星座下失配测距响应的期望旁瓣电平（ESL）和平均主瓣电平的闭式表达式。该分析定量刻画了SNR驱动的符号判决误差如何重塑延迟域旁瓣行为，从而为双基地OFDM-ISAC系统中SNR依赖的测距性能缩放行为提供了理论分析。仿真结果验证了理论推导并确认了所提分析的准确性。

</details>


### [174] [Uplink Positioning for PASS in Multipath Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20069)
*Yaoyu Zhang,Xin Sun,Tianwei Hou,Anna Li,Yuanwei Liu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对捏合天线系统（PASS）在多径环境下的上行链路定位问题，提出了基于矩阵束（MP）和低复杂度Rank-1的两种测距算法，并结合两阶段加权非线性最小二乘（WNLS）定位算法实现三维用户位置估计，理论推导了闭式测距方差和位置误差界（PEB），数值实验验证了所提算法的性能权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: PASS通过将捏合天线（PA）激活或放置在用户附近来增强无线传播，因此准确的上行链路定位对于高效通信至关重要。

Method: 1) 基于矩阵束（MP）的测距算法：利用Hankel矩阵的移位不变性从非视距分量中分离视距（LoS）分量，实现精确距离估计。2) 低复杂度Rank-1测距算法：通过截断奇异值分解直接分离主导LoS时延，避免矩阵求逆。3) 两阶段加权非线性最小二乘（WNLS）定位算法估计三维用户位置。4) 理论分析：推导闭式测距方差和位置误差界（PEB）。

Result: i) MP算法比Rank-1算法精度更高、鲁棒性更强，但Rank-1算法计算复杂度更低。ii) MP算法的定位误差与推导的PEB趋势一致，而Rank-1算法因多径偏差存在误差底限。iii) MP算法的定位精度随子载波数增加而提升。

Conclusion: MP算法在精度和鲁棒性上优于Rank-1算法，适合对定位精度要求高的场景；Rank-1算法计算复杂度低，适合资源受限场景；增加子载波数可提升MP算法定位性能。

Original Abstract: Pinching-antenna systems (PASS) enhance wireless propagation by activating or placing pinching antennas (PAs) near users. Therefore, accurate uplink positioning is essential for efficient communication. In this paper, an uplink multi-carrier positioning framework is established for PASS in multipath environments. Matrix pencil (MP)-based and low-complexity Rank-1 ranging algorithms are proposed to estimate the distances between the PAs and the user. For the MP-based ranging algorithm, the line-of-sight (LoS) component is separated from non-line-of-sight components by exploiting the shift-invariance property of the Hankel matrix, thereby enabling accurate distance estimation. For the Rank-1 ranging algorithm, the dominant LoS delay is directly isolated through truncated singular value decomposition, thereby avoiding matrix inversions. Subsequently, a two-stage weighted nonlinear least-squares (WNLS) positioning algorithm is designed to estimate the three-dimensional user position. To gain further insights, a comprehensive theoretical performance analysis of the proposed ranging and positioning algorithms is conducted. The closed-form ranging variances and position error bound (PEB) are derived to reveal the error propagation mechanism. Numerical results demonstrate that: i) The MP-based algorithm achieves higher accuracy and robustness than the Rank-1-based algorithm, while the Rank-1-based algorithm has lower computational complexity. ii) The positioning error of the MP-based algorithm follows the same trend as the derived PEB, whereas the Rank-1 algorithm exhibits an error floor due to multipath bias. iii) The positioning accuracy of the MP algorithm improves as the number of subcarriers increases.

中文摘要翻译: 捏合天线系统（PASS）通过将捏合天线（PA）激活或放置在用户附近来增强无线传播。因此，准确的上行链路定位对于高效通信至关重要。本文针对多径环境下的PASS建立了一个上行多载波定位框架。提出了基于矩阵束（MP）和低复杂度Rank-1的测距算法，用于估计PA与用户之间的距离。对于MP测距算法，利用Hankel矩阵的移位不变性将视距（LoS）分量与非视距分量分离，从而实现精确的距离估计。对于Rank-1测距算法，通过截断奇异值分解直接隔离主导LoS时延，从而避免矩阵求逆。随后，设计了一种两阶段加权非线性最小二乘（WNLS）定位算法来估计三维用户位置。为获得更深入的见解，对所提出的测距和定位算法进行了全面的理论性能分析。推导了闭式测距方差和位置误差界（PEB），以揭示误差传播机制。数值结果表明：i) MP算法比Rank-1算法具有更高的精度和鲁棒性，而Rank-1算法计算复杂度更低。ii) MP算法的定位误差与推导的PEB变化趋势一致，而Rank-1算法因多径偏差存在误差底限。iii) MP算法的定位精度随子载波数增加而提升。

</details>


### [175] [Temporal Broadening-Aware Multiplexing for Joint Sensing and Communication in THz Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20074)
*Saira Rafique,Ahmed Naeem,Huseyin Arslan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种太赫兹（THz）时域展宽效应（TBE）感知的复用框架，利用距离依赖的TBE特性，将原本为远端感知接收机预留的保护间隔机会性地复用给近端通信用户，实现了通感一体化脉冲的无干扰共存，显著降低了延迟并提升了整体性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: THz频段因分子吸收（MoA）导致时域展宽效应（TBE），使接收回波时域扩散，造成距离分辨率下降且需要长保护间隔避免脉冲重叠，但长保护间隔引入延迟和时域利用低效问题，亟需一种能克服TBE限制、实现通感一体化高效共存的方法。

Method: 提出一种TBE感知的复用框架：利用TBE的距离依赖性，将原本为远端感知接收机最坏情况预留的保护间隔，机会性地嵌入低功率单载波通信脉冲给近端用户。近端用户因TBE有限，通感脉冲保持在其时隙内不相互干扰；远端感知接收机的通信脉冲因展宽和衰减可忽略，无需连续干扰消除（SIC）。

Result: 仿真结果显示，相比功率域非正交多址（PD-NOMA）和固定保护间隔方案，所提方案在误码率、感知精度和延迟性能方面均更优，在高流量下可实现高达66.5%的延迟降低。

Conclusion: 利用TBE距离依赖特性设计保护间隔复用策略，可有效消除通感一体化中的干扰问题，显著降低延迟并提升系统性能，为6G太赫兹通感一体化网络提供了一种高效可行的方案。

Original Abstract: High-resolution wireless sensing has become an integral component of futuristic 6G networks alongside high-rate communication. Terahertz (THz) band enables both functions through its extremely large bandwidth, providing sub-centimeter level sensing precision and multi-gigabit data rates. However, THz propagation suffers from severe channel impairments such as molecular absorption (MoA) and the resulting temporal broadening effect (TBE). For sensing, TBE causes temporal spreading of received echoes, leading to degraded range resolution and necessitating long guard intervals between consecutive sensing pulses to avoid overlap. These guards, while necessary for far sensing receiver (Rxsens), cause latency and inefficient temporal use. To overcome this limitation, this paper proposes a TBE-aware multiplexing framework that exploits the distance-dependent nature of TBE to enable interference-free coexistence of sensing and communication (S&C) pulses. A guard interval preallocated for the worst-case TBE at far Rxsens is opportunistically reused to embed a low-power single-carrier communication pulse for a nearby user experiencing minimal broadening. Limited TBE confines S&C pulses within their designated slots at short distances, while the broadened and attenuated communication pulse at the distant Rxsens becomes negligible, eliminating the need for successive interference cancellation (SIC). Simulation results reveal that compared with power-domain non-orthogonal multiple access (PD-NOMA) and fixed-guard alternative, the proposed scheme achieves superior bit-error rate, sensing accuracy, and latency performance, with up to 66.5% latency reduction under heavy traffic.

中文摘要翻译: 高分辨率无线感知已成为未来6G网络中与高速通信并行的关键组成部分。太赫兹（THz）频段凭借其超大带宽同时支持这两种功能，提供了亚厘米级的感知精度和多吉比特的数据速率。然而，太赫兹传播受到严重的信道损伤，如分子吸收（MoA）及其导致的时域展宽效应（TBE）。在感知方面，TBE造成接收回波的时域扩散，导致距离分辨率下降，并需要在连续感知脉冲之间设置长保护间隔以避免重叠。这些保护间隔虽然对远端感知接收机（Rxsens）是必要的，但会引入延迟并导致时域利用效率低下。为克服这一限制，本文提出一种TBE感知的复用框架，利用TBE的距离依赖性特性，实现感知与通信（S&C）脉冲的无干扰共存。预留用于远端Rxsens最坏情况TBE的保护间隔被机会性地复用，嵌入一个低功率单载波通信脉冲给一个受展宽影响极小的近端用户。在短距离下，受限的TBE将S&C脉冲限制在各自的时隙内；而在远端Rxsens处，展宽并衰减后的通信脉冲影响可忽略，从而无需连续干扰消除（SIC）。仿真结果表明，与功率域非正交多址（PD-NOMA）和固定保护间隔方案相比，所提方案在误码率、感知精度和延迟性能上均更优，在高流量下可实现高达66.5%的延迟降低。

</details>


### [176] [WiFi Sensing via Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20103)
*Heping Wang,Zhongqin Wang,J. Andrew Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: ReWiS提出了一种面向WiFi感知的储层计算框架，通过将CSI转化为结构化微多普勒流、使用图耦合储层编码，并在部署后仅微调轻量级读出来适应新域，在低计算预算下实现了有竞争力的跨域感知性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 实际WiFi感知面临时钟异步链路、跨域变化以及部署后更新等挑战，而AP、路由器和ESP类嵌入式物联网平台的计算预算有限。现有深度学习方法虽然性能高但优化成本大，需要一种轻量级、可适应新域的解决方案。

Method: ReWiS包含三个关键设计：(1) 将CSI转化为包含公共运动、天线特定和差分运动线索的结构化微多普勒流；(2) 使用图耦合储层（graph-coupled reservoir）对这些流进行编码，储层固定不变；(3) 部署后仅对轻量级读出（仅0.72M可训练参数）进行微调以适应新域，储层部分保持冻结。

Result: 在大型WiFi感知基准上，ReWiS达到89.2%的域内宏F1和82.0%的平均跨域宏F1，仅需0.72M可训练读出参数；轻量级部署后适应后提升至88.5%；与近期深度基线（87.5%-89.2%平均跨域宏F1）相比具有竞争力，同时优化成本和CPU延迟更低。

Conclusion: ReWiS提供了一种实用的基于储层计算的WiFi感知设计方案，在低计算预算下实现了有竞争力的跨域感知性能，并具有在低功耗硬件上实现的进一步潜力。

Original Abstract: Practical WiFi sensing must handle clock-asynchronous links, cross-domain variation, and post-deployment updating under the limited compute budget of access point (AP), router, and embedded Internet-of-Things platforms such as ESP-class devices. Reservoir computing (RC) is attractive in this setting because its temporal encoder can remain fixed while only a lightweight readout needs to be optimized and updated. To address these deployment challenges under tight compute budgets, we present ReWiS, a WiFi-sensing-oriented reservoir framework that transforms channel state information (CSI) into structured micro-Doppler streams with common, antenna-specific, and differential motion cues, encodes them with a graph-coupled reservoir, and adapts to a new domain after deployment by freezing the reservoir and fine-tuning only a compact readout with a few labeled target samples. On a large-scale WiFi sensing benchmark, ReWiS achieves 89.2% in-domain macro-F1 and 82.0% mean cross-domain macro-F1 with only a 0.72M trainable readout, improves to 88.5\% after lightweight post-deployment adaptation, and remains competitive with recent deep baselines evaluated under the same protocol, which achieve 87.5%-89.2% mean cross-domain macro-F1, while requiring lower optimization cost and lower CPU latency. These results indicate that ReWiS provides a practical reservoir-based design for deployable WiFi sensing, with further potential for low-power hardware realization.

中文摘要翻译: 实际的WiFi感知必须处理时钟异步链路、跨域变化以及部署后更新等问题，同时受限于接入点（AP）、路由器和ESP类嵌入式物联网平台等设备的有限计算预算。储层计算（RC）在此场景中具有吸引力，因为其时间编码器可以保持固定，仅需优化和更新一个轻量级的读出层。为在严格计算预算下应对这些部署挑战，我们提出ReWiS——一个面向WiFi感知的储层框架。该框架将信道状态信息（CSI）转化为包含公共运动、天线特定和差分运动线索的结构化微多普勒流，使用图耦合储层对其进行编码，并在部署后通过冻结储层、仅用少量带标签的目标样本微调紧凑的读出层来适应新域。在大型WiFi感知基准上，ReWiS实现了89.2%的域内宏F1和82.0%的平均跨域宏F1，仅需0.72M可训练读出参数；经过轻量级的部署后适应后，性能提升至88.5%，并且在相同评估协议下与近期深度基线（平均跨域宏F1为87.5%-89.2%）相比具有竞争力，同时所需优化成本和CPU延迟更低。这些结果表明，ReWiS为可部署的WiFi感知提供了一种实用的基于储层的设计方案，并在低功耗硬件实现方面具有进一步潜力。

</details>


### [177] [Ambiguity-Resolved Micro-Doppler Construction for Asynchronous Bistatic Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20108)
*Yiyi Xu,Zhongqin Wang,Kai Wu,J. Andrew Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对双基地ISAC系统中收发时钟异步导致的CSI相位随机波动问题，提出一种微多普勒构建框架，通过循环差分和延迟-AoA-多普勒流水线消除镜像模糊和残余分量，在WiFi手势数据集上取得90.1%-98.2%的准确率，较基线平均提升约18个百分点。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 双基地ISAC部署中收发时钟不同步会导致CSI相位随机波动，现有CSI-ratio消毒方法引入非线性失真，限制了多目标可扩展性；跨天线共轭乘法（CACC）虽保持线性结构，但存在镜像模糊和二阶残余分量，污染了运动诱导的多普勒特征。需要一种能同时消除模糊和抑制残余分量的方法。

Method: 提出微多普勒构建框架：1）循环差分首次衰减主导镜像分量；2）利用残余项出现在差分延迟坐标且缺乏有序AoA导向结构的特点，设计轻量级延迟-AoA-多普勒流水线，在不相干积累残余分量的情况下隔离期望的动力学响应；3）将滤波后的响应聚合为更高信噪比的微多普勒表示。

Result: 在大规模WiFi手势数据集上，该表示在四种域因子上泛化良好，达到90.1%-98.2%的准确率，平均比代表性基线高出约18个百分点。消融研究证实了残余抑制和多维滤波的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的微多普勒构建框架有效解决了双基地ISAC中时钟异步导致的镜像模糊和残余分量问题，实现了高精度的无线感知，且具有良好的跨域泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) can turn wireless networks into pervasive sensing platforms, but bistatic deployments are impaired by transceiver clock asynchrony, which induces random phase fluctuations in channel state information (CSI). CSI-ratio sanitization introduces nonlinear distortion that limits multi-target scalability and complicates delay- and angle-of-arrival (AoA)-domain processing. Cross-antenna conjugate multiplication (CACC) preserves a linear structure, but leaves mirror ambiguity and second-order by-products that corrupt motion-induced Doppler signatures. We develop a micro-Doppler construction framework that resolves the ambiguity and suppresses these residual by-products. Cyclic differencing first attenuates the dominant mirror component. We then exploit the facts that residual terms occur at differenced delay coordinates and lack an ordered AoA steering structure, designing a lightweight delay-AoA-Doppler pipeline that isolates the desired kinematic response without coherently accumulating residuals at target bins. The filtered responses are aggregated into a higher-SNR micro-Doppler representation. Ablation studies confirm the value of residual suppression and multidimensional filtering. On a large-scale WiFi gesture dataset, the proposed representation generalizes across four domain factors, attaining 90.1%-98.2% accuracy and outperforming representative baselines by about 18 percentage points on average.

中文摘要翻译: 集成感知与通信（ISAC）可将无线网络转变为普适感知平台，但双基地部署受收发器时钟异步的影响，这会在信道状态信息（CSI）中引入随机相位波动。CSI-ratio消毒方法引入了非线性失真，限制了多目标可扩展性，并导致延迟和到达角（AoA）域处理复杂化。跨天线共轭乘法（CACC）保留了线性结构，但留下了镜像模糊和二阶残余分量，污染了运动诱导的多普勒特征。我们开发了一个微多普勒构建框架，该框架解决了模糊问题并抑制了这些残余分量。循环差分首先衰减了主导镜像分量。然后，我们利用残余项出现在差分延迟坐标且缺乏有序AoA导向结构的事实，设计了一个轻量级的延迟-AoA-多普勒流水线，在不相干积累目标单元残余分量的情况下隔离期望的动力学响应。滤波后的响应被聚合为更高信噪比的微多普勒表示。消融研究证实了残余抑制和多维滤波的价值。在大规模WiFi手势数据集上，所提出的表示在四种域因子上泛化良好，达到90.1%-98.2%的准确率，平均比代表性基线高出约18个百分点。

</details>


### [178] [Swarm and Evolutionary Computation for Near-Field Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20139)
*Parisa Ramezani,Seyed Jalaleddin Mousavirad,Mattias O'Nils,Emil Björnson*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文聚焦于群智与演化计算（SEC）在近场定位中的应用，指出这类方法虽在文献中关注较少，但因其能有效处理近场定位的复杂优化问题，相较于传统网格子空间方法和深度学习方法具有独特优势。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随着高频段和超大孔径阵列的发展，近场区域扩大，大量信源进入近场范围，使得天线阵列不仅能定位角度还能定位距离。然而现有定位方法各有局限性，阻碍了实际部署。作者注意到群智与演化计算（SEC）在近场定位中潜力巨大但研究不足，因此聚焦于该类方法。

Method: 群智与演化计算（SEC）方法，该类方法适用于近场定位中复杂的优化景观。

Result: SEC方法在准确性和高效性方面优于传统的基于网格的子空间方法和深度学习方法。

Conclusion: 群智与演化计算（SEC）方法在近场定位中具有强潜力，能够克服传统方法的局限性，值得进一步研究和实际部署。

Original Abstract: Near-field localization has attracted significant attention in recent years due to the move toward higher frequencies and extremely large aperture arrays, which expand the near-field region and bring many sources into it. This implies that antenna arrays can be used to localize not only in angle but also in range. Although a wide range of localization methods has been developed, each comes with limitations that may hinder practical deployment. This article focuses on a class of techniques that has received relatively little attention in the prior literature despite its strong potential for accurate and efficient location estimation: swarm and evolutionary computation (SEC). These methods are well-suited to the complex optimization landscape of near-field localization and can offer important advantages over conventional approaches such as grid-based subspace methods and deep learning approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，随着向更高频段和超大孔径阵列的迈进，近场区域得以扩大并使大量信源进入其中，近场定位因此受到了广泛关注。这意味着天线阵列不仅可以用于角度定位，还可以用于距离定位。尽管已有大量的定位方法被开发出来，但每种方法都存在可能阻碍其实际部署的局限性。本文聚焦于一类在先前文献中关注较少、但在准确高效的位置估计方面具有强大潜力的技术：群智与演化计算。这类方法非常适合近场定位的复杂优化景观，并且相较于传统的基于网格的子空间方法和深度学习方法，能够提供重要的优势。

</details>


### [179] [ISAC-Assisted Channel Knowledge Map Generation for Physical Layer Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20176)
*Luca Bonaventura,Edoardo Gardin,Alessia Barison,Francesco Ardizzon,Stefano Tomasin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出利用通感一体化（ISAC）技术重建多接收器周围物体布局，并应用射线追踪构建信道知识地图（CKM），进而实现物理层认证（PLA），以对抗来自不同位置的欺骗攻击。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 利用ISAC技术获取的环境信息来构建信道知识地图，从而为物理层认证提供高精度的信道参考，以检测和抵御来自不同位置的假冒攻击。

Method: 首先利用ISAC技术重建多接收器周围物体布局；然后对重建环境应用射线追踪，推断不同发射机位置的传播信道，构建信道知识地图（CKM）；最后将接收信号估计的信道与CKM数据进行比对，实现物理层认证。

Result: 评估了ISAC导致的CKM重建误差和接收端信道估计噪声对认证性能（虚警概率和漏检概率）的影响，并利用文献中的ISAC数据集验证了所提方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于ISAC和CKM的物理层认证方法能够有效利用环境信息实现发射机位置验证，对抗冒充攻击，且性能受CKM重建误差和信道估计噪声的影响可被量化评估。

Original Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) enables the acquisition of environmental information by leveraging wireless signals transmitted for communication purposes. In this paper, we utilize this capability to reconstruct the layout of objects surrounding multiple receivers. Ray tracing is then applied to the reconstructed environment to infer the propagation channels for various transmitter positions, thereby constructing a channel knowledge map (CKM). The CKM is then used to verify the position of a legitimate transmitter, authenticating it against an adversarial device attempting to impersonate it from a different location. This physical layer authentication (PLA) mechanism utilizes the approximate known position of the legitimate transmitter, obtained, for instance, from the network as in cross-layer authentication, to compare the channel estimated from the received signal with the corresponding CKM data. We evaluate the impact on the PLA performance of both ISAC-induced CKM reconstruction errors and receiver-side channel estimation noise, in terms of false alarm and missed detection probabilities. Finally, the proposed approach is validated using an ISAC dataset from the literature.

中文摘要翻译: 通感一体化（ISAC）技术能够通过利用用于通信的无线信号来获取环境信息。本文利用这一能力重建多接收器周围物体的布局。然后对重建环境应用射线追踪，推断不同发射机位置的传播信道，从而构建信道知识地图（CKM）。CKM随后被用于验证合法发射机的位置，以对抗试图从不同位置进行冒充的恶意设备。该物理层认证（PLA）机制利用合法发射机的大致已知位置（例如，像跨层认证那样从网络获取），将接收信号估计的信道与相应的CKM数据进行比较。我们从虚警概率和漏检概率的角度，评估了ISAC导致的CKM重建误差和接收端信道估计噪声对PLA性能的影响。最后，利用文献中的ISAC数据集对所提方法进行了验证。

</details>


### [180] [JEPA-CFM: A Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture-based Channel Foundation Model for Robust Fluid Antenna Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20202)
*Yuan Gao,Yiming Liu,Jun Jiang,Jianbo Du,Shunqing Zhang,Xiaoli Chu,Kai-Kit Wong*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的信道基础模型（JEPA-CFM），用于解决流体天线系统（FAS）中信道状态信息获取、信道外推和无线定位三大挑战。该模型通过在高维特征空间中预测抽象表示而非逐像素重建原始CSI，结合掩码自编码器重建损失、JEPA潜在预测损失和切片各向同性高斯正则化三项预训练目标，在真实DeepMIMO城市场景下，信道外推和无线定位性能显著优于传统掩码自编码器基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 流体天线系统（FAS）虽能利用丰富空间分集，但受限于有限孔径内的强空间相关性和观测数据的稀疏性，实时获取信道状态信息（CSI）、外推未测量天线端口信道值以及精确确定用户位置等任务面临重大挑战。

Method: 提出基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的信道基础模型（JEPA-CFM）。模型采用JEPA学习通用表征，提取掩码或未观测信道段的高层潜在嵌入。预训练目标结合三项损失：标准掩码自编码器重建损失、JEPA潜在预测损失以及切片各向同性高斯正则化（SIGReg）项。预训练后冻结编码器，附加轻量级任务头：用于信道外推的解码器和用于无线定位的全局平均池化+MLP回归头。

Result: 在真实DeepMIMO城市场景仿真中，JEPA-CFM在信道外推和无线定位任务上显著优于传统掩码自编码器基线方法。

Conclusion: JEPA-CFM通过聚焦于紧凑特征空间中的抽象结构预测而非原始CSI系数的逐像素重建，结合三项互补损失函数防止表示崩塌并增强鲁棒性，有效解决了FAS中强空间相关性和稀疏观测下的信道建模与定位难题。

Original Abstract: Fluid antenna systems (FAS) have emerged as a promising technology for sixth-generation (6G) wireless networks. By allowing antenna elements to move freely within a compact region, FAS can exploit rich spatial diversity without additional hardware. However, acquiring real-time channel state information (CSI), extrapolating channel values to unmeasured antenna ports, and determining accurate user positions remain major obstacles. These challenges stem mainly from strong spatial correlations within the limited aperture and the scarcity of observable data. To overcome these limitations, this paper introduces joint embedding predictive architecture (JEPA)-based channel foundation model (CFM) specifically designed for FAS. The model adopts JEPA to learn versatile representations by extracting high-level latent embeddings of masked or unobserved channel segments. Unlike conventional approaches that attempt pixel-by-pixel reconstruction of raw CSI coefficients, JEPA-CFM focuses on predicting abstract structures in a compact feature space. The pre-training objective combines three complementary loss terms: the standard masked autoencoder reconstruction loss, the JEPA latent prediction loss, and a sliced isotropic Gaussian regularization (SIGReg) term. Together, these components prevent representation collapse and significantly enhance robustness under severe spatial correlation and highly sparse observations. After pre-training, the encoder is frozen, and lightweight task-specific heads are attached: a decoder for channel extrapolation and a global average pooling layer followed by a multi-layer perceptron regression head for wireless positioning. Extensive simulations in the realistic DeepMIMO urban scenario demonstrate that JEPA-CFM substantially outperforms the conventional masked autoencoder baseline in channel extrapolation and wireless positioning.

中文摘要翻译: 流体天线系统（FAS）已成为第六代（6G）无线网络中的一项有前景的技术。通过允许天线元件在紧凑区域内自由移动，FAS无需额外硬件即可利用丰富的空间分集。然而，获取实时信道状态信息（CSI）、外推未测量天线端口的信道值以及确定精确的用户位置仍然是主要障碍。这些挑战主要源于有限孔径内的强空间相关性以及可观测数据的稀缺性。为克服这些限制，本文引入了专门为FAS设计的基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的信道基础模型（CFM）。该模型采用JEPA，通过提取掩码或未观测信道段的高层潜在嵌入来学习通用表征。与尝试逐像素重建原始CSI系数的传统方法不同，JEPA-CFM专注于在紧凑特征空间中预测抽象结构。预训练目标结合了三个互补的损失项：标准掩码自编码器重建损失、JEPA潜在预测损失以及切片各向同性高斯正则化（SIGReg）项。这些组件共同防止了表示崩塌，并在强空间相关性和高度稀疏观测下显著增强了鲁棒性。预训练后，编码器被冻结，并附加轻量级任务头：用于信道外推的解码器，以及用于无线定位的全局平均池化层后接多层感知机回归头。在真实DeepMIMO城市场景中的大量仿真表明，JEPA-CFM在信道外推和无线定位方面显著优于传统掩码自编码器基线。

</details>


### [181] [Joint Chirp Parameter Selection and Low-Complexity MMSE Receiver Design for AFDM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20227)
*Ruiyuan Mao,Qu Luo,Jianguo Li,Fabien Héliot,Tianqi Mao,Pei Xiao,Hee Wook Kim,Kai Yang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对仿射频率分复用（AFDM）系统，提出了一种联合优化啁啾参数选择和低复杂度MMSE接收机设计的方法，包括快速循环-对角聚合（FCDA）算法用于高效c1选择、低复杂度带状MMSE（LC-BMMSE）接收机以及层次搜索联合优化算法，在降低复杂度的同时实现了优越的性能-复杂度权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在高移动性通信场景下，AFDM波形面临双选择性信道，其性能严重依赖啁啾参数的最优选择，且传统接收机复杂度较高。现有方法在啁啾参数选择和低复杂度接收机设计方面缺乏联合优化，难以在低计算复杂度下获得满意的误码率性能。

Method: 1) 利用DAFT的对角与循环结构推导简化BER度量；2) 基于该度量提出FCDA算法实现高效c1选择；3) 通过路径级结构化稀疏构建循环带状ECM，并采用带状Cholesky分解避免直接矩阵求逆，设计LC-BMMSE接收机；4) 提出HS-JCPS算法，在给定复杂度约束下联合优化啁啾参数与稀疏化模式。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提FCDA算法相比基于BER的准则，将最优c1的搜索时间降低了一个数量级；HS-JCPS算法结合LC-BMMSE接收机能够识别近最优c1，并获得优越的性能-复杂度权衡。

Conclusion: 通过利用AFDM有效信道矩阵的结构特性，联合优化啁啾参数选择与接收机设计，能够在显著降低计算复杂度的同时保持接近最优的误码率性能，为高移动性通信提供了高效的波形方案。

Original Abstract: Affine frequency division multiplexing (AFDM) has emerged as a promising waveform against doubly selective channels under high-mobility communication scenarios. Optimal chirp parameter selection and reduced-complexity receiver design in AFDM are essential for achieving satisfactory bit error rate (BER) performance with low computational complexity. In this paper, we investigate the joint optimization of chirp-parameter selection and low-complexity minimum mean square error (MMSE)-based receiver design by exploiting the structural characteristics of the AFDM effective channel matrix (ECM). First, a simplified BER performance metric is derived by leveraging the diagonal and circulant structure of the discrete affine Fourier transformation (DAFT), based on which a fast circulant-diagonal aggregation (FCDA) algorithm is developed for efficient $c_1$ selection. Then, a low-complexity banded MMSE (LC-BMMSE) receiver is developed by constructing a cyclic-banded ECM through path-wise structured sparsification, where banded Cholesky factorization is employed to avoid direct matrix inversion. Building upon the proposed BER metric and the LC-BMMSE receiver, a hierarchical-search-based joint chirp parameter and structured sparsification (HS-JCPS) algorithm is further proposed to jointly optimize the chirp parameter and sparsification pattern under a given complexity constraint. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed FCDA reduces the search time for the optimal $c_1$ by an order of magnitude compared with using a BER-based criterion. Moreover, the proposed HS-JCPS algorithm with the LC-BMMSE receiver can identify a near-optimal $c_1$, while attaining a superior performance-complexity tradeoff.

中文摘要翻译: 仿射频率分复用（AFDM）已成为高移动性通信场景中对抗双选择性信道的一种有前景的波形。在AFDM中，最优啁啾参数选择和低复杂度接收机设计对于以低计算复杂度获得满意的误码率（BER）性能至关重要。本文通过利用AFDM有效信道矩阵（ECM）的结构特性，研究了啁啾参数选择与低复杂度最小均方误差（MMSE）接收机设计的联合优化。首先，利用离散仿射傅里叶变换（DAFT）的对角与循环结构推导出一种简化的BER性能度量，并基于此提出了一种快速循环-对角聚合（FCDA）算法，用于高效的c1选择。然后，通过路径级结构化稀疏构建循环带状ECM，并采用带状Cholesky分解避免直接矩阵求逆，开发了一种低复杂度带状MMSE（LC-BMMSE）接收机。基于所提出的BER度量和LC-BMMSE接收机，进一步提出了一种基于层次搜索的联合啁啾参数与结构化稀疏化（HS-JCPS）算法，在给定复杂度约束下联合优化啁啾参数和稀疏化模式。仿真结果表明，与使用基于BER的准则相比，所提出的FCDA将最优c1的搜索时间降低了一个数量级。此外，所提出的HS-JCPS算法结合LC-BMMSE接收机能够识别近最优的c1，同时获得优越的性能-复杂度权衡。

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Perceived vertical and eye level as one orientation order parameter: a closed-form account of the Li-Matin rules for egocentric space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19681)
*A. Y. Shavit*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一原则——方向序参量模型（PLUMB），证明视觉诱发的垂直知觉偏差（VPV）的三个规律（线性偏移、两线条线性组合、对称倾斜抵消）均源于方向在倍角域中的圆均值计算，无需每配置额外自由参数即可拟合Li和Matin的实验数据，并扩展到眼水平感知。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Li和Matin（2005a, 2005b）报告了视觉场方向内容影响垂直知觉的三个规律，但这些规律被视为独立发现。本文试图证明这三条规律实际上源于同一个数学原理，从而建立统一的理论框架。

Method: 提出方向序参量模型（PLUMB）：诱发的垂直知觉φ等于刺激方向在倍角域中第一圆矩参数的一半，即φ=½ arg c₁，其中c₁=∑Aⱼe^{i2γⱼ}（每条线按其二倍角计数）。该模型等价于方向结构张量的主轴或方向群体向量。角度加倍是强制性的，因为方向是director（θ≡θ+π），圆均值读出必须是倍角形式。

Result: (1) 该模型线性跟踪、组合和抵消是圆均值读出的固有特征，无需分别解释；(2) 对Li和Matin的2、3、4线条组合系数，角度上无每配置自由参数即可精确匹配，幅度来自一个质量作用长度函数；(3) 短线条和|cos2θ|强度规律（45°为零点）提供了决定性无参数检验；(4) 同一序参量在视差和和差分解下可同时解释垂直知觉和眼水平知觉，恢复Shavit等人的规则，30人试验数据确认了次加性跨场组合。

Conclusion: 视觉诱发的垂直和眼水平知觉可以用刺激侧、图像可计算的序参量统一描述，将这两个知觉现象与经典图像方向描述符联系起来，为视觉空间感知提供了一个简洁的数学框架。

Original Abstract: The visually perceived vertical is biased by the orientation content of the visual field. Li and Matin (2005a, 2005b) reported three regularities of this induced vertical (VPV): a roll-tilted peripheral line shifts it about linearly with orientation; two lines combine about linearly; and symmetric tilts cancel. We show all three follow from one principle. The induced vertical is half the argument of the first circular moment of stimulus orientation in the doubled-angle domain, $φ=\frac{1}{2}\arg c_1$ with $c_1=\sum_j A_j e^{i2γ_j}$ (each line counted at twice its angle). Equivalently it is the principal axis of the orientation structure tensor, or an orientation population vector; we call it the orientation order-parameter model (PLUMB). The angle-doubling is forced: orientation is a director ($θ\equivθ+π$), so a circular-mean readout must be the doubled-angle one, and linear tracking, combination, and cancellation are signatures of any such readout, not separate findings. Li and Matin's 2-, 3-, and 4-line combination coefficients are then closely matched with no per-configuration free parameters in the angles; the magnitudes come from one mass-action length function (their Fig. 5, three constants). The data refute complete summation but do not separate the model from simple averaging for long lines; the decisive parameter-free tests are the short-line regime and a $|\cos 2θ|$ strength law with a null at $45^\circ$. Read as a sum and a difference across the two hemifields, the same order parameter yields perceived vertical and eye level, recovering the reversed rules of Shavit, Li and Matin (2013); their 30-observer trial data confirm the predicted sub-additive cross-field combination. The account is stimulus-side and image-computable, linking induced vertical and eye level to classical image-orientation descriptors.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉感知的垂直方向会受到视野中方向内容的偏置。Li和Matin（2005a, 2005b）报告了这种诱发的垂直方向（VPV）的三个规律：滚转倾斜的周边线条使其大致呈线性偏移；两条线条近似线性组合；对称倾斜相互抵消。我们证明这三条规律源于同一个原理。诱发的垂直方向等于刺激方向在倍角域中第一圆矩参数的一半，即φ=½ arg c₁，其中c₁=∑Aⱼe^{i2γⱼ}（每条线按其两倍角计数）。等价地，该模型是方向结构张量的主轴，或方向群体向量；我们称之为方向序参量模型（PLUMB）。角度加倍是强制性的：方向是director（θ≡θ+π），因此圆均值读出必须是倍角形式；线性跟踪、组合和抵消是任何此类读出的固有特征，而非独立的发现。Li和Matin的2、3、4线条组合系数随后在角度上无需每配置自由参数即可精确匹配；幅度来自一个质量作用长度函数（他们的图5，三个常数）。数据反驳了完全求和，但未能在长线条条件下区分该模型与简单平均；决定性的无参数检验是短线条区间和|cos 2θ|强度规律（45°处为零点）。将同一序参量解读为两个半视野的和与差，可同时得到垂直知觉和眼水平知觉，恢复了Shavit、Li和Matin（2013）的倒转规则；他们的30人试验数据确认了预测的跨场次加性组合。该模型是刺激侧且图像可计算的，将诱发的垂直和眼水平知觉与经典图像方向描述符联系起来。

</details>


### [183] [State-Dependent Observation Noise Reintroduces Epistemic Value in Linear-Gaussian Active Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20306)
*Daniel Corva*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文证明在主动推理框架下，状态依赖的观测噪声（传感器精度随状态变化）是恢复线性高斯状态空间模型认知驱动的最小扰动，打破了标准卡尔曼滤波中增益序列固定不变的限制，实现了双效控制中的信息性行动。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 已有研究表明，在主动推理框架下，线性高斯状态空间模型在任何情况下都会失去认知驱动（获取信息的激励），其期望自由能的认知项变为常数，智能体退化为增益序列预先固定的卡尔曼滤波器。现有唯一能恢复认知驱动的途径是控制以乘法方式进入动力学，而观测侧边界尚未被探索。本文旨在寻找观测侧的最小扰动以恢复认知驱动。

Method: 本文提出状态依赖的观测噪声——协方差R(x)随状态x变化，表示传感器精度随距离衰减。智能体运行标准的一阶高斯滤波器，在预测均值处评估R(x)。通过将R(x)与可控的潜在均值耦合，使得后验协方差及有效卡尔曼增益依赖于动作。在观测映射满足弱秩条件且R(x)满足非退化条件下，分析认知值的非恒常性。

Result: (1) 状态依赖的观测噪声使得后验协方差和有效卡尔曼增益依赖于动作，没有固定的线性高斯滤波器能够复现该智能体；(2) 在观测映射的弱秩条件和R(x)的非退化条件下，认知值不再恒定；(3) 对于标量观测，可达的非恒常性就足以恢复认知驱动；(4) 这是Bar-Shalom-Tse双效控制在智能体维护协方差中的最小构造实例，行动不仅影响状态，还影响未来估计的质量；(5) 提供了开源库cpomdp，可从模型规范中检测不兼容性并抛出类型化错误。

Conclusion: 本文给出了高斯智能体中好奇心在观测侧的精确刻画，建立了双效控制与主动推理之间的桥梁。状态依赖观测噪声是恢复认知驱动的最小观测侧扰动，为理解主动推理中的信息性行为提供了新的视角。

Original Abstract: Recent work established that under active inference, linear-Gaussian state-space models lose their epistemic drive (any incentive to act so as to gain information) "under any circumstances". The epistemic term of the Expected Free Energy becomes constant: the agent flattens to a Kalman filter whose gain sequence is fixed in advance, regardless of action. The minimal departure that restores the drive is unknown; the only established route is control entering the dynamics multiplicatively; the observation side of this boundary is unexplored. We show that state-dependent observation noise is such a departure: a covariance R(x) that varies with the state x, representing a sensor's accuracy degrading with range. The agent runs the standard first-order Gaussian filter of this literature, R evaluated at the predicted mean. Coupling R(x) to a controllable latent mean makes the posterior covariance, and hence the effective Kalman gain, depend on the action. Consequently, no fixed linear-Gaussian filter reproduces the agent and, under a mild rank condition on the observation map and a non-degeneracy condition on R(x), epistemic value is no longer constant; for scalar observations, reachable non-constancy alone is needed. This is a minimal constructive instance of the Bar-Shalom-Tse dual effect in the agent's maintained covariance: actions now influence the quality of future estimates, not merely the state. Our library cpomdp detects the incompatibility from model specification alone and raises a typed IncompatibleLinearizationError. The theorem ships with an executable witness: exhibiting any fixed filter that reproduced the agent's beliefs would refute both theorem and witness at once. Together this offers a precise, observation-side characterisation of curiosity in a Gaussian agent, bridging dual control and active inference.

中文摘要翻译: 近期研究确立了如下事实：在主动推理框架下，线性高斯状态空间模型"在任何情况下"都会丧失其认知驱动力（即任何为获取信息而采取行动的激励）。其期望自由能的认知项变为常数：智能体退化为卡尔曼滤波器，其增益序列预先固定，与行动无关。能够恢复该驱动力的最小偏离尚属未知；唯一已知的途径是控制以乘法方式进入动力学；该边界的观测侧尚未被探索。我们表明，状态依赖的观测噪声正是这样一种偏离：一个随状态x变化的协方差R(x)，代表传感器精度随距离衰减。智能体运行该文献中标准的一阶高斯滤波器，在预测均值处评估R(x)。将R(x)与可控的潜在均值耦合，使得后验协方差以及由此产生的有效卡尔曼增益依赖于行动。因此，不存在固定的线性高斯滤波器能够复现该智能体，并且在观测映射满足弱秩条件及R(x)满足非退化条件时，认知值不再恒定；对于标量观测，只需可达的非恒常性即可。这是Bar-Shalom-Tse双效控制在智能体维护协方差中的一个最小构造性实例：行动现在不仅影响状态，还影响未来估计的质量。我们的cpomdp库能够仅从模型规范中检测不兼容性，并抛出类型化的IncompatibleLinearizationError错误。该定理附有一个可执行的见证：任何能够复现智能体信念的固定滤波器都将同时证伪该定理及其见证。综上，这为高斯智能体中的好奇心提供了一个精确的观测侧刻画，架起了双效控制与主动推理之间的桥梁。

</details>


### [184] [When to Smell in Stereo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20307)
*Sina Tootoonian,Andreas T. Schaefer*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 通过粗略计算，分析了立体嗅觉（双鼻孔）相比单鼻孔嗅觉的优势条件，发现其在气味浓度变化大、空间相关尺度大（如近地面边界层）时更有用，尤其适用于动物追踪气味轨迹时的边缘检测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究立体嗅觉（双鼻孔嗅觉）在何种条件下比单鼻孔嗅觉更具优势，从而解释动物为何演化出双鼻孔嗅觉机制。

Method: 采用“信封背面”（back of the envelope）近似计算方法，对立体嗅觉与单鼻孔嗅觉在不同条件下的有效性进行理论分析。

Result: 立体嗅觉在气味浓度相对变化较大、空气中气味空间相关长度较大（如近表面边界层）时具有优势，表明其在动物搜索表面气味边缘（如追踪气味轨迹）时更为有效。

Conclusion: 立体嗅觉在动物追踪气味轨迹、搜索表面气味边缘等场景中具有明显的优势，这解释了为何多种动物演化出双鼻孔嗅觉。

Original Abstract: Recent works have highlighted the use of dual nostril 'stereo' olfaction by a variety of animals. In this work we perform "back of the envelope" calculations to determine when stereo olfaction is useful compared to single nostril 'mono' olfaction. We find that stereo olfaction is advantageous when there are large relative changes in the odour concentration, and when the spatial length scales of correlations in air are large, such as in the boundary layer near surfaces. In other words, stereo olfaction is useful when animals are searching surfaces for olfactory edges, such as when tracking odour trails.

中文摘要翻译: 近期研究强调了多种动物使用双鼻孔“立体”嗅觉的现象。本文通过“信封背面”近似计算，确定了立体嗅觉相较于单鼻孔“单耳”嗅觉在何时更有用。我们发现，当气味浓度存在较大相对变化，且空气中气味空间相关长度较大时（例如在近表面边界层中），立体嗅觉更具优势。换言之，当动物在表面搜索气味边缘时（例如追踪气味轨迹时），立体嗅觉是有用的。

</details>


### [185] [Capturing Inner Experience At Scale: An AI Interviewer Co-Developed with the Founder of a Landmark Phenomenological Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20310)
*Jona Carmon,Clara Bersch,Charles Fernyhough,Russell T. Hurlburt,Simone Kühn*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的AI访谈系统，将描述性经验抽样（DES）方法操作化为显式、可检查的推理架构，旨在在深度和大规模之间平衡内省经验的采集。该系统通过11个质量维度评估参与者信息、维护已确立内容的保守记录、选择阶段合适的干预策略，并生成非引导性提问。它基于完整的DES转录语料库开发，并经方法创始人Hurlburt优化，运行于Introscope应用平台。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统经验抽样方法（如EMA）虽可实时捕捉经验，但受限于预设响应格式；描述性经验抽样（DES）能深入探究特定时刻，但依赖稀缺的培训访谈员导致样本量小。现有LLM系统虽能扩展定性访谈，但尚无基于内省经验研究方法的AI访谈者。因此需要开发一种既能保持DES深度又能大规模应用的AI访谈系统。

Method: 将DES方法操作化为显式推理架构：在每个对话轮次中，AI访谈者对参与者消息进行11个质量维度评估，维护已确立内容的保守记录，选择阶段合适的干预策略，并生成单一非引导性提问，始终坚持时间锚定优先于经验内容的原则。系统基于完整DES转录语料库开发，经方法创始人Hurlburt优化，部署在Introscope应用中。

Result: 论文未提供定量实验结果，但展示了AI访谈者系统本身及其配套平台Introscope的实现细节。系统目前已通过视频演示，待验证研究完成后将免费向研究人员和公众开放。

Conclusion: 该AI访谈者是首个基于已建立的内省经验研究方法论的AI访谈系统，有望通过众包采样和个人探索来大规模扩展内省经验的研究，同时保持DES方法的深度和严谨性。

Original Abstract: Subjective experience is central to psychological science, yet methods for studying it force a choice between depth and scale. Classical Experience sampling, as in ecological momentary assessments (EMA), captures experience as it occurs, but it confines participants to predetermined response formats that prescribe how experience is measured. Descriptive Experience Sampling (DES) instead investigates specific moments in depth through expert expositional interviews, but its reliance on scarce trained interviewers keeps samples small. Large language model (LLM) systems can scale qualitative interviewing. Some models operationalize established interviewing methods such as motivational interviewing, yet none is grounded in a method for apprehending inner experience. Here we present an AI interviewer that aspires to operationalize DES into an explicit, inspectable reasoning architecture. At each turn it appraises the participant's message across eleven quality dimensions, maintains a conservative account of what has been established, selects a stage-appropriate intervention, and composes a single non-leading query, always holding that temporal grounding precedes experiential content. It was derived from the full corpus of DES transcripts and refined with the method's originator Russell T. Hurlburt. To our knowledge it is the first AI interviewer grounded in an established method for studying inner experience. The interviewer runs inside Introscope, an application that delivers the beeps and conducts the interviews and a study platform that lets researchers run studies via shareable links and review the sampled experience. It is demonstrated in an accompanying video https://introscope.mpib-berlin.mpg.de/video. Pending validation studies, we will make it freely available to researchers and the public, for crowdsourced sampling and individual exploration of inner experience.

中文摘要翻译: 主观经验是心理科学的核心，然而研究它的方法在深度和规模之间面临抉择。经典经验抽样法（如生态瞬时评估EMA）能捕捉实时发生的经验，但将参与者局限于预设的响应格式，限制了经验的测量方式。描述性经验抽样法（DES）则通过专家访谈深入探究特定时刻，但其对稀缺培训访谈员的依赖导致样本量较小。大语言模型（LLM）系统可以规模化定性访谈。一些模型已实现了动机访谈等成熟访谈方法，但尚无一种方法基于内省经验的捕捉方法。本文提出了一种AI访谈者，致力于将DES操作化为一种显式、可检查的推理架构。在每个对话轮次中，它对参与者的消息进行11个质量维度的评估，维护已确立内容的保守记录，选择阶段合适的干预策略，并生成单一非引导性提问——始终坚持时间锚定优先于经验内容的原则。该系统基于完整的DES转录语料库开发，并经方法创始人Russell T. Hurlburt优化。据我们所知，这是首个基于已建立的内省经验研究方法的AI访谈者。该访谈者运行于Introscope——一个提供信号提示和进行访谈的应用程序，也是一个让研究者通过可共享链接开展研究并审查所采集经验的研究平台。在随附的视频（https://introscope.mpib-berlin.mpg.de/video）中进行了演示。待验证研究完成后，我们将免费向研究人员和公众开放，用于众包采样和个人内省经验探索。

</details>
